Existing pruning strategies often depend on task-specific criteria or LLM-oriented importance measures, making them unsuitable for task-agnostic pruning, where no task-specific samples are available at pruning time and the pruned model remains broadly applicable. We introduce a retraining-free VLM pruning framework called PORTA that derives a task- and modality-agnostic importance formulation based on activation variation, estimated from generic calibration data, which reliably captures feature-level representation utility across modalities. PORTA further incorporates an adaptive sparsity allocation mechanism that assigns layer-wise pruning ratios based on output feature variability, avoiding the limitations of uniform sparsity and reducing performance degradation at high compression levels. Extensive experiments across VLM architectures, such as CLIP, BLIP, and Qwen2-VL, demonstrate that PORTA achieves competitive downstream performance under high sparsity without requiring any retraining, supporting efficient VLM compression. Code is available at https://github.com/cau-hai-lab/PORTA.git.

</details>


### [33] [MuST-VAD: Mutual Structured Learning for Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06913)
*Satoshi Hashimoto,Hitoshi Nishimura,Mori Kurokawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MuST-VAD，一种用于弱监督视频异常检测的互学习框架，通过让异常检测器与大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）进行双向知识交换，打破以往单向特征迁移的限制。在UCF-Crime数据集上，AUROC从88.15%提升至88.63%，平均精度（AP）从37.25%提升至42.46%，AP比最先进方法高出4.13个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱监督视频异常检测中，检测器从固定的任务无关骨干网络提取特征学习异常分数，这些固定特征限制了检测精度的上限。现有方法虽然将LVLM语义作为更丰富的特征迁移到检测器，但该迁移是单向的——检测器从目标视频中学到的知识永远不会回流到LVLM，导致双向知识未能充分利用。

Method: MuST-VAD将单向迁移扩展为双向学习循环：检测器的最新预测监督LVLM的适应，适应后的LVLM返回更新后的表示来重新训练检测器，两个模型在小视频组上交替更新。具体机制包括：仅使用检测器选择的关键片段进行训练、通过置信度加权和标注锚定的问答机制确保交换监督信息的可靠性。

Result: 在UCF-Crime数据集上，互学习将单次迁移基线从88.15%提升至88.63%的AUROC，从37.25%提升至42.46%的平均精度（AP），AP比最先进方法高出4.13个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过检测器与LVLM之间的互学习循环，MuST-VAD验证了双向知识交换相比单向特征迁移能更有效地提升弱监督视频异常检测的性能，尤其在平均精度指标上取得了显著改进。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose MuST-VAD, a mutual structured learning framework for weakly supervised video anomaly detection (VAD) in which an anomaly detector and a large vision-language model (LVLM) exchange their acquired knowledge. Detectors in weakly supervised VAD learn anomaly scores from features extracted by a fixed, task-agnostic backbone. These fixed features bound the achievable detection accuracy. Recent methods therefore transfer LVLM semantics into the detector as richer features. However, this transfer is one-way: what the detector learns about the target videos never returns to the LVLM. MuST-VAD extends the one-way transfer into a bidirectional learning loop. In this loop, the latest detector predictions supervise the LVLM adaptation, and the adapted LVLM returns updated representations that retrain the detector; the two models alternate these updates over small video groups. Both models train on detector-selected key clips, while confidence weighting and annotation-anchored question answering keep the exchanged supervision reliable. On UCF-Crime, our mutual learning improves the one-pass transfer baseline from 88.15% to 88.63% AUROC and from 37.25% to 42.46% average precision (AP), outperforming the state-of-the-art method in AP by 4.13 points.

</details>


### [34] [RibAssist 3D: Biplanar Rib-Fracture Detection, Addressing, and Selective 3D Localization from CT-Derived Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06914)
*Kabila Haile Soboka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探讨能否将从CT双正交投影（前后位AP和侧位）检测到的肋骨骨折配对并进行三角测量，得到可靠的3D骨折点。通过分期诊断研究，发现双平面几何本身是精确的（正确配对时中位3D误差4.0 mm），真正瓶颈在于置信度受限的跨视角对应关系，而非几何或定位。通过侧位检测器重训练提升了双视角可用性（开发集0.52→0.76），在密封队列上实现了2.50%的端到端承诺产出率，每例仅0.436个假3D点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肋骨骨折在CT上定位耗时且临床上具有重要意义，现有方法缺少一种能够控制伪3D输出率的跨视角配对与三角测量方法。作者希望验证双正交投影下的骨折配对能否产生可靠的三维定位结果，并识别系统中的真正瓶颈环节。

Method: 采用分期诊断研究设计：1）利用双平面几何将两个正交投影（AP和侧位）检测到的骨折中心配对并三角化为3D点；2）评估检测器预测中心的3D重建误差；3）对侧位检测器进行重训练以提升双视角可用性；4）在密封55例队列上运行预指定流程，以受控的假3D输出率评估端到端效果。

Result: 双平面几何精确——正确配对时中位3D重建误差4.0 mm，密封队列双视角可用性达61.1%，58.4%的骨折在候选图中包含正确配对。侧位检测器重训练将双视角可用性从0.52提升至0.76，并在10 mm处将前沿从0%推进到2.44%召回率。政策承诺正确配对时，密封队列中位误差1.49 mm，肋骨级精确率93%。端到端流程在601处骨折中促进15处获得正确3D定位，每例0.436个假3D点，承诺产出率2.50%。

Conclusion: 贡献在于：验证了具有高条件定位保真度的双平面重建几何；分期识别出跨视角对应置信度是有效瓶颈（而非几何或定位）；提出了一种选择性辅助工作流，保留不确定的发现而非作为独立的自动重建器。该方法为临床提供可控假阳性率的辅助工具，而非完全自主的骨折重建系统。

Abstract: Rib fractures are common, clinically significant, and time-consuming to localize on computed tomography (CT). We ask whether fractures detected in two orthogonal projections (anteroposterior, AP, and lateral) can be paired across views and triangulated into reliable 3D fracture points at a controlled rate of false 3D outputs. We answer this with a staged diagnostic study. Biplanar geometry is exact: detector-predicted centers reconstruct to median 4.0 mm 3D error when correspondence is correct. On the sealed cohort, dual-view availability reaches 61.1% and the candidate graph contains a correct pair for 58.4% of fractures. The binding limitation is not geometry or localization but confidence-limited cross-view correspondence. Lateral-detector retraining lifts dual-view availability (0.52 to 0.76 in development) and moves the frontier from 0% to 2.44% recall at 10 mm. When the policy commits a correct pair, the emitted point is geometrically accurate (sealed median 1.49 mm, rib-exact 93%). A pre-specified pass on the untouched 55-case cohort promotes 15 of 601 fractures to correct 3D localizations at 0.436 false 3D points per case, yielding 2.50% end-to-end commitment yield. The contribution is validated biplanar reconstruction geometry with high conditional localization fidelity, a staged identification of cross-view correspondence confidence as the effective bottleneck, and a selective assistive workflow that preserves uncertain findings rather than a standalone automatic reconstructor.

</details>


### [35] [MaskFlow: Precise, Consistent and Seamless Regional Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06929)
*Rui Xu,Yang Yong,Shunzi Yang,Ruihao Gong,Chengtao Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MaskFlow是一个用于区域图像编辑的训练框架，通过将掩码融入概率路径和流匹配目标，实现了精确的空间定位、背景一致性保持和无缝边界过渡，并配套构建了MEData数据集（基于掩码的图像编辑数据集）用于训练和评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令式（instruction-based）和掩码参考式（mask-reference-based）编辑方法虽能达到较强的语义对齐，但在可靠区域控制方面仍有挑战——编辑必须精确定位，并与保留的上下文自然融合，这需要同时解决区域定位精度、背景一致性保持和边界无缝过渡三个问题。

Method: MaskFlow由三部分组成：(1) 将掩码直接融入概率路径和流匹配目标，协调可编辑区域内的生成与区域外的源图像保留；(2) 提出Soft-Poisson de-seaming模块，在训练和采样阶段细化预测的矢量场，改善前景编辑与背景保留之间的平滑集成；(3) 设计数据合成管道构建MEData数据集（基于掩码的图像编辑数据集），用于训练区域图像编辑模型。

Result: 在自然场景和infographic图像上的实验表明，MaskFlow在定量和定性评估中均一致优于现有的对比方法。

Conclusion: MaskFlow通过在流匹配框架中显式编码掩码信息并引入Soft-Poisson去接缝细化模块，有效解决了区域编辑中的定位精度、背景保持与边界融合问题，并提供了MEData数据集以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Regional image editing has attracted considerable attention for its spatial controllability. Although instruction-based and mask-reference-based editing methods can achieve strong semantic alignment, reliable regional control remains challenging, where an edit must be accurately localized and naturally integrated with the preserved context. We propose \textbf{MaskFlow}, a training framework for precise localization, consistent background preservation, and seamless boundary transitions. MaskFlow incorporates the mask into the probability path and flow-matching objective, coordinating generation within the editable region with source preservation outside it. The proposed Soft-Poisson de-seaming module further refines the predicted vector field during both training and sampling to improve the smooth integration of the edited foreground with the preserved background. We also design a data synthesis pipeline to construct MEData, a mask-based image editing dataset for training regional image editing models and facilitating further research. Experiments on natural scenes and infographic images demonstrate consistent improvements over competing methods in both quantitative and qualitative evaluations.

</details>


### [36] [AVCap: Reinforcing Audio-Video Joint Caption with Detail-Aware Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06930)
*Mingyang Wu,Kaituo Feng,Bohao Li,Kaixiong Gong,Zihao Yin,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AVCap-100K数据集、AVCap模型及AVCap-Bench/AVCap-Score基准与指标，系统性地解决音视频联合详细描述任务中数据稀缺、强化学习奖励粗糙和缺乏原子级评估基准三大问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 详细的音视频联合描述对于多模态视频理解与生成至关重要，但现有工作受限于三个主要瓶颈：(1)缺乏高质量、细粒度音视频联合字幕的公开数据集；(2)强化学习方法依赖粗糙的奖励信号；(3)缺少在原子级别评估详细音视频字幕的基准和指标。

Method: 提出三方面贡献：(1)构建AVCap-100K数据集，包含10万条时间对齐、细节丰富的音视频联合描述；(2)训练AVCap模型，通过Detail-Aware GRPO (Da-GRPO)优化策略进行强化学习调优；(3)建立AVCap-Bench基准和AVCap-Score指标，用于评估音视频字幕中的原子级细节。

Result: AVCap模型在开源模型中达到最先进水平，在多项评测中匹配甚至超越闭源专有模型。

Conclusion: 通过高质量数据、细粒度强化学习策略以及专门化评估基准三者协同，有效突破了音视频联合详细描述领域的关键瓶颈，代码、模型和数据集均已开源。

Abstract: Detailed audio-video joint captioning is essential for multimodal video understanding and generation. However, prior works are constrained by three main limitations: (1) the scarcity of high-quality public datasets with fine-grained audio-visual joint captions; (2) reinforcement-learning methods that rely on coarse reward signals; and (3) the lack of a benchmark and metric for evaluating detailed audiovisual captions at the atomic level. To address these challenges, we propose: (1) AVCap-100K, a high-quality dataset of 100K temporally aligned, detail-rich audio-video captions; (2) AVCap, a model optimized via Detail-Aware GRPO (Da-GRPO) that achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-source models and matches or surpasses proprietary models on several evaluations; and (3) AVCap-Bench and AVCap-Score, a specialized benchmark and metric for evaluating atomic-level details in audiovisual captions. Our code, models, and datasets are available at https://huggingface.co/collections/Apryle/avcap.

</details>


### [37] [Debias in Text, Believe Your Eyes: Text-Anchored Cross-Modal Transfer for Visual Counter-Commonsense Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06938)
*Chen Ling,Hanqian Li,Dongnan Liu,Keyu Qian,Jungang Li,Xinglong liu,Shiyi Wang,Xin Dong,Pengcheng Zhu,Wei Zhou,Linjian Mo,Nai Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在视觉反常识推理中的瓶颈并非视觉感知不足，而是共享语言解码器偏向主导性语言先验。作者提出TACT框架，通过文本锚定的数据构建和后训练方法去偏置语言解码器，无需视觉训练数据即可显著提升视觉反常识推理能力，并实现文本到视觉的跨模态迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究假设视觉反常识推理失败源于视觉输入不足，但作者的实证分析发现MLLM已经捕获相关视觉证据且正确答案存在于解码空间中。真正的问题是共享语言解码器在化解"先验—证据"冲突时偏向主导性语言先验，尤其对低频事实场景更为严重。因此需要重新审视问题定位并寻找去偏置解码器的解决方案。

Method: 提出一条文本锚定的数据构建管线，核心组件为事实频率蒸馏（FFD），用于估计常识事实的先验强度并蒸馏出经过验证的反常识场景文本语料。在此基础上，提出TACT后训练框架，将"跟随证据"和"先验驱动"两种推理轨迹路由到不同的优化阶段，从而在不依赖任何视觉训练数据的情况下对共享语言解码器进行去偏置。

Result: 在多个反常识视觉基准上，TACT大幅提升了视觉推理能力，同时保持了一般能力不退化，验证了文本到视觉跨模态迁移的有效性。

Conclusion: 视觉反常识推理的瓶颈在于语言解码器的先验偏见而非视觉感知，通过文本锚定的后训练可以有效地去偏置共享解码器，实现无需视觉数据的跨模态推理能力提升。

Abstract: The visual reasoning ability of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is crucial for downstream applications, particularly counter-commonsense reasoning, which requires models to reason beyond common assumptions. Recent studies mainly improve visual counter-commonsense reasoning by enhancing visual inputs, following the assumption that failures originate from insufficient visual grounding. However, our empirical analysis reveals that the bottleneck is not visual perception. MLLMs already capture the relevant visual evidence, and the correct answer exists in their decoding space. Instead, the shared language decoder resolves prior--evidence conflicts by favoring dominant language priors, especially for low-frequency factual scenarios. Motivated by this, we first propose a text-anchored data construction pipeline, whose core component, Fact-Frequency Distillation (FFD), estimates the prior strength of commonsense facts and distills verified counter-commonsense scenarios into a high-quality text corpus. Building upon this corpus, we introduce TACT, a text-anchored post-training framework that debiases the shared language decoder without requiring any visual training data. TACT routes evidence-following and prior-driven reasoning trajectories into different optimization stages, enabling the decoder to resolve prior--evidence conflicts. Across counter-commonsense visual benchmarks, TACT substantially improves visual reasoning while preserving general capabilities, demonstrating effective text-to-vision cross-modal transfer.

</details>


### [38] [Degradation-Aware Prompt Learning with Cross-Modal Compensation for Adverse Weather Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06939)
*Wanshu Fan,Yunzhe Zhang,Yue Shen,Liyan Wang,Jing Qin,Kin-Man Lam,Cong Wang,Jinshan Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DCMPC-Net，一种利用预训练视觉-语言模型的跨模态退化提示来指导统一框架下多种天气退化图像恢复的网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有一体化恢复模型虽试图在统一框架内处理多种退化类型，但缺乏对退化特征的显式空间和语义建模，导致对不同天气条件的适应性有限。

Method: DCMPC-Net由三个核心模块组成：跨模态提示生成器（CMPG）融合文本嵌入与视觉特征生成退化感知提示；提示引导注意力对齐模块（PGAAM）将提示注入解码器并自适应对齐退化区域语义信息；双特征补偿模块（DFCM）解耦退化伪影与场景结构以恢复细粒度纹理。

Result: 在任务特定和统一设置下均优于现有最先进方法，取得更优的准确性和视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 通过跨模态语义引导、空间对齐与结构增强的整合，DCMPC-Net在多种天气条件下实现了鲁棒且感知一致的图像恢复。

Abstract: Adverse weather causes diverse and complex image degradations, severely compromising the reliability of computer vision systems. Existing all-in-one restoration models attempt to address multiple degradation types within a unified framework, but often lack explicit spatial and semantic modeling of degradation characteristics, limiting their adaptability to diverse weather conditions. To address this limitation, we propose a Degradation-Aware Cross-Modal Prompt Compensation Network (DCMPC-Net) that leverages cross-modal degradation cues from a pretrained vision-language model to condition restoration features within a unified backbone. Specifically, our DCMPC-Net mainly consists of the Cross-Modal Prompt Generator (CMPG), Prompt-Guided Attention Alignment Module (PGAAM), and Dual Feature Compensation Module (DFCM). The CMPG integrates textual embeddings with visual features to produce degradation-aware prompts that encode degradation-related semantic and contextual cues. These prompts are injected into the decoder via a PGAAM, which adaptively aligns semantic information with degraded regions to facilitate context-aware restoration. To further enhance structural fidelity, DFCM is introduced that disentangles degradation artifacts from scene structures, thereby improving the reconstruction of fine textures and detailed content. By integrating cross-modal semantic guidance with spatial alignment and structural enhancement, DCMPC-Net achieves robust and perceptually consistent restoration across diverse weather conditions. Extensive experiments show that DCMPC-Net outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both task-specific and unified settings, achieving superior accuracy and visual fidelity.

</details>


### [39] [Dual-Space Modality Consistency Learning for Universal Cross-Modal Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06943)
*Yujian Zhao,Yukang Zhao,Hankun Liu,Haoxuan Xu,Bo Li,Hanzi Wan,Guanglin Niu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种双空间模态一致性学习（DSMCL）框架，用于通用跨模态行人/船舶重识别。该框架同时建模空间特征分布一致性和频域判别一致性，通过高斯特征对齐和身份感知的高频跨模态对比学习，构建了可直接即插即用地集成到现有跨模态ReID架构中的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态ReID方法虽然在空间嵌入空间中学到了模态一致性，但往往忽视了频域中的模态差异，尤其是既具有高判别性又对模态敏感的高频表示。此外，大多数方法针对特定模态设置定制，限制了其在多样化跨模态场景中的适用性。

Method: 提出DSMCL框架，包含两个分支：(1) 空间模态一致性学习（SMCL）分支，执行基于高斯的特征对齐；(2) 频域感知判别一致性学习（FDCL）策略，通过身份感知的跨模态对比学习对高频表征进行正则化。两者联合捕获模态特有特征和模态共享的身份线索，形成即插即用的统一框架。

Result: 在SYSU-MM01、RegDB、LLCM、HOSS-ReID和CMShipReID五个数据集上，跨越17个评估协议进行了大量实验，结果显示DSMCL能够持续改进多个代表性基线模型。

Conclusion: DSMCL通过联合建模空间和频域两个空间的模态一致性，学习到鲁棒的表征，并建立了一个能够适应多样化异构模态设置的统一框架，且以即插即用方式提升现有方法性能。

Abstract: Cross-modal Re-Identification (ReID) aims to retrieve the same identity across heterogeneous imaging modalities and has been widely studied in visible-infrared person ReID and cross-modal ship ReID. Existing methods have achieved promising performance by learning modality consistency in the spatial embedding space, yet often overlook frequency-domain modality discrepancy, particularly in high-frequency representations that are both highly discriminative and modality-sensitive. In addition, most approaches are tailored to specific modality settings, limiting their applicability across diverse cross-modal scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose a Dual-Space Modality Consistency Learning (DSMCL) framework for universal cross-modal ReID. Specifically, DSMCL jointly models spatial feature distribution consistency and frequency-domain discriminative consistency. A Spatial Modality Consistency Learning (SMCL) branch performs Gaussian-based feature alignment, while a Frequency-aware Discriminative Consistency Learning (FDCL) strategy regularizes high-frequency representations through identity-aware cross-modal contrastive learning. By jointly capturing modality-specific characteristics and modality-shared identity cues, DSMCL learns robust representations and establishes a unified framework capable of accommodating diverse heterogeneous modality settings. Moreover, DSMCL is a plug-and-play framework that can be readily integrated into existing cross-modal ReID architectures. Extensive experiments on SYSU-MM01, RegDB, LLCM, HOSS-ReID, and CMShipReID across seventeen evaluation protocols show that DSMCL consistently improves multiple representative baselines.

</details>


### [40] [Summarize First, Download Later: Onboard VLMs for Bandwidth-Efficient Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06959)
*Junghwan Park,Sangcheol Sim,Woojin Cho,Darongsae Kwon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"先总结后下载"的卫星下行链路范式，利用星载边缘计算和量化视觉语言模型（VLM）先在星上生成自然语言摘要，地面操作员通过VQA查询验证场景相关性，仅对确认为关键信息的高分辨率图像进行下载，从而将下行链路从被动批量传输转变为主动的语义感知对话，在NVIDIA Jetson平台上验证了带宽节省和时效性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代对地观测卫星搭载日益先进的传感器，产生海量高分辨率多光谱数据，但下行链路容量仍是关键瓶颈，在有限的接触窗口内常造成显著延迟或宝贵观测数据丢失。现有下行链路方案缺乏语义感知能力，无法区分哪些数据真正值得传输。

Method: 采用"Summarize First, Download Later"三相交互协议：(1) 卫星通过量化星载VLM生成简洁的自然语言摘要并下行传输；(2) 地面操作员发出目标导向的VQA查询（如检测野火或海事异常）以验证场景相关性；(3) 仅在确认包含关键信息时下载全分辨率图像。系统在资源受限的NVIDIA Jetson平台上实现和评估。

Result: 在多样化的遥感场景实验中，所提出的策略显著降低了带宽消耗，同时加速了时间敏感任务的洞察获取时间。系统在Jetson这一资源受限平台上的可行性得到验证。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了将UAV与VLM结合进行星载语义理解，可将下行链路从被动批量数据传输转变为主动、语义感知的交互式对话，为实现高效、事件驱动的遥感数据获取提供了新范式。

Abstract: Modern Earth observation (EO) satellites carry increasingly advanced sensors that produce vast volumes of high-resolution, multispectral data, yet downlink capacity remains a critical bottleneck -- often causing significant latency or the loss of valuable observations within limited contact windows. We propose a "Summarize First, Download Later" paradigm that exploits recent advances in onboard edge computing and Vision-Language Models (VLMs). Rather than indiscriminately downlinking raw imagery, the system follows a three-phase interaction protocol: the satellite first transmits concise natural language summaries generated by a quantized onboard VLM; ground operators then issue targeted Visual Question Answering (VQA) queries to verify scene relevance (e.g., wildfires or maritime anomalies); and full-resolution images are downloaded only when critical information is confirmed. This transforms the downlink from passive bulk transfer into an active, semantics-aware dialogue. We implement and evaluate the system on a resource-constrained NVIDIA Jetson platform, and experiments on diverse remote sensing scenes show that the proposed strategy substantially reduces bandwidth consumption while accelerating time-to-insight for time-sensitive missions.

</details>


### [41] [Generative Embedding Benchmark: How Much Information Survives in a Dense Embedding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06972)
*Yun Li,Biao Yang,Peixi Wu,Yunhao Zhou,Mingzhou Jiang,Wei Yuan,Fan Yang,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式嵌入基准(GEB)，用于评估嵌入中可恢复的生成性信息量。与传统的基于可分性的判别式基准不同，GEB让解码器仅凭冻结嵌入和问题文本回答视觉问题。在900项测试集上，纯视觉嵌入得分为28.25-33.21，视觉-语言联合编码的最佳模型达65.56，而可访问原始图像的参考模型达84.30。结果表明生成式读出能暴露可分性评估无法捕获的信息瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入基准主要通过判别式任务或几何可分性标准评估表示质量，但这些评估无法回答一个关键问题：压缩进嵌入的内容是否真的能被下游生成模型访问和利用。需要一种新的评估范式来测量嵌入中可恢复的生成性信息。

Method: GEB采用解码器-读出的范式：解码器仅使用冻结嵌入和问题文本回答问题，不接触原始图像或中间视觉特征。构建了包含1,800项开发集和900项保留测试集的视觉问答数据集，覆盖自然图像、场景文本和视觉文档三种类型。使用统一解码器和训练方案评估7个公共嵌入模型，分别在纯视觉模式和视觉-语言联合模式下测试。

Result: 测试集上，纯视觉模式得分为28.25-33.21；使用图像-问题联合编码时，所有5个基于VLM的嵌入模型得分更高，最佳达65.56。匹配嵌入优于文本输入、零嵌入和打乱嵌入。自然图像信息比场景文本或视觉文档信息更易恢复，而可访问原始图像的Qwen3-VL-2B参考模型达到84.30。

Conclusion: 生成式读出评估揭示了嵌入中的信息瓶颈，而这些瓶颈无法通过基于可分性的传统评估捕获，表明需要将生成性信息恢复能力纳入嵌入质量评估标准。

Abstract: Embeddings have emerged as a standard representational interface linking foundation models with downstream systems. Most embedding benchmarks assess representations through discriminative tasks or geometric criteria centered on separability in embedding space. However, strong performance on such evaluations does not establish whether content compressed into an embedding remains accessible to a downstream generator. To address this gap, we introduce the Generative Embedding Benchmark (GEB), in which a decoder answers questions using only a frozen embedding and question text, without access to the original image or intermediate visual features. Answer quality under this readout measures generative information: the answer-relevant content recoverable from an embedding. GEB includes a curated visual-question-answering dataset with a 1,800-item development split and a held-out 900-item test split covering natural images, scene text, and visual documents. Using a common decoder and training recipe, we evaluate seven public embedding models in visual-only and vision-language joint modes. On the test set, visual-only scores range from 28.25 to 33.21; with image-question joint encoding, all five VLM-based embedding models score higher, and the best reaches 65.56. Matched embeddings also outperform text-only inputs, zero embeddings, and shuffled embeddings. Natural-image information is much easier to recover than scene text or visual-document information, while a Qwen3-VL-2B reference with access to the original image reaches 84.30. Together, these results show that generative readout exposes information bottlenecks that separability-based evaluation does not capture.

</details>


### [42] [When One Modality Is Not Enough: Multimodal Sex and Life-Stage Classification of Red Deer from Aerial RGB-Thermal Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06973)
*Hugo Markoff,Christoph Praschl,Ivan Ludoški,Sara Beery,Michael Ørsted,David C. Schedl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合航拍RGB与热成像、基于自监督DINOv3特征的多模态深度学习流水线，用于自动对红鹿进行物种、性别与年龄分类。该流水线在四个航次、26头检测个体中正确分类25头，准确率达96.0%，远超单一传感器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空无人机调查常用于野生动物种群估算，但种群动态需要物种组成、性别比例和年龄结构等信息，而不仅仅是数量。红鹿作为测试案例，其成年雄鹿的鹿角标志具有季节性变化，且两种成像模态（彩色与热成像）在相反条件下各自失效：彩色在树冠下会与地面融为一体，热成像损失细节。因此需要融合两种模态以实现可靠的分类。

Method: 提出一个全阶段融合RGB与热成像的多模态流水线：1) 使用自监督DINOv3特征对两种模态分别进行动物跟踪；2) 仅在两相机同时确认时认定动物为有效；3) 保留清晰无遮挡的帧；4) 通过投票机制跨帧分配物种与性别；5) 生命阶段（成体/幼体）独立依据地理参考的体尺寸判定。

Result: 在跨越鹿角季节的四个航次中，融合流水线正确分类了26头中的25头（8头成年雄鹿中7头、16头成年雌鹿全部、2头幼鹿），而任一单一传感器的准确率仅为26头中的20头。多模态物种分类准确率达96.0%；性别分类中传感器融合效果最显著，RGB+热成像组合模型在各环境和季节中最稳健。

Conclusion: 自动化人口统计学分类将无人机航拍从单纯的计数转变为对畜群结构的可重复读取，使管理人员已依赖的性别比例和年龄结构数据能够随每次航拍调查的频率进行采集。

Abstract: Aerial drone surveys increasingly support wildlife population estimation, yet a useful census is more than a count: population dynamics are defined by species composition, sex ratios and age structure, that is, by which species are present and how a herd splits into adult males, adult females and juveniles. We use red deer ($\textit{Cervus elaphus}$) as a test case, because managers act on these dynamics and because the visible cue defining adult males, the antlers, is seasonally variable. Surveys are flown nadir, high enough not to disturb the animals, so each deer occupies only a small, low-resolution patch. The two recording modalities fail in opposite conditions: in color a deer under canopy blends into the ground, while in thermal it becomes a bright blob that loses fine detail. Rather than trust either modality alone, we fuse them at every stage using self-supervised DINOv3 features. Our pipeline tracks animals in both modalities, treats an animal as confirmed only when the two cameras agree, keeps only the clear, non-occluded frames, and assigns species and sex by a vote across them; life stage is read separately from geo-referenced body size, since at survey resolution a juvenile often only differs from an adult female in size. Across four flights spanning the antler season the fused pipeline correctly classifies 25 of the 26 detected individuals (7 of 8 adult males, all 16 adult females and 2 juveniles), against 20 of 26 for either sensor alone. Multimodal species classification reaches 96.0%, while for sex classification fusing the two sensors matters most: the combined RGB+thermal model is the most robust across environments and seasons. Automating the demographic classification turns a drone flight from a count into a repeatable reading of herd structure, so the sex ratios and age structure that managers already act on can be gathered as often as a survey can be flown.

</details>


### [43] [Local Epistemic Uncertainty Guided Active Sampling for Plug-and-play Diffusive Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06981)
*Jiaqi Zhang,Zheng Pang,Rongrong Gao,Qiyuan Zhang,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LEADer是一个即插即用的局部认知不确定性引导主动采样框架，通过空间域像素级不确定性动态调制先验强度、时间域不确定性迹实现自适应轨迹剪枝，在保证严格数据一致性和确定性误差界的前提下，显著提升多种扩散模型图像复原（DMIR）方法的性能并大幅降低采样时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的图像复原方法在引导重建时依赖固定的数据约束和均匀步长，忽略了生成过程的动态特性。这种刚性设计在空间非均匀退化场景下容易导致结构扭曲和细节丢失，同时均匀步长带来计算冗余，而朴素的步长缩减策略又会累积近似误差。

Method: LEADer框架包含两大核心模块：(1) 空间域：利用像素级认知不确定性动态调制零空间中的先验强度，在细节保留和伪影抑制之间取得平衡；(2) 时间域：通过不确定性迹（uncertainty trace）量化采样稳定性，实现自适应轨迹剪枝以加速收敛。理论证明该框架能达到严格数据一致性，且轨迹剪枝策略具有确定性误差界，保证跳跃采样下的稳定收敛。

Result: 大量实验表明LEADer能够提升多种最先进的DMIR方法的性能，同时显著减少采样时间，且内存开销可忽略不计。该方法是即插即用的，可无缝集成到各类DMIR基线中。

Conclusion: LEADer通过时空双维度的动态自适应策略有效克服了现有DMIR方法中固定约束和均匀步长的局限性，既提升了复原质量又加速了推理过程，为扩散模型图像复原提供了一种通用且高效的可插拔增强方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models have demonstrated remarkable effectiveness in image restoration tasks. However, when guiding image reconstruction, existing Diffusion Model-based Image Restoration (DMIR) methods typically rely on fixed data constraints and uniform step sizes, thereby overlooking the dynamic nature of the generative process. Such rigid designs render the models vulnerable to spatially non-uniform degradations, thus resulting in structural distortions and loss of fine details. Meanwhile, uniform step sizes introduce computational redundancy, whereas naïve step reduction strategies tend to accumulate approximation errors. To address these limitations, we propose a Local Epistemic Uncertainty Guided Active Sampling framework (LEADer). In the spatial domain, LEADer leverages pixel-wise uncertainty to dynamically modulate the prior strength within the null space, which effectively balances detail preservation and artifact suppression. In the temporal domain, it quantifies sampling stability via the uncertainty trace to enable adaptive trajectory pruning, thereby accelerating convergence. Theoretical proofs demonstrate that our framework achieves strict data consistency, while the trajectory pruning strategy admits a deterministic error bound, thereby guaranteeing stable convergence under skip sampling. Notably, our plug-and-play method can be seamlessly integrated into various DMIR baselines. Extensive experiments show that LEADer improves the performance of multiple state-of-the-art DMIR methods, while significantly reducing sampling time with negligible memory overhead. Code is available at https://github.com/JiaqiZhang-Sengoku/LEADer.

</details>


### [44] [HRDiT: Training-Free High-Resolution Image Generation with Off-the-Shelf Diffusion Transformer Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07003)
*Yu Xue,Haoxuan Qu,Zhuoling Li,Hongbin Xu,Jianxiong Yin,Simon See,Hossein Rahmani,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练即可将现成Diffusion Transformer（DiT）模型适配到高分辨率图像生成的方法，针对其面临的空间混乱和生成时间长两大挑战进行了针对性解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无训练高分辨率图像生成研究主要聚焦于U-Net架构的扩散模型，而DiT模型虽在有限分辨率下展现出强大的文生图能力，但将其适配到高分辨率时存在空间混乱和生成时间过长的问题，目前缺乏针对DiT模型的专门解决方案。

Abstract: Training-free text-to-high-resolution image generation has recently attracted growing research attention. However, existing studies on this task primarily focus on adapting off-the-shelf U-Net-based diffusion models to high resolutions, with limited progress on adapting off-the-shelf Diffusion Transformer (DiT) models despite their strong text-to-image generation capabilities at limited resolutions. In this work, we find two key challenges particularly hindering the application of off-the-shelf DiT models for high-resolution image synthesis in a training-free manner, namely, spatial disorder and long generation time. To address these challenges, we propose a novel method tailored to adapt off-the-shelf DiT models for high-resolution image synthesis. Extensive experiments show the efficacy of our method. Our code is available at: https://github.com/zylwithxy/HRDiT.

</details>


### [45] [Scenix: Sparse-View 3D Scene Reconstruction via Executable Scene Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07012)
*Kai Li,Lutao Jiang,Zhenyang Li,Jiayu Dong,Jierui Zhang,Yingda Yin,Runze Zhang,Kai Yan,Xiaoyang Huang,Keyang Luo,Xin Wang,Xiangyu Zhao,Weikai Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Scenix是一个从稀疏非标定RGB视角重建结构化可编辑3D室内场景的框架，通过可执行场景程序实现，无需人工标注或连续视觉输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景生成方法要么依赖文本输入，要么需要连续视觉输入及额外先验（如人工标注掩码或精确3D布局），劳动密集且难以泛化到一般场景。

Method: Scenix采用可执行场景程序作为结构化表示，可直接实例化为可编辑的3D场景。给定稀疏视图，通过感知接地资产实例化和闭环空间细化来预测可执行场景程序。同时构建了约11万个合成及真实室内场景的数据集，引入观测一致监督以对齐目标场景与输入视图中的视觉证据。

Result: 在保留的XScene场景、真实室内图像以及分布外的SpatialGen案例上进行了实验，评估了结构化场景预测、物体接地和空间细化三个方面。

Conclusion: Scenix证明了通过可执行场景程序，仅凭少量稀疏视图即可实现结构化和可编辑的3D室内场景重建，克服了传统方法对额外先验信息的依赖。

Abstract: Synthesizing a structured and editable 3D indoor scene from a few uncalibrated RGB views requires more than generating high-quality individual assets: a system must infer the room structure, associate objects across incomplete observations, and recover a globally consistent spatial configuration. Previous methods mainly focus on 3D scene generation with text input or require continuous visual inputs with additional priors, \ e.g., human-annotated masks or accurate 3D layouts, which makes these methods labor demanding and hard to apply in general cases. We present \textsc{Scenix}, a sparse-view 3D scene reconstruction framework via executable scene programs, a structured representation that can be directly instantiated into editable 3D scenes. Given sparse views, \textsc{Scenix} predicts executable scene programs through perception-grounded asset instantiation and closed-loop spatial refinement. % We present \method, a framework that predicts an executable scene representation from sparse views and realizes it through perception-grounded asset instantiation and closed-loop spatial refinement. To support this task, we construct \dataset, a dataset of approximately 110,000 synthetic and real indoor scenes with multiview imagery, room structures, object-centric descriptions, and metric spatial annotations. We further introduce observation-consistent supervision that aligns each target scene with the visual evidence available in its input views. Experiments on held-out \textsc{XScene} scenes, real indoor images, and out-of-distribution SpatialGen cases evaluate structured scene prediction, object grounding, and spatial refinement.

</details>


### [46] [Stable Curves, Unstable Items: Item-Level Scaling Heterogeneity in Video LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07014)
*Wenzhang Sun,Chunfeng Wang,Xiangchen Yin,Yujia Chen,Hao Li,Kun Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示视频大语言模型（Video LLMs）在视觉预算（帧数、分辨率等）增加时，虽然聚合尺度曲线看似平滑提升或饱和，但逐项（item-level）层面存在大量相反的、相互抵消的变化。通过受控视觉预算下的响应轨迹表示每个模型-样本对，作者定义了对齐网格度量（配置互补性、有害转变、文本覆盖），并在五个开源视频LLM、三个架构家族、多项基准上证明：没有单一视觉预算能服务所有样本；项目中存在大量在更高预算下反而答错的情况；受控采样干预可恢复29.0%的最终回归；置信度级联可在降低31.7%平均共享帧成本的同时匹配固定-128帧的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合尺度曲线（aggregate scaling curves）可能掩盖逐项层面的大量相反变化，导致研究者误以为视觉预算增加时模型表现平滑提升或饱和。作者旨在揭示这种"平均掩盖个体"的现象，并为视频LLM的逐项响应行为提供系统性审计工具和度量。

Method: 1) 将每个冻结模型-样本对表示为受控视觉预算下的响应轨迹（response trajectory）；2) 定义匹配网格度量：配置互补性（configuration complementarity）、有害转变（harmful transitions）、文本覆盖（text overwrite）；3) 在五个开源视频LLM、三类架构、四个MCQA基准分割、开放问答/摘要、固定历史对话生成上系统评估；4) 跨帧数、空间分辨率、采样策略、时空分配及raw-video和cached管道验证效应的持续性；5) 进行受控采样干预和结构化帧审计以识别恢复路径和证据模式；6) 开发置信度级联（confidence cascade）作为响应矩阵的操作应用。

Result: 在四模型匹配MCQA网格上，逐项oracle余量达8.8-18.9准确率点，12.5-25.5%的样本在较低预算下正确但在较高预算下错误。Token-F1的oracle差距在MLVU生成为2.7-3.7分，在AVSD当前轮生成为3.8-4.8分，即便均值质量随预算提升。效应跨帧数、分辨率、采样策略等协议选择持续存在。受控采样干预恢复29.0%的终局回归，结构化帧审计识别出若干反复出现的证据路径。置信度级联在降低31.7%平均共享帧成本的同时匹配固定128帧的准确率。

Conclusion: 聚合尺度曲线不足以评估视频LLM的视觉预算扩展效果；必须采用逐项级别的审计。不同样本对视觉预算的最优配置存在显著互补性，且高预算并非总是更好。作者发布的审计工件（逐项轨迹、协议来源、派生标注、可复现分析代码）和置信度级联展示了响应矩阵的实用价值，为更细粒度的视频LLM评估与资源分配提供了新范式。

Abstract: Aggregate scaling curves suggest that Video LLMs improve smoothly or saturate as visual budgets grow. We show that this view can conceal large, opposing changes at the item level. We represent each frozen model--item pair by its response trajectory under controlled visual budgets and derive matched-grid measures of configuration complementarity, harmful transitions, and text overwrite. Across five open Video LLMs from three architecture families, four multiple-choice benchmark splits, open-ended QA and summarization, and fixed-history dialogue generation, no single budget serves all items. On the four-model matched MCQA grid, item-level oracle headroom spans $8.8$--$18.9$ accuracy points and $12.5$--$25.5\%$ of items are correct at a lower budget but wrong at a higher one. Task-appropriate continuous metrics show the same complementarity beyond multiple choice: Token-F1 oracle gaps are $2.7$--$3.7$ score points on MLVU generation and $3.8$--$4.8$ points on AVSD current-turn generation, even when mean quality improves with budget. The effect persists across frame count, spatial resolution, sampling policy, temporal--spatial allocation, and independently executed raw-video and cached pipelines, with per-item rates and membership tracking protocol choices. A controlled sampling intervention recovers $29.0\%$ of terminal regressions, and a structured frame audit identifies several recurring evidence pathways. We release per-item trajectories, protocol provenance, derived annotations, and reproducible analysis code as an auditing artifact. A confidence cascade matches fixed-$128f$ accuracy while reducing average shared frame cost by $31.7\%$, illustrating one operational use of the response matrix.

</details>


### [47] [Understand Before Detect: Vision--Language Learning for Omni-Domain Infrared Small Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07015)
*Haoyang Yuan,Boyang Li,Yingqian Wang,Yimian Dai,Nuo Chen,Xinfei Huang,Shuqi Yi,Zaiping Lin,Weidong Sheng,Wei An*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"先理解后检测"的范式（JinSight），通过语言监督实现跨域红外小目标检测，并构建了首个大规模多模态全领域红外小目标视觉-语言数据集OmniIRST-VL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红外小目标检测方法采用任务特定的监督学习范式，将全场景红外观测简化为稀疏目标标注，丢失了跨异构域不变语义信息，导致在域偏移情境下检测性能显著下降。

Method: 提出"理解先于检测"（understand before detect）范式，将全领域红外小目标检测建模为理解驱动的过程。具体包括：1）通过语言监督建立整体红外目标理解，将学习到的跨域表征迁移至精确小目标检测；2）提出潜在语义交互（LSI）模块，在紧凑低秩空间中交换语言对齐的全局语义与细粒度空间特征；3）构建OmniIRST-VL数据集，包含超过39k标注，覆盖六个互补的指令任务，涉及场景级理解与目标中心推理。

Result: JinSight通过单一模型即可在异构红外域间泛化，OmniIRST-VL作为首个大规模、高多样性的全领域红外小目标检测视觉-语言数据集，支持场景级理解与目标中心推理等多种任务。

Conclusion: 通过将红外表征锚定在语言语义上，JinSight打破了传统任务特定监督的局限，实现了跨异构红外域的单模型泛化，为全领域红外小目标检测提供了新的理解驱动范式。

Abstract: Omni-domain infrared small target (IRST) detection is crucial for infrared surveillance, yet remains challenging due to heterogeneous imaging domains and inconsistent target characteristics. Previous deep learning-based methods have been developed for visual-only paradigms and achieved promising performance on domain-specific tasks. However, existing methods follow the task-specific supervised learning paradigm. This paradigm simplifies the full-scene infrared observations to sparse target supervision, discarding the semantics that remain invariant across heterogeneous domains. Consequently, detection performance suffers substantially under domain shifts. To handle this issue, we introduce \textbf{``understand before detect''}, a paradigm that formulates omni-domain IRST detection as an understanding-driven process, where holistic infrared target understanding precedes precise detection. Building on this paradigm, we propose \textbf{JinSight}, which first develops holistic IRST understanding through language supervision and then transfers the learned cross-domain representations to precise small-target detection. By grounding infrared representations in language semantics, JinSight enables a single model to generalize across heterogeneous infrared domains. We then introduce Latent Semantic Interaction (LSI), which exchanges language-aligned global semantics with fine-grained spatial features in a compact low-rank space. To address the lack of multimodal omni-domain IRST benchmarks, we build \textbf{OmniIRST-VL}, the first large-scale, highly diverse vision--language dataset for omni-domain IRST detection. It comprises over 39k annotations across six complementary instruction tasks covering both scene-level understanding and target-centric reasoning.

</details>


### [48] [YOLO-PEFT: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning on YOLO Family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07051)
*Xu Lin,WenJie Nie,Jinlong Peng,Weifu Fu,YueXiao Ma,Xiawu Zheng,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出YOLO-PEFT，一个结构感知的参数高效微调框架，将适配器放置建模为可审计的约束规划问题，在实时目标检测器（如YOLO和RT-DETR）上优于全量微调，并能自主拒绝不合适的PEFT配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从语言模型迁移而来的通用PEFT方法在实时检测器上会静默失败，因为这些检测器具有异构算子、检测专用组件以及额外的放置约束，这些约束在常规Transformer堆叠中并不存在。因此需要一种能够感知检测器结构、明确建模放置约束的PEFT方案。

Method: YOLO-PEFT将适配器放置形式化为可审计的约束规划问题：给定检测器图、PEFT请求和资源预算，分配算子与语义角色，评估算子有效性、检测器语义、图接口和部署谓词四类约束，为每个被排除模块记录原因代码，最终输出带预算的目标模块计划，或在训练前返回Refuse。

Result: 在VOC07+12 trainval-to-VOC07测试协议下，RS-LoRA在YOLO11s和YOLO12s上分别达到0.7138和0.7307 mAP50-95，优于Full-SFT的0.6428和0.6662。在RT-DETR-L上，全部七个LoRA系列配置均越过预设灾难性阈值，支撑了校准后的Refuse-to-Full-SFT决策。YOLO11审计显示LoRA峰值训练内存降低43.9%，但训练时间延长1.72倍。

Conclusion: 在评估的检测器家族、放置策略和校准覆盖范围内，YOLO-PEFT用显式、可检查的规划替代了手动目标模块试错，同时保留了经过验证的训练-保存-合并-导出链路；对未见检测器架构的拒绝决策仍是待解决的开放验证问题。

Abstract: Generic parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods transferred from language models can fail silently on real-time detectors, whose heterogeneous operators and detection-specific components impose placement constraints absent from regular Transformer stacks. We propose YOLO-PEFT, a structure-aware framework that formulates adapter placement as an auditable constraint-planning problem. Given a detector graph, a PEFT request, and a resource budget, YOLO-PEFT assigns operator and semantic roles, evaluates explicit operator-validity, detector-semantic, graph-interface, and deployment predicates, records a reason code for each excluded module, and either emits a budgeted target-module plan or returns Refuse before training. Under the official VOC07+12 trainval-to-VOC07 test protocol, planner-selected RS-LoRA reaches 0.7138 and 0.7307 mAP50-95 on YOLO11s and YOLO12s, respectively, compared with 0.6428 and 0.6662 for Full-SFT. On RT-DETR-L, all seven evaluated LoRA-family configurations cross the predefined catastrophic threshold, supporting a calibrated Refuse-to-Full-SFT decision within the evaluated coverage. A controlled YOLO11 audit further shows that LoRA reduces peak training memory by 43.9 percent, although training takes 1.72 times longer. Within the evaluated detector families, placement policies, and calibration coverage, YOLO-PEFT replaces manual target-module trial and error with explicit, inspectable planning while preserving verified train-save-merge-export paths; refusal on unseen detector architectures remains an open validation problem. Project Page: github.com/Tencent/YOLO-Master

</details>


### [49] [KnifeHunter: Structured Local Representation Learning for Fine-Grained Knife Image Retrieval in Law Enforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07057)
*Syed Sameed Husain,Eng-Jon Ong,Stephen Simpson,Trevor Hamshere,Matt Turner,Miroslaw Bober*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出KnifeHunter，一个与英国执法机构合作开发的端到端法医刀具图像检索系统，包含25,843张图像、543个刀具类别的数据集，以及紧凑型单描述符检索架构CoRe-Net，在Medium协议下达到88.0% mAP，并在2023-2025年Operation Sceptre部署中实现99.2% mP@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刀具暴力构成重大公共安全挑战，执法机构需要可扩展的工具进行目录级刀具识别、情报分析和来源归因。人工视觉比对需要专业知识、耗时且难以在操作成像条件下规模化。

Method: 提出CoRe-Net紧凑型单描述符检索架构，结合全局上下文与空间局部判别证据。引入结构化互补表征学习（SCRL）将局部证据组织为互补的原型表征，以及双向互惠融合（BDRF）通过残差投影和门控局部到全局注入来整合全局与局部证据。同时构建KnifeHunter数据集，包含警方证据、零售目录和边境扣押的图像，配以结构化元数据及Medium/Hard评估协议。

Result: 使用EVA02-Base骨干网络和余弦相似度检索，CoRe-Net在Medium协议下达到88.0% mAP和86.7% mP@10，在干扰条件下达到85.1% mAP和83.8% mP@10。系统在2023-2025年Operation Sceptre部署中于现场查询实现99.2% mP@1。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了在执法实际操作环境中，用于细粒度法医刀具匹配的实用且有效的多媒体检索框架，证明了CoRe-Net架构及其数据集的部署价值。

Abstract: Knife-enabled violence presents a major public safety challenge, and law enforcement agencies require scalable tools for catalogue-level knife identification, intelligence analysis, and source attribution. Manual visual comparison is specialist, time-consuming, and difficult to scale under operational imaging conditions. We introduce KnifeHunter, an end-to-end forensic knife image retrieval system developed with UK law enforcement. The work contributes the KnifeHunter dataset, comprising 25,843 images across 543 knife classes from police evidence, retail catalogues, and border-force seizures, with structured metadata, Medium/Hard evaluation protocols, and large-scale distractor evaluation. We further propose CoRe-Net, a compact single-descriptor retrieval architecture that combines global context with spatially localised discriminative evidence. CoRe-Net introduces Structured Complementary Representation Learning (SCRL) to organise local evidence into complementary prototype-based representations, and Bi-Directional Reciprocal Fusion (BDRF) to integrate global and local evidence through residual projection and gated local-to-global injection. Using an EVA02-Base backbone and cosine-similarity retrieval, CoRe-Net achieves 88.0% mAP and 86.7% mP@10 on the Medium protocol, and 85.1% mAP and 83.8% mP@10 under distractor conditions. KnifeHunter was deployed by UK police forces during Operation Sceptre deployments from 2023 to 2025, achieving 99.2% mP@1 on field queries. These results demonstrate a practical and effective multimedia retrieval framework for fine-grained forensic knife matching in operational law-enforcement settings.

</details>


### [50] [Explanation Stability of Test-Time Adaptation in Computational Pathology: A Large-Scale Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07062)
*R. G. Bahumanya,Harshith V. M.,Shreyank N. Gowda,Anala M. R*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了测试时适应（TTA）在计算病理学中对模型解释稳定性的影响。通过两个组织病理学基准、五种架构、十七种TTA方法和四种归因族，进行2,958次适应实验后发现：冻结骨干的TTA方法几乎不改变归因，而持续学习方法（如CoTTA、RoTTA）导致最大漂移；卷积网络比Transformer和基础模型骨干更敏感；解释稳定性与适应质量仅弱耦合，存在准确率或解释评估单独无法捕获的静默失败。作者提出解释稳定性是TTA的独立可靠性轴，并发布指标、协议和完整基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有TTA方法通常仅以准确率评估效果，而在计算病理学这一对可解释性和临床可审计性高度敏感的领域，模型解释在适应后是否仍然可靠几乎未被测量。临床使用不仅取决于准确率，还取决于模型解释的可靠性，因此有必要系统评估TTA对解释稳定性的影响。

Method: 在Camelyon17和NCT CRC-HE两个基准上，使用五种架构（从卷积网络到视觉Transformer再到病理基础模型）、十七种TTA方法及四种归因族，进行2,958次适应运行，系统测量解释漂移并分析其与适应强度、批大小、骨干架构及适应质量（准确率、校准）的关系。

Result: 发现：1) 冻结骨干方法使归因几乎不变，而持续方法（CoTTA、RoTTA）造成最大解释漂移；2) 卷积网络比Transformer和基础模型骨干显著更敏感；3) 解释漂移随适应强度增加，但对批大小不敏感；4) 解释稳定性与适应质量仅弱耦合，某些方法几乎完美保留解释但退化校准或准确率，产生准确率/解释单独评估均无法发现的静默失败。

Conclusion: 解释稳定性是TTA在计算病理学中区别于适应质量的一个独立可靠性轴。当前TTA方法在解释稳定性上差异显著且存在静默失败风险，因此未来需开发不仅准确且稳定的适应方法，并建议采用准确率与解释稳定性联合评估，以保障临床可审计性。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) has become a practical way to adapt deployed models to unlabeled target data, a setting that is especially relevant in computational pathology where staining, scanner, and cohort shifts are routine. While most TTA methods are evaluated by their effect on accuracy, clinical use also depends on whether the model's explanations remain reliable after adaptation. In this paper, we take a closer look at this largely unmeasured effect. We study explanation stability under TTA across two histopathology benchmarks, Camelyon17 and NCT CRC-HE, using five architectures ranging from convolutional networks to vision transformers and a pathology foundation model, seventeen TTA methods, and four attribution families. Across 2,958 adaptation runs, we observe a clear and systematic pattern: TTA methods differ sharply in how much they move model explanations, with frozen-backbone methods leaving attributions almost unchanged and continual methods such as CoTTA and RoTTA causing the largest drift. This effect is not uniform. Convolutional networks are substantially more sensitive than transformer and foundation-model backbones, and explanation drift increases with adaptation strength while remaining largely insensitive to batch size. Surprisingly, explanation stability is only weakly coupled to adaptation quality. Some methods preserve explanations almost perfectly while degrading calibration or accuracy, producing silent failures that would be missed by accuracy-only or explanation-only evaluation. These findings show that explanation stability is a distinct reliability axis for TTA in computational pathology. We release the metric, protocol, and full benchmark to support future work on adaptation methods that are not only accurate, but also stable and clinically auditable. Code: https://github.com/bahumanyarg11/tta-explanation-stability-pipeline

</details>


### [51] [RoRA: Role-Oriented Regional Allocation for Visual Token Pruning in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07088)
*Qiyanhui Lu,Han Wu,Rongjian Xu,Tingzhang Luo,Cheng Fan,Xinghao Chen,Minjing Dong,Jufeng Yang,Jianyuan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoRA是一个免训练的视觉token剪枝框架，通过"角色导向的区域证据分配"将token划分为受保护的语义核心、补充上下文和细粒度细节三类，利用Attention-Anchored Regions (AARs)追踪已覆盖的对象区域，在多种MLLM上以较低计算开销保持良好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型将图像编码为长视觉token序列，导致预填充和KV缓存存储成本高昂。现有免训练剪枝方法虽基于重要性、多样性或空间覆盖来选择token，但将保留的token视为可互换的，未显式跟踪哪些与对象相关的区域已被覆盖，缺乏角色导向的区域级分配机制。

Method: RoRA框架包含以下关键步骤：(1) 用位置先验和提示校准的对象先验对文本条件注意力进行校准；(2) 从高置信度锚点构建Attention-Anchored Regions (AARs)作为已覆盖对象支持的轻量代理；(3) 在AARs外部主要探索上下文，同时用少量AAR引导的预算恢复局部细节；(4) 仅在上下文阶段使用成对相似度进行冗余过滤。

Result: 在匹配预算下，RoRA在LLaVA和Qwen-VL系列中一致优于强免训练基线。在LLaVA-1.5上，88.9%剪枝率下保留96.5%的完整性能；在Qwen3-VL上，75-90%剪枝率下比D2Pruner提升约5%。在66.7%剪枝率下，token选择仅需0.7ms，端到端推理时间减少24.6%，在NVIDIA H800上实现1.33倍加速。

Conclusion: RoRA通过引入角色导向的区域证据分配和AAR机制，有效解决了现有免训练剪枝方法未考虑对象区域覆盖追踪的问题，在极高压剪枝率下仍能保持大部分未剪枝性能，同时显著降低推理开销，是一种高效且实用的视觉token剪枝方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) encode images as long visual token sequences, making prefilling and KV-cache storage expensive. Existing training-free pruning methods select tokens by importance, diversity, or spatial coverage, but treat retained tokens as interchangeable and do not explicitly track which object-related regions are already covered. We present RoRA, a training-free framework that casts visual token pruning as role-oriented regional evidence allocation. Given a fixed budget, RoRA partitions tokens into a protected semantic core, complementary context, and fine-grained detail. It first calibrates text-conditioned attention with a positional prior and a prompt-calibrated object prior, then builds Attention-Anchored Regions (AARs) from high-confidence anchors as lightweight proxies for covered object support. Context is explored mainly outside AARs, while a small AAR-guided budget restores local detail; pairwise similarity is used only for context-stage redundancy filtering. Under matched budgets, RoRA consistently outperforms strong training-free baselines across LLaVA and Qwen-VL families, retaining most of the unpruned accuracy even at aggressive pruning ratios, e.g., 96.5% of full performance at 88.9% pruning on LLaVA-1.5, and improving over D2Pruner by about 5% on Qwen3-VL at 75-90% pruning. At a 66.7% pruning ratio, RoRA requires only 0.7 ms for token selection and reduces end-to-end inference time by 24.6%, corresponding to a 1.33x speedup over unpruned inference on an NVIDIA H800.

</details>


### [52] [International Transfer of Stochastic Cortical Self-Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07092)
*Fabian Bongratz,Zhizheng Zhuo,Chao Zhang,Yaou Liu,Dennis M. Hedderich,Christian Wachinger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机皮层自重建（SCSR）方法从UK Biobank到中国人群的跨人群泛化能力。通过四种训练策略（直接应用、微调、从头训练、联合训练）和两种骨干网络（MLP和SUNet）的比较，验证了SCSR在中国人群中检测皮层萎缩的鲁棒性，其中微调后的SUNet模型取得了最佳判别性能（平均成对AUC=0.848），且重建误差在整个人生跨度内保持较低，表明SCSR具有良好的跨人群可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的规范建模方法（normative modeling）通常以粗粒度区域级别操作，且受限于训练时包含的协变量，无法在顶点级别提供个体化的健康参考。SCSR能够直接从顶点级别的皮层厚度估计个体化健康参考，从而检测细微的、受试者特异性的偏离。然而，SCSR的跨人群泛化能力尚未得到验证，本文旨在探究其在独立中国人群数据集上的可迁移性。

Method: 本文评估了四种训练策略：直接应用UKB预训练模型、在中国数据上微调、从头训练、以及UKB与中国数据联合训练。使用MLP和Spherical UNet（SUNet）两种重建骨干网络。通过SCSR导出的Z分数来区分健康扫描、轻度认知障碍（MCI）和阿尔茨海默病（AD）患者，同时评估模型在整个人生跨度上的鲁棒性。

Result: 所有评估模型在中国人群中均表现出对皮层萎缩的稳健检测能力。微调后的SUNet模型取得了最高的判别性能（平均成对AUC=0.848），紧随其后的是UKB训练的SUNet模型。重建误差在整个生命周期内保持较低水平，即使训练人群的年龄分布明显更窄，仍表现出较强的跨人群迁移性。

Conclusion: SCSR作为一种个性化皮层萎缩映射方法，具有出色的跨人群可迁移性和泛化能力。即使面对不同种族背景和不同年龄分布的人群，SCSR仍能提供稳健的皮层萎缩检测，其中SUNet骨干结合微调策略是最优配置。这一发现支持SCSR在不同临床环境和多样化人群中的广泛应用。

Abstract: Stochastic cortical self-reconstruction (SCSR) enables personalized mapping of gray matter atrophy, a hallmark of neurodegenerative disorders such as Alzheimer's disease (AD), onto high-resolution cortical surfaces. Unlike conventional normative modeling approaches, which typically operate at a coarse regional level and remain inherently constrained by the covariates included during training, SCSR estimates an individualized healthy reference directly from the observed cortical thickness at the vertex level. This allows the detection of subtle, subject-specific deviations from healthy cortical shape. In this work, we investigate the generalization and transferability of SCSR, originally trained on UK Biobank (UKB) data, to an independent Chinese population dataset. Specifically, we evaluate the ability of SCSR-derived Z-scores to discriminate between healthy scans, individuals with mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and patients with AD, while also assessing model robustness across the lifespan. We compare four training strategies: direct application of the UKB-trained model, fine-tuning on Chinese data, training from scratch, and joint training on UKB and Chinese cohorts. As reconstruction backbones, we consider both a multilayer perceptron (MLP) and a Spherical UNet (SUNet). Our results demonstrate that SCSR provides robust detection of cortical atrophy in the Chinese population across all evaluated models. The highest discriminative performance was achieved by the fine-tuned SUNet model (average pairwise AUC = 0.848), followed closely by the UKB-trained SUNet. Moreover, reconstruction errors remained low across the lifespan, even when the training population exhibited a substantially narrower age distribution, indicating strong cross-population transferability.

</details>


### [53] [Geometry-Aware Camera Localization for Bronchoscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07116)
*Lumin Chen,Qingyao Tian,Jinpeng Li,Haoyu Jiang,Huai Liao,Xinyan Huang,Hongbin Liu,Dong Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的几何感知支气管镜定位框架（GABL），通过融合术前结构先验与术中视频，结合图引导的粗到细定位方案和基于Transformer的跟踪模型，实现了毫米级精度和高实时性的6-DoF相机位姿估计，在平移和旋转误差上分别较最先进方法降低8.37%和31.76%，推理速度提升4倍（33.6 FPS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 支气管镜相机定位面临精度要求严苛、实时性约束强和训练数据有限三大挑战。相比自然场景，受限的解剖结构要求毫米级精度，术中导航需要低延迟推理，但现有方法未能有效利用术前几何先验，导致鲁棒性和精度受限。

Method: 提出统一框架GABL：(1) 融合术前结构先验与配对术中视频估计6-DoF相机位姿；(2) 提出图引导的粗到细定位方案，利用结构先验解决复杂气道中的视觉模糊性；(3) 集成基于Transformer的跟踪模型并设计新颖的RGB-深度匹配目标函数，联合施加时空一致性与几何一致性约束，缓解位姿抖动并弥合视觉-结构域差距。

Result: 与最先进方法相比，平移误差降低8.37%，旋转误差降低31.76%，推理速度提升4倍，达到33.6 FPS，满足了支气管镜实时定位的实际部署需求。

Conclusion: GABL框架有效证明了术前几何先验与术中视频融合的可行性，为支气管镜实时定位提供了一种鲁棒且高效的解决方案，兼顾了精度与实时性。

Abstract: Camera localization in bronchoscopy remains a challenging problem due to stringent accuracy requirements, real-time constraints, and limited training data. Compared to natural scenes, the confined anatomical structures demand millimeter-level precision, while intraoperative guidance necessitates low-latency inference. However, existing methods often fail to effectively exploit preoperative geometric priors, limiting their robustness and accuracy. To address these limitations, we propose a unified geometry-aware bronchoscope localization framework (GABL) that effectively fuses preoperative structural priors with paired intraoperative video to estimate 6-DoF camera poses. Specifically, to address visual ambiguity in complex airways, we propose a graph-guided coarse-to-fine localization scheme that effectively leverages structural priors for precise pose estimation. Furthermore, to mitigate pose jitter and bridge the visual-structural gap, we integrate a Transformer-based tracking model with a novel RGB-depth matching objective, jointly enforcing spatio-temporal and geometric consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method yields remarkable reductions of 8.37% and 31.76% in translation and rotation errors over the prior state-of-the-art, alongside 4 times inference speedup (33.6 FPS) for robust real-time bronchoscope localization. Project website: https://paulili08.github.io/GABL/.

</details>


### [54] [Beyond Fluency: A Clinical Benchmark and Anomaly-Enhanced Baseline for Spine MRI Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07117)
*Bruno Palau,Franziska Vogt,Daria Laslo,Haobo Li,Ender Konukoglu,Maria Monzon,Catherine R. Jutzeler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对腰椎MRI的自动化报告生成任务，指出现有VLM在标准词法与语义指标上得分高但临床诊断正确性差的问题，提出一种与架构无关的框架，通过半监督U-Net++生成盘级异常热图来增强VLM输入，从而提升解剖敏感性和提供可解释性输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射科报告书写耗时且存在阅片者间变异性，自动化报告生成是VLM的重要临床应用。然而标准词法与语义指标无法反映临床正确性——流畅规范的报告可能包含严重诊断错误，需要更可靠的评估与改方法。

Method: 提出一个与架构无关的框架，使用半监督U-Net++模型生成空间定位的、盘级异常热图，将其作为额外输入增强VLM，为模型提供显式视觉grounding，同时热图本身作为独立的可解释性输出供临床监督。

Result: 标准词法与语义评估指标不能有效反映临床正确性；所提出的热图增强框架能提升模型的解剖敏感性（通过显式视觉grounding），并提供独立的可解释性输出以支持临床监督。

Conclusion: 基于空间定位的异常热图增强策略，能够推动VLM在腰椎MRI解读中向诊断可靠且视觉可grounding的方向发展，提高临床实用性和可信任度。

Abstract: Radiology reporting is time-consuming and subject to inter-rater variability, making automated report generation an attractive clinical application for Vision-Language Models (VLMs). We benchmark state-of-the-art VLMs on lumbar spine MRI with a focus on diagnostic accuracy and demonstrate that standard lexical and semantic metrics poorly reflect clinical correctness: fluent, well-structured reports can score highly while containing clinically meaningful diagnostic errors. To address this failure mode, we propose an architecture-agnostic framework that augments VLM inputs with spatially localized, disc-level anomaly heatmaps generated by a semi-supervised U-Net++ model. These heatmaps both improve anatomical sensitivity through explicit visual grounding and provide an independent interpretability output for clinical oversight, moving us closer to diagnostically reliable, visually grounded VLMs for lumbar spine MRI interpretation.

</details>


### [55] [Multiple Hypothesis Flow Estimation for Video Frame Interpolation under Matching Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07120)
*Zibo Su,Jing Kong,Ruixing Wang,Zhanhe Zhang,Kun Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多假设光流估计框架用于视频帧插值（VFI）。传统单流方法在模糊区域（重复纹理、对称结构、快速运动）只能保留一个候选位移，可能选错导致伪影。该方法保留top-K候选对应关系，通过可靠性引导路由器逐位置选择一个假设，并从粗匹配锚点初始化、经锚点中心局部注意力分别细化每个假设，帧合成基于选定的单一流-外观假设而非候选运动的软组合。在MA-HD基准和公开VFI基准上，该方法在LPIPS和DISTS指标上达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于光流的VFI方法在模糊/歧义区域中，单流估计器只能保留一个位移而丢弃其他合理的候选匹配。若选错或与邻居不一致，会导致重影、结构畸变或模糊。由于缺乏光流标注且真值中间帧的监督具有间接性，无法唯一定位模糊区域的潜在对应关系，因此需要一种保留多个候选假设的选择机制来克服此局限。

Method: 提出多假设光流估计框架：1）保留top-K候选对应关系而非单一匹配；2）通过一个可靠性引导的路由器在逐位置上选择最优假设；3）每个假设从粗匹配锚点初始化，并通过锚点中心的局部注意力独立细化；4）帧合成基于被选中的单一流-外观假设进行条件化，而非对候选运动做软组合。

Result: 在所提出的MA-HD基准测试集以及多个公开VFI基准上，该方法在LPIPS和DISTS两项感知质量指标上均超越对比方法，取得最优成绩。

Conclusion: 通过保留并智能选择多个光流假设，而非强制单流估计，可以有效解决模糊区域中匹配歧义的问题，从而减少重影与结构失真，提升帧插值结果的感知质量。

Abstract: Many flow-based video frame interpolation (VFI) methods synthesize an intermediate frame by estimating optical flow fields, warping the two input frames, and blending the warped observations. These latent flow fields are typically learned through image-level reconstruction supervision without direct flow annotations. In ambiguous regions containing repetitive or stochastic textures, rotating symmetric structures, or fast motion with blur, the matching evidence for a single query may contain multiple comparable and spatially separated peaks. Although the ground-truth intermediate frame provides indirect supervision, it may not uniquely identify the latent correspondence in ambiguous regions.When several locations provide multiple plausible matches, a single-flow estimator can retain only one displacement and discard the remaining candidates. If the selected match is incorrect or inconsistent with those of neighboring pixels, warping samples content from mismatched locations, producing ghosting, structural distortion, or blur.To address this limitation, we propose a multiple hypothesis flow estimation framework that preserves top-K candidate correspondences and selects one per location through a reliability-guided router. Each hypothesis is initialized from a coarse matching anchor and refined separately through anchor-centered local attention. Frame synthesis is thus conditioned on one selected flow-appearance hypothesis rather than a soft combination of candidate motions.Experiments on the proposed MA-HD benchmark and public VFI benchmarks show that our method achieves the best LPIPS and DISTS among the compared methods.

</details>


### [56] [Human-AI Perceptual Alignment by Playing Hues and Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07141)
*Nuria Alabau-Bosque,Jorge Vila-Tomás,Paula Daudén-Oliver,Pablo Hernández-Cámara,Valero Laparra,Jesús Malo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于桌面游戏Hues and Cues的色彩空间来评估对比视觉语言模型(CVLM)与人类感知对齐度的新框架。通过将480个颜色单元映射到CIE xy色度图，基于325名人类观察者的颜色关联数据建立人类一致性基线，评估了162个模型。结果发现CVLMs能复现人类认知偏差（如食物和植物的理想化记忆色），但在抽象、主观和流行文化领域系统性偏离人类基线，并识别出语义错分类和不确定性坍塌为默认蓝色两种严重失配模式。高度精选的预训练数据比大规模未精选语料更能缓解这些失配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基准测试往往忽视细粒度的语义和文化细微差别，无法充分评估CVLM与人类感知的对齐程度。需要一种新颖的评估框架来揭示模型在细粒度颜色语义理解上的深层偏差。

Method: 利用棋盘游戏Hues and Cues的游戏化离散颜色空间，将480个颜色单元映射到CIE xy色度图；通过自定义数字界面收集325名人类观察者的颜色关联数据，用留一法交叉验证建立人类一致性基线（经验下界）；评估跨多个架构家族和预训练数据集的162个模型。

Result: CVLMs能复制人类的认知偏差（如具体物理指涉物的理想化记忆颜色），但在抽象、主观和流行文化领域系统性偏离人类基线。识别出两种严重失配模式：语义错分类和不确定性坍塌为默认蓝色坐标。高度精选的预训练数据集比大规模未精选语料更有效缓解严重失配。

Conclusion: 当前CVLMs尽管具备广泛分类能力，仍无法捕捉人类颜色记忆的细微、局部化共识。游戏化任务在揭示模型固有偏差方面具有重要价值，数据与代码已公开以供进一步测试。

Abstract: Evaluating the perceptual alignment between Contrastive Vision-Language Models (CVLMs) and humans is typically constrained by traditional benchmarks that overlook fine-grained semantic and cultural nuances. In this work, we propose a novel evaluation framework that leverages the gamified, discrete color space of the board game Hues and Cues. By mapping the board's 480 color cells to the CIE xy chromaticity diagram, we calculate empirical perceptual distances across a carefully curated 100-word vocabulary spanning seven semantic categories. To properly contextualize model performance, we establish an empirical lower bound of expected error-the Human Consistency baseline-calculated via Leave-One-Out (LOO) cross-validation on a dense dataset of color associations collected from 325 human observers through a custom digital interface. We evaluate 162 models across multiple architectural families and pre-training datasets to assess their semantic color grounding. Our results demonstrate that while CVLMs successfully replicate human cognitive biases, such as idealized memory colors for concrete physical referents (e.g., food and plants), they systematically diverge from the human baseline in abstract, subjective, and pop-culture domains. We identify two distinct failure modes in severely misaligned concepts: semantic misclassification and a systematic uncertainty collapse into a default blue coordinate. Furthermore, we reveal that highly curated pre-training datasets are significantly more effective than massive, uncurated corpora in mitigating these severe misalignments. Ultimately, this work highlights that despite their broad categorization capabilities, current CVLMs still fail to capture the nuanced, localized consensus of human color memory, emphasizing the value of gamified tasks in exposing underlying model biases. The data and code are publicly available to test other metrics.

</details>


### [57] [InstanceSplat: Instance-Aware Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting for Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07144)
*Minchao Jiang,Xiaoxuan Ma,Shunyu Jia,Haoru Wang,Zhang Liang,Wentao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InstanceSplat是一个统一的feed-forward 3DGS框架，从无位姿多视角图像中同时实现通用3D重建和实例感知场景理解。它在单次前向传播中构建实例感知的高斯表示，联合编码外观、几何、实例身份和语言对齐语义，并通过实例中心学习策略实现重建、实例学习和语义学习的相互促进，在多种任务上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有feed-forward 3DGS方法在场景理解上仍是类别导向的，而实例感知3DGS方法依赖逐场景优化，且常将重建与实例/语义学习解耦，缺乏三者间的交互促进。本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: InstanceSplat包含三个关键设计：(1) 统一的实例感知高斯表示，联合编码外观、几何、实例身份和语言对齐语义；(2) 共享3D高斯跨视图锚定实例身份，生成可渲染且跨视图一致的实例特征；(3) 实例中心学习策略，通过共享实例结构连接重建、实例学习和语义学习——实例线索引导重建、语言对齐语义增强同类别难分实例的判别力、实例区域将语义证据聚合为连贯的物体级预测。

Result: 在新视角合成、实例分割和开放词汇语义理解任务上，在不同输入视角设置和未见数据集上均达到最先进性能，同时展示了实际效率和强泛化能力。

Conclusion: InstanceSplat首次实现了无需逐场景优化的通用实例感知3DGS框架，证明了重建、实例学习和语义学习之间通过共享实例结构可以实现互利互惠，为通用3D场景理解提供了统一高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables efficient and generalizable 3D reconstruction, but current feed-forward 3DGS methods for scene understanding remain largely category-oriented. In contrast, instance-aware 3DGS methods typically rely on per-scene optimization and often decouple reconstruction from instance and semantic learning, limiting reciprocal interactions among them. We present InstanceSplat, a unified feed-forward 3DGS framework for generalizable 3D reconstruction and instance-aware scene understanding from pose-free multi-view images. In a single forward pass, InstanceSplat constructs an instance-aware Gaussian representation that jointly encodes appearance, geometry, instance identity, and language-aligned semantics. Shared 3D Gaussians ground instance identities across views, producing renderable and cross-view-consistent instance features. To allow reconstruction and scene understanding to benefit from each other, we further design an instance-centric learning strategy that connects reconstruction, instance learning, and semantic learning through shared instance structure. Specifically, instance cues guide reconstruction, language-aligned semantics strengthen the discrimination of confusing same-category instances, and instance regions aggregate semantic evidence into coherent object-level predictions. Experiments on novel-view synthesis, instance segmentation, and open-vocabulary semantic understanding under varying input-view settings and on an unseen dataset demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, practical efficiency, and strong generalization.

</details>


### [58] [Representation-driven Endoscopic Visual Embedding Alignment for Latent Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07176)
*Francisco Caetano,Tim J. M. Jaspers,Haiko Middeljans,Martijn R. Jong,Rixta A. H. van Eijck van Heslinga,Floor Slooter,Albert J. de Groof,Jacques J. Bergman,Peter H. N. De With,Fons van der Sommen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REVEAL是迄今为止最大的内窥镜生成式基础模型，基于500万帧多中心数据集GastroNet-5M训练，通过在 endoscopic 分布上直接预训练的编码器对齐扩散隐空间与领域特定视觉特征，既实现高保真图像生成，又可作为特征提取器，在分类基准上与EndoViT、Endo-FM等内窥镜基础模型表现相当甚至更优，同时具备较强的表征鲁棒性和结构性编辑能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内窥镜领域的生成式基础模型发展受限于两个瓶颈：自然图像与临床图像之间存在域差距，以及大规模扩散Transformer的训练计算成本高昂。尽管表示对齐（representation alignment）在通用计算机视觉中已证明能提升效率，但其在内窥镜图像这一高度专业化的领域中是否有效仍不清楚，因此需要探索是否可以通过领域内表示对齐来构建高效的内窥镜生成基础模型。

Method: 本文提出了REVEAL模型，其核心创新在于：1）构建了GastroNet-5M（GN-5M）多中心内窥镜图像数据集（500万帧）；2）不使用域外先验，而是采用直接在内窥镜分布上预训练的编码器，将扩散模型的隐空间与内窥镜领域特定的视觉特征进行对齐，从而保留细粒度纹理和精细解剖结构；3）该模型同时作为生成模型和特征提取器使用。

Result: REVEAL在多个基准上表现出色：作为特征提取器，其分类性能与内窥镜领域专有基础模型（如EndoViT和Endo-FM）相当甚至超越；在真实成像退化条件下展现出强大的表征鲁棒性；生成高保真图像，并在潜空间编辑（如inpainting和outpainting）中保持稳健的结构一致性。

Conclusion: REVEAL通过领域内预训练编码器与扩散隐空间的表示对齐，成功降低了构建专用临床工具的计算门槛。该模型提供了一个开放、通用的基础平台，可用于条件合成、分割以及未来智能胃肠病学系统中的分布外检测，证明了表示对齐策略在内窥镜这一专业领域中的有效性。

Abstract: Developing foundation generative models for endoscopy is limited by the gap between natural and clinical images and the computational cost of training large Diffusion Transformers. Although representation alignment has improved efficiency in general computer vision, its role within the highly specialized endoscopic image space remains unclear. We introduce REVEAL (Representation-driven Endoscopic Visual Embedding Alignment), the largest generative foundation model for endoscopy to date, trained on GastroNet-5M (GN-5M), a multicenter dataset of 5 million endoscopic frames. Instead of depending on out-of-domain priors, REVEAL employs encoders pretrained directly on the endoscopic distribution to align diffusion latents with domain-specific visual features, preserving fine textures and intricate anatomical structures. Beyond image generation, REVEAL also serves as a powerful feature extractor; in multiple benchmarks, it delivers performance that is competitive with, and in several cases exceeds, endoscopic foundation models such as EndoViT and Endo-FM, specifically tuned for classification tasks, while demonstrating strong representation robustness under realistic imaging corruptions. REVEAL produces high-fidelity images and maintains robust structural coherence in latent-space edits such as inpainting and outpainting. This high-capacity backbone lowers the computational threshold for building specialized clinical tools, offering an open, versatile foundation for conditional synthesis, segmentation, and out-of-distribution detection in future intelligent gastroenterology systems.

</details>


### [59] [Flow-Corrected Shape Optimization: Taming Manifold Drift in High-Dimensional 3D Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07199)
*Emilien Seiler,Nicolas Talabot,Yingxuan You,Federico Stella,Pascal Fua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种optimizer-corrector框架，通过在目标最小化的梯度步与基于guided flow matching的校正步之间交替，解决深度生成模型潜在空间中3D形状优化时的manifold drift（流形漂移）问题，在保持几何有效性与表达力的同时实现计算上可扩展的3D形状优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在深度生成模型潜在空间中进行3D形状优化是计算机辅助工程的基础，但梯度优化常使潜在向量偏离有效形状流形（即manifold drift）。该问题在状态先进的高维潜在空间模型中尤为严重，因为有效形状仅占据极小的空间比例。现有缓解策略（如潜在正则化和流匹配方法）要么牺牲表达能力，要么在目标引导与生成保真度之间存在难以调和的权衡而仍易发生流形漂移，要么在现代化大容量3D形状模型上计算上不可行。

Method: 提出一种新颖的optimizer-corrector框架：在目标最小化的梯度步与guided flow matching校正步之间交替进行。通过将目标优化与基于流的校正解耦——优化自由进行、校正严格约束——该交替设计避免了内在权衡，在不牺牲表达力的前提下保持几何有效性，并在现代3D形状模型上保持计算可行性。

Result: 在多种复杂度的生成先验上验证了有效性，从简单的向量潜在空间到大规模架构，涵盖多种下游优化任务，包括气动减阻和物体柔度优化。

Conclusion: 该optimizer-corrector框架有效解决了3D形状潜在空间优化中的流形漂移问题，通过解耦优化与校正避免了表达力与保真度之间的权衡，具有良好的可扩展性，可应用于现代大规模3D形状模型。

Abstract: Optimizing 3D shapes within the latent spaces of deep generative models is fundamental to computer assisted engineering, yet remains prone to a critical failure mode we term manifold drift: the tendency of gradient-based optimization to move latent vectors away from the manifold of valid shapes. This problem is exacerbated in state-of-the-art 3D shape generative models that operate in increasingly high-dimensional latent spaces where valid shapes occupy a vanishingly small fraction of the full space. Existing mitigation strategies, including latent regularization and flow-matching approaches, either sacrifice expressiveness, demand a difficult trade-off between objective guidance and generative fidelity that remains prone to manifold drift, or are computationally infeasible to scale to modern, large-capacity 3D shape models. We introduce a novel optimizer-corrector framework that alternates between gradient steps for objective minimization and guided flow matching to drive the latent state back to the valid shape manifold. By decoupling objective minimization from flow-based correction, optimizing freely and correcting strictly, this alternating design avoids inherent trade-offs, preserving geometric validity without sacrificing expressiveness while remaining computationally feasible on modern 3D shape models. We demonstrate its effectiveness across generative priors of varying complexity, from simple vector latent spaces to large-scale architectures across a variety of downstream optimization tasks, including aerodynamic drag reduction and object compliance optimization.

</details>


### [60] [CANIS: Generation-Assisted 3D Canonicalization via an Image-Semantic Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07256)
*Kendong Liu,Yuxin Yao,Junhui Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CANIS是一种类别无关的、生成辅助的3D物体姿态规范化框架，利用冻结的图像到3D生成模型的语义朝向先验，无需专门的规范化训练或类别模板，通过渲染、生成代理对象、语义锚点约束几何匹配来估计刚性变换，实现对3D物体朝向的语义规范化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D物体朝向规范化对于3D理解与分析至关重要，但现有方法大多依赖几何线索，而真正的3D规范化需要语义上有意义的朝向。现有方法缺乏语义信息，无法解决这一根本性差距。

Method: CANIS首先从候选视点渲染输入对象，选取信息量最大的视图，并利用冻结的图像到3D生成模型生成处于规范化朝向的代理对象；生成过程中，输入对象的稀疏结构潜变量引导代理保持几何结构；随后以所选图像为语义桥梁，通过图像补丁识别代理上的语义区域，再利用深度反投影在输入上定位对应区域，得到语义锚点来约束几何匹配，从而估计将输入规范化的刚性变换。整个过程无需类别特定的模板或规范化专属训练。

Result: 在合成基准上验证了CANIS及其关键组件的有效性；在部分观测和OmniObject3D上的定性结果表明其适用于不完整及真实世界扫描数据。CANIS还提升了任意旋转下下游3D分类、部件分割和稠密对应任务的性能。

Conclusion: CANIS通过融合生成模型的语义朝向先验与几何匹配，成功实现了类别无关的3D语义朝向规范化，并在真实与合成数据上展现出良好泛化能力，显著改善了下游3D理解任务的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Canonicalizing 3D object orientation is fundamental to 3D understanding and analysis. Existing approaches often rely on geometric cues, although 3D canonicalization ultimately requires a semantically meaningful orientation. To address this gap, we propose CANIS, a category-agnostic, generation-assisted framework that introduces the semantic orientation prior of a frozen image-to-3D generative model into 3D canonicalization, without canonicalization-specific training or category-specific templates. Specifically, CANIS first renders the input object from candidate viewpoints, selects an informative view, and generates a proxy in a canonical orientation. During generation, a sparse structural latent encoded from the input guides the proxy to preserve the geometry of an object. CANIS then uses the selected image as a semantic bridge between the input and the proxy. Image patches identify semantic regions on the proxy, and depth back-projection locates the corresponding regions on the input. The resulting semantic anchors constrain geometric matching, from which we estimate the rigid transformation that canonicalizes the input. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks validate CANIS and its key components, while qualitative results on partial observations and OmniObject3D suggest its applicability to incomplete and real-world scans. CANIS also improves downstream 3D classification, part segmentation, and dense correspondence under arbitrary rotations. Project page: https://kenkenzaii.github.io/Canis.

</details>


### [61] [Foundation Models Adaptation for Multi-View Multi-modal Cardiac MRI Segmentation and Direct Ejection Fraction Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07291)
*Sina Amirrajab,Cian M Scannell,Volker Vehof,Michael Bietenbeck,Ali Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索了基础模型在心脏MRI多视图、多序列分割和左心室射血分数估计中的迁移能力，通过微调CineMA模型进行cine和LGE分割，并结合两个冻结的CMR基础模型通过注意力多实例学习实现LVEF直接回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在心脏MRI中展现出强迁移能力，但其在异质性的多视图、多序列CMR分析中的有效性尚不明确，因此需要系统评估基础模型在CMR-Multi挑战中的微调与组合效果。

Method: 采用两种策略：1) 微调CineMA基础模型用于cine和LGE分割，覆盖短轴和长轴视图；2) 使用两个冻结的CMR基础模型提取嵌入向量，通过基于注意力的多实例学习融合嵌入进行LVEF直接回归。

Result: cine分割在短轴、两腔心和四腔心视图上分别取得0.862、0.883和0.902的Dice分数；LGE分割Dice分数在0.621到0.846之间；LVEF直接回归模型MAE为4.96个百分点，Pearson相关系数为0.91。

Conclusion: 基础模型可有效适配和组合用于多视图CMR分析，但准确的LGE疤痕分割仍具有挑战性。

Abstract: Foundation models have shown strong transferability in cardiac MRI (CMR), but their effectiveness for heterogeneous multi-view and multi-sequence CMR analysis remains unclear. In this work, we explore the effectiveness of fine-tuning and combining different CMR foundation models for the Universal Multi-Sequence, Multi-Center and Multi-View CMR Segmentation (CMR-Multi) Challenge. CineMA was fine-tuned for cine and late gadolinium enhancement (LGE) segmentation across short-axis and long-axis views. For direct left-ventricular ejection fraction (LVEF) estimation, we used two recent frozen CMR foundation models to extract embedding vectors that were then combined using attention-based multiple-instance learning for LVEF regression. In the challenge validation set, cine segmentation achieved Dice scores of 0.862, 0.883, and 0.902 for short-axis, two-chamber and four-chamber cine MRI, respectively. LGE segmentation achieved Dice scores between 0.621 and 0.846 across views. The direct LVEF regression model achieved an MAE of 4.96 percentage points and a Pearson correlation of 0.91. These results indicate that foundation models can be effectively adapted and combined for multi-view CMR analysis, while accurate LGE scar segmentation remains a challenging task.

</details>


### [62] [EliSeg: Verified Target Construction for Report-Grounded Abnormality Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07299)
*Chengyi Peng,Haoyu Yang,Meixing Shi,Yuxiang Cai,Yankai Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EliSeg，一个面向报告引导的异常分割框架，采用actor-verify-revise架构，无需目标身份或空间提示，直接从非过滤放射报告中确定目标资格、基数及发现到掩码的对应关系，并生成分割掩码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的放射学分割方法在推理前需要目标身份或空间提示作为"隐藏目标oracle"，绕过了报告的歧义性。然而实际放射报告包含存在、否定、既往、不确定或不相关的发现，且多种异常可能共存，模型需要直接从未过滤的报告中确定目标资格、基数和发现-掩码对应关系，这一挑战尚未被系统解决。

Method: 提出EliSeg框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 语法约束的Actor负责提出目标槽位和对应的掩码；2) 独立的纯文本Verifier重建合格发现清单（inventory）；3) Revision阶段当Actor与Verifier的目标结构不一致时，选择性重新执行共享Actor。整体形成actor-verify-revise的迭代流程。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR-ILS数据集上，EliSeg在各类发现上持续优于直接分割方法和extract-then-segment级联方法，同时有效抑制不合格报告提及的掩码。消融实验证实了验证与修订的互补作用；在CheXlocalize上的评估展示了对外部数据集的有效迁移。

Conclusion: EliSeg无需预定义目标身份、发现提示、点或边界框即可实现报告引导的异常分割，通过actor-verify-revise框架有效处理报告的歧义性，并在内外数据集上均表现优异。

Abstract: Radiology reports describe clinical observations but do not specify executable segmentation targets. They may contain present, negated, prior,uncertain, or irrelevant findings, while multiple valid abnormalities may coexist. Existing segmentation methods largely bypass this ambiguity by receiving a target identity or spatial prompt before inference, which acts as a hidden target oracle. We study report-grounded abnormality segmentation, where a model must determine target eligibility, cardinality, and finding-to-mask correspondence directly from an unfiltered report before delineating the corresponding regions. We propose \textbf{EliSeg}, an atcor--verify--revise framework that integrates target construction with mask generation. A grammar-constrained Actor proposes target slots and masks, an independent text-only Verifier reconstructs the eligible finding inventory, and Revision selectively re-executes the shared Actor when their target structures disagree. EliSeg requires no predefined target identity, finding prompt, point, or bounding box. Experiments on MIMIC-CXR-ILS show that EliSeg consistently outperforms direct segmentation methods and extract-then-segment cascades across findings, while effectively suppressing masks for ineligible report mentions. Ablation studies confirm the complementary roles of verification and revision, and evaluation on CheXlocalize demonstrates effective transfer of the EliSeg to an external dataset.Code is available at https://github.com/Maybach-dream/EliSeg.

</details>


### [63] [Same Attention, Different Truths: Put Logit-Lens over Visual Attention to Detect and Mitigate LVLM Object Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07302)
*Zichuan Wang,Songlin Yang,Bo Peng,Zhenchen Tang,Yang Li,Beibei Dong,Jing Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了LVLM幻觉的核心机制：幻觉并非源于视觉注意力不足，而是源于"所关注区域无法正确解码为目标对象"。作者提出训练无关的Detect-Mitigate框架，通过Logit Lens一致性检查检测幻觉，并针对两种机制（视觉不确定性与上下文先验）分别采用HARM和VEED修复方法，在多个幻觉基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究将LVLM对象幻觉归因于视觉注意力不足，但作者发现真实对象与幻觉对象在模型中后期层获得同等强度的视觉注意力。因此真正的问题不在于"模型关注多少"，而在于"模型关注什么、为何关注"，需要深入剖析幻觉的底层机制并据此设计针对性的解决方案。

Method: (1) 使用Logit Lens解码高注意力区域的视觉特征，发现真实对象区域可正确解码为目标token，而幻觉对象区域无法解码。(2) 识别两种幻觉机制：视觉不确定性（由语义相似/混淆区域触发）和上下文先验（由强共现先验触发）。(3) 提出训练无关的Detect-Mitigate框架：Logit-Lens一致性检查用于检测幻觉；HARM（高注意力区域掩蔽）用于视觉不确定性幻觉，VEED（视觉证据增强解码）用于上下文先验幻觉。

Result: 该框架在多个幻觉基准测试上取得了最先进的结果，证明无需重新训练即可有效缓解LVLM的对象幻觉问题。

Conclusion: LVLM幻觉的根源在于高注意力区域本身缺乏可解码为目标的视觉证据，而非注意力总量不足。通过区分并针对性处理视觉不确定性与上下文先验两类机制，训练无关的方法即可显著缓解幻觉，为理解与修复LVLM幻觉提供了新的视角和实用工具。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) often suffer from object hallucination, generating objects that are absent from the image. Prior work largely attributes this to insufficient visual attention. However, we find that both real and hallucinated objects receive equally strong visual attention in the model's mid-to-late layers, suggesting that the key issue may not be how much the model attends, but what it attends to and why. To this end, we decode the visual features of high-attention regions using Logit Lens, and observe that regions corresponding to real objects can be correctly decoded to the target object tokens, whereas those for hallucinated objects cannot. Building on this, we identify two hallucination mechanisms: (i) visual uncertainty, triggered by semantically similar or confusable regions; masking these regions eliminates the hallucination. (ii) contextual prior, triggered by strong co-occurrence priors; even when the initially attended region is masked, the hallucination persists and attention drifts to other regions. Based on these findings, we propose a simple yet effective training-free Detect-Mitigate framework comprising a Logit-Lens Consistency Check to detect hallucination and targeted remedies: High-Attention Regions Masking (HARM) for visual uncertainty hallucination, and Visual Evidence Enhanced Decoding (VEED) for contextual prior hallucination. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art results on multiple hallucination benchmarks. Code will be available.

</details>


### [64] [H2AL: Hyperbolic Hierarchy-aware Aggregative Learning for Registration-based Few-shot Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07340)
*Jia Wang,Jiaming Cai,Zunying Hu,Zhanjie Wu,Jinyuan Liu,Hua Cheng,Yun Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出H2AL框架，将双曲空间的层次化建模能力引入基于配准的少样本医学图像分割（RFMIS），通过层次感知注入模块和梯度聚合的联合优化算法，同时提升配准变形合理性与解剖区域判别力，在五个实验设置下验证了方法的有效性与高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RFMIS方法主要基于欧几里得空间进行像素级优化与推理，将解剖结构视为平坦且孤立的对象，忽略了其固有的层次结构。这种假设导致伪标签质量下降，模糊区域的判别能力减弱，从而限制了分割性能。

Method: 提出H2AL框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Hyperbolic Hierarchy-aware Infusion (H2I)模块，利用双曲空间的层次建模能力，通过变换引导的监督式双曲对比学习学习精确的层次感知表示，并通过门控注入块将层次先验注入欧几里得空间； (2) 端到端联合优化算法，通过梯度聚合将来自配准解码器和分割解码器的语义与层次梯度聚合起来，共同更新共享编码器，促进跨任务协作学习。

Result: 在两个解剖区域、五种实验设置下的大量实验表明，该方法在配准和分割任务上均表现出有效性和高效性，代码已公开。

Conclusion: 通过将双曲空间的层次化建模能力引入RFMIS框架，H2AL有效解决了传统欧几里得空间方法忽视解剖结构层次性的问题，显著增强了变形合理性和解剖判别能力，为双任务协同学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Registration-based Few-shot medical image segmentation (RFMIS) aims to generate pseudo-labels for unlabeled images by warping a labeled image through registration. However, existing methods primarily perform pixel-level optimization and inference in Euclidean space, treating anatomical structures as flat and disjoint. This neglect of inherent hierarchies degrades pseudo-label quality and weakens the discrimination of ambiguous regions, limiting the segmentation performance. To overcome this challenge, we propose a Hyperbolic Hierarchy-aware Aggregative Learning framework for RFMIS, termed H2AL, that enhances both deformation plausibility and anatomical discrimination for dual-task learning.
