*Jing Chen,Yang Sun,Li Zhang,Lin Xu,Jie Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出轨迹归因（trajectory attribution）任务，构建了统一的基准和标注框架，将异构智能体轨迹按统一组件模式组织，并提供主要归因组件、攻击链和执行链的标注。基于AgentDojo和Agent3Sigma的Stage及Canary设置实例化出1300+条标注轨迹，定义了主归因定位和归因链恢复两个评估任务，并提供了两种参考基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体基准主要评估行为结果，缺乏对细粒度归因分析的支持，无法解释智能体在长时程轨迹中决策是由哪个组件（用户指令、工具使用、外部观察或记忆）驱动的。

Method: 提出统一组件模式（unified component schema）组织异构轨迹；构建标注框架提供主归因组件、攻击链和执行链标注；定义主归因定位和归因链恢复两个评估任务；提供基于增量轨迹贡献和组件级留一置换两种参考基线；发布可复用的标注技能以支持新模型轨迹的标准化标注。

Result: 实例化得到1300+条标注轨迹，覆盖任务对齐行为、不安全行为和安全拒绝场景；参考基线在不同归因设置（局部/长程/结构化归因链）下表现差异显著，初步刻画了基准的归因挑战。

Conclusion: 该基准和标注框架为LLM智能体轨迹归因分析提供了系统性支持，通过可复用标注技能实现跨模型的扩展能力，并为归因研究提供初始基准结果。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly operate through long-horizon trajectories involving user instructions, tool use, external observations, and memory. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate behavioral outcomes but provide limited support for fine-grained attribution analysis. We introduce trajectory attribution and develop a benchmark and annotation framework for this task. The benchmark organizes heterogeneous trajectories under a unified component schema and provides annotations of the primary attribution component, together with attack and execution chains where applicable. Instantiating the benchmark with trajectories from AgentDojo and the Stage and Canary settings of Agent3Sigma yields more than 1,300 annotated trajectories covering task-aligned actions, unsafe actions, and safety refusals. The benchmark defines two evaluation tasks, primary attribution localization and attribution-chain recovery, and provides reference baselines based on incremental trajectory contribution and component-level leave-one-out perturbation. It captures diverse attribution settings, including local and long-range attribution as well as structured attribution chains. Reference baseline results exhibit substantial performance differences across these settings, providing an initial characterization of the benchmark's attribution challenges. Beyond this initial instantiation, we release a reusable annotation skill that enables trajectories generated by new agent models to be standardized, annotated, and evaluated under the same framework. Project resources and future releases are available at https://github.com/chenjing-2024/agent-trajectory-attribution.

</details>


### [163] [Fast LapSum: Exact Differentiable Top-k at Million Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06912)
*Łukasz Struski,Joanna Wojciechowicz,Jakub Antczak,Marcin Mazur,Kamil Książek,Jacek Tabor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Fast LapSum，一种精确预算的soft top-k原语，其GPU求解器在排序后以线性时间运行，是首个在保持完全可微的同时精确保留k个选择质量的soft top-k方法。在百万到亿级分数上的开销几乎可忽略（0.41/1.15/5.23 ms），并展示了在百万像素稀疏对抗样本生成和全可微稀疏图像编码器训练中的实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: top-k操作是稀疏计算的核心构建块，但硬top-k阻塞梯度，而现有的连续（软）松弛方法在大规模模型上成本过高。此外，先前的线性时间方法（如DFTopK）放松了归一化约束，无法精确保持k个选择质量。

Method: Fast LapSum将线性时间阈值计算与分析向量-雅可比积（VJP）相结合；对于极端规模，采用概率括弧（probabilistic bracketing）技术，仅对核噪分数的不确定中间带进行排序。

Result: 求解器处理10^6、10^7、10^8个分数分别仅需0.41、1.15、5.23 ms。在生成百万像素级稀疏对抗样本（精确软预算约0.02%像素）时比最先进方法快一个数量级，并成功训练了完全可微的稀疏图像编码器。

Conclusion: Fast LapSum使精确soft top-k在大规模稀疏路由、检索和大规模优化中变得实用，是首个同时满足线性复杂度、全可微性和精确预算约束的方法。

Abstract: The top-$k$ operation is a fundamental building block of modern sparse computation, enabling token routing, expert activation, memory selection, and attention pruning. Yet standard hard top-$k$ blocks gradients, while existing continuous (soft) relaxations remain too costly for large-scale models. We introduce Fast LapSum, an exact-budget soft top-$k$ primitive whose GPU solver runs in linear time after sorting. Unlike prior linear-time methods such as DFTopK, which relax the normalization constraint, Fast LapSum is, to our knowledge, the first method to preserve an exact selection mass of $k$ while remaining fully differentiable end-to-end. Our solver combines a linear-time threshold computation with an analytical vector--Jacobian product, and for extreme scales employs probabilistic bracketing to sort only the uncertain middle band of kernel-noised scores. The resulting overhead is almost negligible: the solver processes $10^6$, $10^7$, and $10^8$ scores in $0.41$, $1.15$, and $5.23$\,ms, respectively. This makes exact soft top-$k$ practical for sparse routing, retrieval, and large-scale optimization. We demonstrate Fast LapSum on two demanding applications operating over millions of coordinates inside the training loop: generating megapixel sparse adversarial examples with an exact soft budget of ${\sim}0.02\%$ of an image's pixels, achieving an order-of-magnitude speedup over state-of-the-art methods, and training a fully differentiable sparse image coder from scratch.

</details>


### [164] [ReGraph: Learning to Generate Recipe Graphs from Food Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06917)
*Guoshan Liu,Bin Zhu,Pengkun Jiao,Jingjing Chen,Chong-Wah Ngo,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReGraph大规模食谱图数据集（包含食材、烹饪动作、工具等实体及类型化关系）和Recipe Graph Learning (RGL)两阶段框架，使大型多模态模型能够从食物图像生成结构化的烹饪流程图谱。实验发现现有方法在文本质量上表现良好但程序化结构恢复有限，而RGL在实体和关系生成上持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的从食物图像生成食谱的方法虽然取得了不错的文本质量，但烹饪本质上是一种结构化转换过程，食材在有序动作下发生状态变化，而自由形式的食谱语言将这些实体、中间状态和依赖关系隐式化且纠缠不清。需要一个图表示来显式地表达程序性知识，以评估模型输出是否真正编码了过程级知识，而不只是生成看似合理的文本描述。

Method: 构建ReGraph数据集，将食材、烹饪动作和工具建模为实体，用实体属性描述食材状态变化，用类型化关系编码操作目标、目的地和程序顺序；引入显式的Recipe Reasoning Chain-of-Thought (RR-CoT)轨迹为程序化分解和结构化图生成提供辅助监督。在此基础上提出Recipe Graph Learning (RGL)两阶段框架，使LMMs从食物图像生成结构化食谱图形式的细粒度烹饪工作流。

Result: 在确定性、模式感知的匹配协议下，实验显示文本生成质量与可恢复的程序化结构之间存在巨大差距：现有方法生成的食谱达到有竞争力的文本生成分数，但在ReGraph模式下只能恢复有限的参考对齐实体和关系结构。相比之下，在两个代表性LMM骨干网络上，RGL一致地提升了烹饪实体和程序关系的生成；进一步分析显示细粒度食材状态捕获仍是最具挑战性的维度。

Conclusion: RGL框架能有效提升LMMs在结构化烹饪流程知识上的生成能力，但食材中间状态变化的细粒度捕捉仍是未来需要突破的关键难点；同时需要关注文本质量与程序化结构能力之间的差距。

Abstract: Recent Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have achieved impressive performance in recipe generation from food images.However, cooking is a structured transformation process in which ingredients undergo state changes through ordered actions,while free-form recipe language leaves the corresponding entities, intermediate states, and dependencies largely implicit and entangled.A graph representation makes this procedural knowledge explicit and compositional, providing a structured basis for assessing whether model outputs encode process-level knowledge rather than merely presenting plausible textual descriptions. To address this limitation, we present ReGraph, a large-scale recipe graph dataset that represents ingredients, cooking actions, and tools as entities, uses entity attributes to describe ingredient state changes, and employs typed relations to encode manipulation targets, destinations, and procedural ordering. ReGraph further incorporates explicit Recipe Reasoning Chain-of-Thought (RR-CoT) traces, providing auxiliary supervision for procedural decomposition and structured graph generation. Building on ReGraph, we propose Recipe Graph Learning (RGL), a two-stage framework that enables LMMs to generate a plausible fine-grained cooking workflow from a food image in the form of a structured recipe graph. Under a deterministic, schema-aware matching protocol, our experiments reveal a substantial gap between text-generation quality and recoverable procedural structure: recipes produced by existing approaches achieve competitive text-generation scores yet yield limited reference-aligned entity and relation structure under the ReGraph schema. In contrast, across two representative LMM backbones, RGL consistently improves the generation of cooking entities and procedural relations, while our analysis further shows that fine-grained ingredient-state capture remains the most challenging dimension.

</details>


### [165] [Deal Me Maybe: The Role of Emotions in Multi-Agent Negotiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06922)
*Massimiliano Luca,Apoorva Singh,Bruno Lepri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了提示词情感状态如何影响LLM智能体的价格谈判，通过对350件真实消费品在两种预算条件下的谈判实验，发现情感显著影响谈判结果：愤怒买家几乎从不达成协议（成交率0.39%），快乐买家最常达成协议（28.91%）但价格较差，恐惧买家能获得更优价格。情感效应具有角色依赖性，且影响语言、终止行为和价格轨迹等多方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体谈判基准通常将智能体视为情感中立的，但人类谈判行为中情感是一个关键驱动因素。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统地研究提示词条件设定的情感状态对LLM谈判表现的影响。

Method: 构建受控谈判框架：买方和卖方智能体被独立分配六种情感状态之一，在两种预算条件下对350种真实消费品进行谈判；涵盖36种情感配对设置，测试五种主流LLM。通过在买家/卖家系统中注入特定情感状态的提示词条件，系统性地测量情感对成交率、价格轨迹、终止行为等方面的影响。

Result: 情感强烈塑造谈判结果：愤怒买家几乎永不达成协议（0.39%成交率），快乐买家最常达成协议（28.91%）但获得的价格劣于恐惧买家；情感效应具有角色依赖性——买家的情感主要驱动接受与拒绝，卖家的情感主要塑造让步动态；情感影响不仅体现在语言层面，还涉及终止行为和价格轨迹。

Conclusion: 情感条件化的LLM智能体在商务谈判中会产生显著的行为和结果差异，这对商业应用中情感条件化智能体的部署提出了安全性和可靠性方面的担忧，同时也说明在评估LLM谈判能力时需要将情感因素纳入考量。

Abstract: Negotiation is a demanding social task for LLM agents, requiring strategic reasoning, persuasion, and interpersonal adaptation. Yet existing benchmarks often treat agents as emotionally neutral, overlooking a key driver of human bargaining behavior. We study how prompt-conditioned emotions affect LLM-based price negotiation. In a controlled framework, buyer and seller agents are independently assigned one of six emotional states and negotiate over 350 real consumer products under two budget conditions. Across 36 emotion-pair settings and five widely used LLMs, we find that emotions strongly shape outcomes. Angry buyers almost never reach agreement (0.39% deal rate), while happy buyers agree most often (28.91%), but obtain worse prices than fearful buyers. Emotion effects are role-dependent: buyer emotion mainly drives acceptance and rejection, whereas seller emotion shapes concession dynamics. These effects influence not only language, but also termination behavior and price trajectories, raising concerns for emotion-conditioned agents in commerce.

</details>


### [166] [TRIBE: Predicting Team Performance via Communication Behavior Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06926)
*Ali Jalal-Kamali,Nikolos Gurney,David V. Pynadath,Fred Morstatter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRIBE是一个领域无关的方法，通过分析通信模式将团队分类为可预测表现的"行为部落"，最早在任务完成10%时即可实现及时干预，并在四个数据集上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计能有效协助人类团队的自主代理需要理解团队动态，但传统的绩效指标无法揭示这些动态，且需要任务特定知识。作者旨在提出一种无需任务特定知识的领域无关方法来揭示团队行为动态。

Method: TRIBE通过分析团队的通信模式，将团队分类为表现可预测的行为部落（behavioral tribes）。该方法在任务进行到10%时即可进行分类，支持及时干预。研究在四个不同数据集上测试，并进行了时间维度分析，比较了AI代理和人类顾问对团队行为轨迹的影响，同时与Llama进行了对比并优化了pipeline。

Result: 通信模式能够预测团队表现，预测强度随任务结构允许的行为自由度而变化。时间分析显示AI代理显著改变了团队行为轨迹，而人类顾问与自然动态保持一致；团队在协作过程中始终保持行为灵活性。TRIBE与Llama相比实现了显著加速且性能有所提升。

Conclusion: TRIBE提供了一种高效、领域无关的团队行为洞察方法，能够早期预测团队表现，并揭示了AI代理与人类顾问对团队动态的不同影响，为设计更有效的自主协作辅助代理提供了基础。

Abstract: Designing autonomous agents that effectively assist human teams hinges on understanding team dynamics, often without task specific knowledge. We present TRIBE, a domain independent approach that reveals team behavioral dynamics invisible to traditional performance metrics. We show that communication patterns can categorize teams into performance predictive behavioral tribes, as early as 10% into the task, enabling timely interventions. We test TRIBE on four diverse datasets and demonstrate that communication patterns predict team performance while the prediction strength varies by the degree a task structure allows for behavioral freedom. Our temporal analysis reveals that AI agents significantly alter team behavioral trajectories while human advisors align with natural dynamics, and that teams maintain behavioral flexibility throughout collaboration. Further, we compare TRIBE to Llama and optimize the pipeline, achieving significant speedup with performance improvement.

</details>


### [167] [Science Edge Evaluation: SEE the Missing Step Toward Real Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06931)
*Taolin Han,Yuchen Zhang,Jinghang Wang,Yun Wu,Wai Yuet Chiu,Zhaohai Li,Yifei Zhang,Jinxin Wang,Yuhao Zhou,Chen Zhao,Jiajia Li,Jiaxin Li,Qile Jin,Kewei Sun,Shuang Wu,Weiqi Zhai,Renquan Lv,Junchao Li,Ruodan Chen,Qingteng Chen,Zhibo Yang,Hu Wei,Lin Qu,Shuai Bai,Bing Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Science Edge Evaluation (SEE)，一个基于同行评审文献和实验实践的多模态基准，用于评估大语言模型在真实实验室科学中的推理能力。对19个多模态大语言模型的评估显示，最佳模型仅达到48.7%的准确率，使用工具后提升至52.7%；通用模型平均表现优于科学专用模型，且当前模型仍无法可靠地进行有证据约束的科学推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被用于科学发现，但其能否支持复杂的真实实验室科学尚不明确。现有评估缺乏针对实验证据推理的基准，因此需要构建一个基于专家设计和同行评审文献的基准来系统检验MLLMs在化学、生物学和材料科学领域的实验推理能力。

Method: 作者构建了SEE基准，包含来自化学、生物学和材料科学领域、基于同行评审文献和实验实践并由专家策划的多模态问题；对19个多模态大语言模型（含通用模型和科学专用模型）进行系统评估，并进行视觉智能体（visual-agent）评估以测试工具使用对模型推理能力的影响。

Result: 最佳模型仅达到48.7%的准确率；通用模型平均表现优于科学专用模型；使用工具可将最佳准确率提升至52.7%。尽管工具能扩展模型可获取的信息量，但更多信息并不等同于更可靠的科学推理，模型难以将工具提供的额外信息约束在原始实验证据范围之内。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大语言模型仍无法可靠地从实验结果中进行有依据且有证据边界约束的推理，这是真实科学发现中的关键能力。要弥合这一差距，模型需要从解释已确立的科学概念转向从实验数据中推导出新颖且基于证据的洞见。核心挑战在于模型能否将工具信息来源管理在原始实验证据的边界内。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly involved in scientific discovery, yet it remains unclear whether they can support complex real laboratory science. Here we introduce Science Edge Evaluation (SEE), a multimodal benchmark of expert-curated questions grounded in peer-reviewed literature and experimental practice in chemistry, biology, and materials science. Evaluation of 19 multimodal large language models (MLLMs) shows that even the best-performing model reaches only 48.7% accuracy. Moreover, general-purpose models outperform science-specialized models on average. In the visual-agent evaluation, the use of tools increases the best accuracy to 52.7%. Tool use can expand the information available to models, but more information does not necessarily lead to reliable scientific reasoning. The key challenge is whether models can manage tool-derived information within the boundaries of the original experimental evidence. Together, these findings reveal that current MLLMs still cannot reliably make justified and evidence-bounded inferences from experimental results, which is an essential capability in real scientific discovery. Bridging this gap requires MLLMs to transition from explaining established scientific concepts to deriving novel and evidence-based insights from experimental data.

</details>


### [168] [Blind to the Pivotal Vote: Aggregate Independence Metrics Miss Where Verification Actually Helps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06940)
*Yang Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出LLM评审组的错误高度相关（9个评审员仅相当于约2个独立评审的信息量），而引入不同证据源（如执行测试套件）在总体层面几乎不改变有效票数（变化-0.04，95%CI [-0.10,+0.02]）。但作者通过多数表决的算术分析发现，只有在"一票之差"的临界查询上替换票才会改变结果，且精度提升恰好全部集中在这些关键查询上（+10.4至+23.3个百分点），在其他查询上为零。通过多数侧替换规则，在HumanEval+/MBPP+上总体准确率从82.44%提升至85.62%，仅在16.2%的查询上调用信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评审组作为标准评估工具存在系统性误差相关性问题——多个评审员提供的有效信息量远低于其数量（9个约等于2个独立评审），而聚合只能缩小很小一部分差距。作者同时发现，从不同证据源（如执行测试套件）引入信号在总体层面没有产生可辨别的变化，因此需要厘清总体层面的依赖诊断与条件决策效用是不同的问题。

Method: 作者运用多数表决的初等算术分析进行理论刻画：对于单票替换策略，只有"一票之差"（one-vote margin）的决策才可能被改变。基于此，作者提出了"受影响集"（affected set）的表征，并设计了一种"多数侧替换规则"（majority-side replacement rule），仅在关键查询上调用外部信号。实验在三个代码基准和四种评审组规模上进行验证，包括9评审员的扩展和56个依赖子采样检查。

Result: 实证结果证实了理论预测：全部准确率提升恰好集中在临界（pivotal）查询上（三种主流配置下+10.4至+23.3个百分点），而其他查询上准确率增益精确为零。在HumanEval+/MBPP+上，多数侧替换规则将总体准确率从82.44%提升至85.62%，仅在16.2%的查询上调用信号；纯信号方案更强（87.60%）。不同面板规模下增益为+6.5至+16.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 总体层面的相关性诊断与按边距分层的效用评估是互补的而非矛盾的。作者提出的"受影响集"表征为任何指定的单票替换策略提供了一种调用缩减规则（call-reduction rule），使外部信号仅在能改变结果的少数关键查询上被激活，从而在保持成本效率的同时获得最大的精度收益。

Abstract: LLM judge panels are a standard evaluation tool, but prior work reports highly correlated panel errors: nine judges provide roughly the effective information of two independent ones, and aggregation closes only a small fraction of the gap. A natural remedy--a signal from a different evidence source, e.g., executing a test suite--produced no distinguishable change in the panel's effective-vote count at scale (-0.04, 95\% CI [-0.10, +0.02]). Aggregate dependence and conditional decision utility are different questions. Elementary majority arithmetic fixes the affected set for single-ballot substitution: only decisions with a one-vote margin can change. The empirical question is whether panel error rates rise and useful substitutions concentrate there. They do: the entire accuracy gain concentrates on these pivotal queries, where it is large (+10.4 to +23.3 percentage points across three headline configurations), and is exactly zero elsewhere. We confirm the pattern across three code benchmarks and four panel sizes (a 9-judge extension and 56 dependent subsampling checks, gain +6.5 to +16.1 percentage points). On HumanEval+/MBPP+, a majority-side replacement rule raises overall accuracy from 82.44\% to 85.62\% while invoking the signal on 16.2\% of queries; signal-only remains stronger at 87.60\%. Thus population-level dependence diagnostics and margin-stratified utility are complementary, and the affected-set characterization yields a call-reduction rule for any specified single-ballot substitution policy.

</details>


### [169] [LMM Modality Transfer: A Pre-requisite for Autonomous GIS Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06948)
*Ivan Majic,Zexian Huang,Franziska Hübl,Krzysztof Janowicz,Meilin Shi,Mina Karimi,Zilong Liu,Alexandra Fortacz-Lazan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模态转移任务来量化大型多模态模型（LMMs）在空间信息上的图像-文本双向转移能力：先让LMM描述一个彩色方块网格图像，再用另一个LMM实例根据文本描述重新生成原图像。实验表明（如OpenAI的）最新LMMs在简单的空间网格图像再生任务中仍难以完成模态转移，揭示出跨模态对齐是强健地理空间理解的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的空间能力研究（如空间推理）大多局限于纯文本模态，而人类进行GIS工作流时往往同时、交替、互补地使用文本与视觉两种模态。为了实现自动化的GIS分析管线或执行人工设计的GIS工作流，AI模型（尤其是LMMs）需要在图像和文本模态之间无缝切换，因此需要量化其空间信息的模态转移能力。

Method: 设计一个模态转移任务：首先让LMM描述输入的规则网格中彩色方块图像（图像到文本），然后将该文本描述输入给另一个新的LMM实例，要求其重新生成原始空间场景的图像（文本到图像）。通过这一两阶段流程，从空间信息论的角度量化LMMs在图像与文本模态之间转移空间信息的能力。

Result: 实验结果显示，来自OpenAI的最新LMMs仍然难以完成模态转移任务，即便任务场景仅为简单的彩色方块空间网格，在重新生成图像时仍表现困难。

Conclusion: 这项工作通过空间信息论的视角揭示了LMMs面临的一个关键瓶颈：要实现强大且稳健的地学空间理解，必须进行严谨的多模态对齐（multi-modal alignment）。单纯在某一模态上的空间能力不足以支撑完整的GIS自动化工作流。

Abstract: AI models are becoming increasingly adept at understanding and processing spatial information, thereby facilitating agentic problem-solving in spatial tasks and workflows. However, most of the research on their spatial capabilities (e.g., spatial reasoning) has focused on the textual modality as input and output. This contrasts with the human approach to GIS workflows, where text and visual modalities are often used together, interchangeably, and in a complementary manner. Thus, to truly achieve an automated GIS analysis pipeline or carry out human-designed GIS workflows, AI models --- Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) in particular --- need to be able to seamlessly transition between image- and text-based modalities that are traditionally used in such workflows. We present a modality transfer task that (1) asks an LMM to first describe an input image of colored squares in a regular grid, and (2) asks a new LMM instance to re-generate an image of the original spatial scene using the textual description output by the former model. This task quantifies the ability of LMMs to transfer spatial information between image and text modalities. Ultimately, by examining the modality transfer capability of LMMs through the lens of spatial information theory, this work highlights a critical bottleneck: achieving strong and robust geospatial understanding in LMMs requires rigorous, multi-modal alignment. Our results indicate that recent LMMs (here from OpenAI) still struggle with modality transfer, when tasked with re-generating an image of a simple spatial grid of color squares.

</details>


### [170] [Does Splitting a Triage Decision Across Agents Hide Bias or Help Catch It? A Multi-Agent Simulation Study of LLM-Based Resource Allocation Under Audit Capacity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06949)
*Paul-Peter Arslan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究对比了单一大语言模型与角色区分多智能体流水线（评估-分配-独立审计）在临终资源分配决策中的偏差情况。结果显示，多智能体流水线并未显著减少偏差发生频率（6.9% vs 6.1%, p=0.498），但审计容量显著影响偏差是否被发现：过载时43.8%的偏差完全未被捕获。偏差漏检主要由覆盖率的崩塌（100%降至65.6%）而非审计判断力退化驱动。通过按风险排序审计队列可在相同容量约束下恢复大部分覆盖率（65.6%升至91.7%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已证明单个LLM在做生死资源分配决策时存在可测量的人口统计学偏差。然而实际部署通常使用多智能体流水线并包含审查步骤，旨在捕获此类失败。作者想研究：当决策分布在角色区分的多智能体流水线中时，偏差是否真正减少，以及审计环节在何种条件下能够有效捕获偏差。

Method: 使用合成灾难分诊模拟器，配对在临床上除一个人口统计属性外完全相同的病例。在GPT-4o-mini上运行192个回合（2,304对已解决病例），比较单智能体控制条件与九智能体流水线，在三个独立变化的压力维度下进行实验。随后进行跟进实验，测试按估计风险重排审计队列（而非先到先服务）对覆盖率的影响。

Result: 两条件间偏差发生频率无显著差异（6.9% vs 6.1%, p=0.498）。审计容量对偏差是否被捕获有显著影响：30.0%的有偏差结果完全未被检测，审计者过载时升至43.8%，未过载时降至18.4%。该效应几乎完全由覆盖率驱动（过载时从100%塌陷至65.6%，p<0.001），而非被审计病例上的判断力退化（81.6% vs 85.7%, p=1.000，方向反转）。按风险排序审计队列可将覆盖率从65.6%恢复至91.7%（p=0.028）。

Conclusion: 在LLM代理流水线中增加独立监督并不必然减少偏差本身，但审计容量对偏差检测有效性至关重要；覆盖率崩塌（而非判断退化）是偏差漏检的主要机制，通过风险驱动的审计队列排序可在资源受限条件下显著恢复检测覆盖率。作者坦诚报告局限：单一模型、样本量有限、无对抗性复制。

Abstract: Prior benchmarking work has shown that a single large language model (LLM), forced to make life-or-death resource-allocation decisions, exhibits measurable demographic bias. Real deployments, however, rarely use a single agent: they use pipelines, with review steps meant to catch exactly this kind of failure. We study what happens to bias when the same decision is distributed across a role-differentiated multi-agent pipeline (assessment, allocation, independent audit) instead of made and checked by one model alone. Using a synthetic disaster-triage simulator with paired cases that are clinically identical except for one demographic attribute, we run 192 episodes (2,304 resolved case pairs) on GPT-4o-mini comparing a single-agent control condition to a nine-agent pipeline under three independently varied pressure dimensions. We find no measurable difference in how often biased outcomes occur between the two conditions (6.9% vs. 6.1%, p = 0.498). We do find a large and significant effect of audit capacity on whether bias is caught: 30.0% of biased outcomes go entirely undetected, rising to 43.8% when the auditor is overloaded and falling to 18.4% when it is not. Decomposing this effect shows it is driven almost entirely by coverage (whether a case is reviewed at all, which collapses from 100.0% to 65.6% under load, p < 0.001) rather than by degraded judgment on the cases that are reviewed (81.6% vs. 85.7%, p = 1.000, direction reversed). A follow-up experiment shows that reordering the audit queue by estimated risk, rather than first-come-first-served, recovers most of the lost coverage under the same capacity constraint (65.6% to 91.7%, p = 0.028). We discuss the implications for any system that adds independent oversight to an LLM agent pipeline under resource constraints, and report the study's limitations honestly: one model, modest sample sizes, and no adversarial replication.

</details>


### [171] [Critical Acclaim Orientation in Large Language Models: Evidence from Film Preference Elicitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06955)
*Jonghyun Jee,Aaron Shaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨大型语言模型（LLMs）是否系统地重现电影评价层级，通过对4个模型家族（Anthropic、OpenAI、Alibaba、Mistral）的8个模型、200部电影基准及每模型20,000次成对强制比较，发现所有模型均表现出"评论界好评导向"：受评论界认可但商业冷门的电影优于商业成功但未获评论认可的电影，且该模式随模型规模增大而增强。协变量回归显示评价导向、公共可见度与大众接受度可分别解释偏好差异，调整可见度与接受度后偏好反转或减弱，提示该偏差可能在真实部署中间接显现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs训练语料包含人类对电影、书籍等的主观评价，但模型是否系统性地重现评价层级尚不清楚。先前关于LLM文化偏见的研究提供了竞争性预期：模型可能复制互联网文本的流行度信号，也可能复制评论话语中的声望形式。本研究旨在通过电影评价实证检验这两种假设。

Method: 采用200部电影基准，分为批判性好评、商业成功和双重合法性（评论+商业）三类；对8个模型（4个家族各2个规模）各进行20,000次成对强制选择比较，用Bradley-Terry模型估计偏好；使用嵌套OLS回归分析评价导向、公共可见度、大众接受度对偏好的独立贡献；并比较评价式与推荐式提示框架下排名差异。

Result: 所有模型一致呈现评论界好评导向；该模式随模型规模增大而增强；嵌套OLS回归显示三个因素均显著解释偏好；调整公共可见度后，模型对双重合法性电影的偏好相对于仅评论认同电影发生反转；额外控制大众接受度后，仅商业成功电影的劣势大幅减弱；评价式与推荐式提示框架产生不同排名。

Conclusion: LLMs并非单纯复制流行度信号，而是系统性地倾向于重现评论话语中的文化声望层级，这反映了训练语料中评价性文本的影响力。该偏好随规模增大而增强，且受公共可见度等外部因素调节，在真实部署中可能以间接方式影响用户的电影获取与判断。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are trained on corpora that contain expressions of human judgment about films, books, music, and more. Yet whether LLMs systematically reproduce evaluative hierarchies remains unclear. Prior research on cultural bias in LLMs suggests competing expectations: models may mirror the popularity signals of internet texts, or may reproduce forms of prestige embedded in critical discourse. We probe this question through a study of film evaluations with eight models from four families (Anthropic, OpenAI, Alibaba, and Mistral), using a 200-film benchmark partitioned into critically acclaimed, commercially successful, and dual-legitimacy (critical acclaim + commercial success) films. Across 20,000 pairwise forced-choice comparisons per model analyzed with Bradley--Terry estimation, we observe a consistent critical acclaim orientation with all models: critically acclaimed yet commercially obscure films are selected over commercially successful yet critically unrecognized ones. This pattern grows with model scale within each family. In addition, nested OLS regression analyses show that evaluative orientation, public visibility, and popular reception distinctly help explain preferences. Adjusting for public visibility reverses the models' preference for dual-legitimacy films over critical acclaim-only films, while additionally accounting for popular reception attenuates much of the disadvantage of films with commercial success only. Finally, evaluative and recommendation-oriented prompt framings produce divergent rankings, suggesting that critical acclaim orientation may manifest indirectly in real-world LLM deployments.

</details>


### [172] [CAi Copilot: Reducing Operational Workload in Molecular Design through Intent-Driven Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06961)
*Zhu Wang,Jiangyu Chen,Yingjun Shang,Yuhui Yao,Laiao Lu,Tianfan Fu,Na Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAi Copilot是一个面向专家的分子设计智能体，将研究意图转化为可执行、可追溯的工作流，涵盖生成、筛选、多准则评估等环节，在45项任务中以84.59的结果得分超越次优方案18.07分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期分子设计是迭代过程而非单一生成任务，现有的AI功能分散在各类专用工具中，专家仍需手动协调各步骤、判断中间结果并整合证据。核心挑战是将研究意图转化为基于科学工具的自适应、可追溯的执行流程。

Method: CAi Copilot采用三层架构：研究界面层（Research Interface Layer）将意图转化为可执行计划；智能体推理层（Agent Reasoning Layer）利用中间结果引导每次运行；执行基座（Execution Substrate）提供分子工具、度量指标、可复用工具和后端服务。

Result: 在45项任务中，CAi取得最强综合性能，结果分数为84.59，超过次优结果18.07分。额外基准测试了CAi在生成、筛选和多准则评估方面的协调能力，同时暴露了长视域执行方面的局限。

Conclusion: CAi将宽泛的分子设计意图转化为透明、可追溯的工作流，将中间决策与候选级证据相连接，从而支持专家驱动的迭代式分子设计流程。

Abstract: Early-stage molecular design is an iterative process, not just a task of generating molecules. Researchers turn broad goals into design strategies, refine candidates, assess many properties, and gather evidence before synthesis and tests. AI methods can generate molecules, optimize several goals, predict properties, dock compounds, and account for synthesis. Yet these functions are spread across specialized tools. Experts must still coordinate each step, judge interim results, and integrate evidence. The central challenge is thus to turn research intent into adaptive, traceable runs grounded in scientific tools. We cast this challenge as intent-to-evidence molecular design workflow execution and present CAi Copilot, an expert-oriented agent with three linked layers. The Research Interface Layer turns intent into an executable plan. The Agent Reasoning Layer uses interim results to guide each run. The Execution Substrate supplies molecular tools, metrics, reusable utilities, and backend services. Across 45 tasks, CAi achieves the strongest overall performance, with an outcome score of 84.59, exceeding the next-best result by 18.07 points. Additional benchmarks test how CAi coordinates generation, screening, and multi-criteria evaluation, while exposing limits in long-horizon execution. These results show that CAi turns broad molecular-design intent into transparent, traceable workflows that connect interim decisions to candidate-level evidence.

</details>


### [173] [Learning in Deep Networks under Dale's Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06963)
*Roy Abel,Shimon Ullman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种符合Dale约束的生物学合理神经网络架构，利用两个互补的非负通道（on-off表示）分别表征正负贡献，通过局部Hebbian学习规则在完全非负的神经活动和固定符号突触下，理论上精确恢复反向传播更新，并在Tiny ImageNet上取得显著优于普通网络的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生物学合理学习模型允许神经元或突触表示混合符号值（正负皆有），违背了皮层回路的基本特性——Dale约束（神经元要么兴奋性要么抑制性，突触不能改变符号）。这一差异是连接生物神经网络与人工反向传播学习之间的关键障碍。

Method: 提出on-off双通道神经架构：用两个互补且相互作用的非负通道分别表示正负贡献，受大脑中on-off表征的启发。该通道通过简单神经回路基序实现，在自底向上和自顶向下通路中重复部署。结合局部Hebbian学习规则，仅通过神经元之间的局部交互传播学习信号并更新权重。理论上证明该方案能精确恢复反向传播更新，尽管仅依赖非负误差信号。

Result: 理论结果表明学习方案在当前生物约束下能精确恢复反向传播更新；实证上，on-off架构在满足更强生物约束的同时学到高效表征，在Tiny ImageNet基准上相较于同类vanilla网络取得了显著增益。

Conclusion: 有效学习可以从生物学合理的机制中涌现，不需要混合符号信号。这项工作向更真实的神经计算模型迈出了一步，弥合了Dale约束与反向传播学习之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Biologically plausible learning models aim to explain how neural circuits can implement effective learning under the constraints of real neurons. Although significant progress has been made, a major remaining challenge is that existing models often allow neurons or synapses to represent mixed-sign values, both positive and negative, in violation of a basic aspect of cortical circuitry -- Dale's constraint: biological neurons are either excitatory or inhibitory, but not both, and synapses cannot change sign. In this work, we address this discrepancy by introducing a biologically motivated neural architecture in which both neural activations and learning signals are represented by non-negative activity, and synapses have fixed sign, while still supporting backpropagation-like learning. Our approach uses two complementary interacting non-negative channels to represent positive and negative contributions, inspired by evidence of on-off representations in the brain. These channels are implemented through a simple neural circuit motif, which is repeated throughout the network in both bottom-up and top-down pathways. Combined with a local Hebbian learning rule, the resulting model propagates learning signals and updates weights using only local interactions between neurons. We show theoretically that our learning scheme can exactly recover the backpropagation update despite relying solely on non-negative error signals. Empirically, beyond satisfying stronger biological constraints, the on-off architecture learns efficient representations, yielding substantial gains over comparable vanilla networks on the Tiny ImageNet benchmark. These results demonstrate that effective learning can emerge from biologically plausible mechanisms without requiring mixed-sign signals, providing a step toward more realistic models of neural computation.

</details>


### [174] [Finding Usable Weight Mechanisms with Tiled SVD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06969)
*Ash Manvi,Samreena Tajreen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种不使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）的机制可解释性方法：直接在网络的线性映射层（linear sites）上做按列瓦片化的SVD分解，提取"机制挂载点"（mechanism mounts），每个挂载点为三元组(v,u,σ)，分别表示触发向量、写入向量和强度。在Gemma-2-2B上对WikiText-2子样本进行全写能量（full-write energy lift）预注册评估，所有七类线性映射182/182全部通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的机制可解释性方法依赖代理字典（如稀疏自编码器）和最大激活文本标注特征，但概念身份存在于学习到的字典中而非网络权重本身。作者希望直接从网络权重的线性映射中提取机制，使特征身份内置于权重规则中，而非依赖外部代理字典。

Method: 提出按列瓦片化SVD（column-tiled SVD）方法，直接在网络的线性映射层（如mlp.down、attn.o等）上分解权重矩阵，得到机制挂载点三元组(v,u,σ)。评估采用预注册测试套件，评判标准为全写能量提升（full-write energy lift）而非局部的tile-local lift，在层后RMSNorm后施加引导（steer）。

Result: 在Gemma-2-2B模型和WikiText-2（16,384 token子样本）上，七类线性映射均被评分：残差写入映射（mlp.down、attn.o）获得A/B/C评级（每个通过52/52个site-layer）；其他映射（mlp.gate、attn.q、attn.k、有效mlp.up、attn.v）仅获A/B评级（每个26/26）。总计182/182全部通过（GO）。

Conclusion: 直接对线性映射做SVD分解即可提取语义上有效的机制挂载点，无需训练代理字典；该方法的特征身份由权重规则直接定义。作者同时释放了库代码、语料构建器、实验入口和单元测试，便于复现与扩展。

Abstract: The dominant approach to mechanistic interpretability trains proxy dictionaries such as sparse autoencoders and labels features from max-activating text. The best such atlases identify con- cepts, but that identity lives in the learned dictionary rather than in the network weights them- selves. We propose extracting mechanism mounts directly from linear sites by column-tiled SVD: each mount is a triple (v,u,σ) read as trigger, write, and strength. Identity is the weight rule. We evaluate mounts with a pre-registered suite judged on full-write energy lift rather than tile-local lift. On Gemma-2-2B with WikiText-2 (16,384-token subsample), all seven linear maps are scored: residual writes (mlp.down, attn.o) receive full A/B/C with steer after post-sublayer RMSNorm and pass 52/52 site-layers; other maps receive A/B only (mlp.gate/attn.q/attn.k/effective mlp.up/attn.v 26/26 each). Aggregate: 182/182 GO. We release library code, the corpus builder, the experiment entrypoint, and unit tests.

</details>


### [175] [FedLBW: A Loss-Based Weighting Strategy for Federated Learning on Non-IID Data in Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07007)
*Majid Kundroo,Tinku Singh,Taehong Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FedLBW（基于损失加权的联邦学习聚合方法），通过服务器端小型代理数据集计算各客户端的验证损失，并以验证损失的倒数作为聚合权重，取代传统基于数据集大小的加权方式，从而在非IID数据和客户端掉线频繁的无线网络环境中实现更高的准确率、更快的收敛速度和更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习算法（如FedAvg）仅依据数据集大小对客户端更新进行加权，这会在非IID数据、异常值和客户端掉线频发的无线网络环境中引入偏置，导致模型收敛效率低下且性能不稳定。

Method: 提出FedLBW聚合方法：在服务器端使用小型代理数据集计算每个客户端的验证损失，并将各客户端更新的权重设为该验证损失的倒数，使低损失（更可靠）的模型在聚合中占据更大影响力。

Result: 在FashionMNIST（CNN）、CIFAR-10（ResNet-18）和CIFAR-100（ResNet-34）等数据集上，FedLBW相比FedAvg、FedAvgM、FedProx、FedNova、FedLAW和FedDkw等基线算法获得更高准确率和更快收敛速度，在CIFAR-10极端非IID场景下准确率提升高达7.6%；同时在高客户端掉线概率下仍保持显著更优的准确率。

Conclusion: FedLBW是面向无线网络环境的有效且具有韧性的联邦学习解决方案，在模型准确率、收敛速度和对抗非IID数据及客户端掉线的鲁棒性方面均有显著改进。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative machine learning (ML) across distributed clients while preserving privacy. However, efficient model convergence in FL remains challenging, especially in wireless networks where non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) data and frequent client dropouts are common. Traditional FL algorithms, such as FedAvg, rely solely on dataset size to weight client updates. This introduces biases towards clients with larger datasets and makes the process sensitive to non-IID data, outliers, and client dropouts. To address these challenges, we propose Federated Learning with Loss-Based Weighting (FedLBW), a novel aggregation method that assigns each client's update a weight proportional to the inverse of its validation loss, computed using a small proxy dataset on the server, rather than its dataset size. This ensures that lower-loss models exert greater influence during aggregation, prioritizing the most reliable updates and boosting overall performance. Through extensive experiments across multiple datasets, including FashionMNIST (CNN), CIFAR-10 (ResNet-18), and CIFAR-100 (ResNet-34), we demonstrate that FedLBW achieves higher accuracy and faster convergence compared to baseline algorithms such as FedAvg, FedAvgM, FedProx, FedNova, FedLAW and FedDkw, with notable improvements of up to 7.6 % higher accuracy on CIFAR-10 in extreme non-IID cases. Moreover, FedLBW showcases exceptional resilience to increasing dropout probabilities, consistently maintaining significantly higher accuracy even in challenging conditions. These results establish FedLBW as an effective and resilient solution for FL in wireless network environments, offering marked improvements in model accuracy, convergence speed, and robustness to non-IID data and client dropouts.

</details>


### [176] [ReQuant: Fixed-Grid Discrete Refinement for Post-Training Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07019)
*Yongge Ma,Guoan Wang,Feiyu Wang,Yaoming Li,Qian Zhang,Zihan Yan,Yinjun Han,Tong Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReQuant 是一种免反向传播的固定网格精炼方法，作为 PTQ 后的即插即用阶段，在保持量化格式不变的前提下，通过迭代更新离散权重赋值以降低均方重构误差，显著提升各类预量化模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ方法通常在启发式规则或贪心优化下获得初始量化模型，量化完成后整数赋值被视为最终结果，缺乏进一步优化空间。作者希望引入一个互补优化阶段，在保持可执行量化格式的前提下，让量化权重仍可被改进。

Method: ReQuant 是一种免反向传播的固定网格精炼过程，与PTQ初始化器无关。它以现有量化模型为可行起点，在固定量化网格上迭代重新访问离散权重赋值，仅接受能严格降低均方重构误差且保持在原网格上的更新，从而将PTQ输出转化为可迭代优化的离散解。

Result: 在多种模型家族、位宽和下游任务上的实验表明，ReQuant 一致地改善了来自不同PTQ初始化器的量化模型，尤其在简单初始化器和较低位宽上增益显著。ReQuant 经过多轮扫描可将简单的最近舍入初始化精炼至接近或超越 GPTAQ 在相同量化格式下的性能。

Conclusion: ReQuant 被确立为一种实用的、可即插即用的互补阶段，能够进一步改进现有 PTQ 流水线。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is widely used to reduce the memory and computational cost of large language models. Existing PTQ methods typically obtain an initial quantized model through heuristic rules or greedy optimization, and once quantization is completed the resulting integer assignments are usually treated as final. This observation motivates a complementary optimization stage within PTQ that keeps quantized weights improvable after an executable quantized model has been produced, while preserving the quantized format. We introduce ReQuant, a backpropagation-free fixed-grid refinement procedure for this stage. Agnostic to the PTQ initializer, ReQuant takes an existing quantized model as a feasible starting point and iteratively revisits its discrete weight assignments on the fixed quantization grid. Accepted updates strictly reduce the mean squared reconstruction error and remain on the original grid. In this way, ReQuant turns the initially fixed PTQ output into an iteratively optimizable discrete solution and serves as a plug-and-play post-processing stage for existing PTQ pipelines. Experiments across diverse model families, bit-widths, and downstream tasks show that ReQuant consistently improves quantized models from heterogeneous PTQ initializers, with especially large gains on simple initializers and lower bit-widths. Notably, ReQuant can refine a simple round-to-nearest initialization across multiple sweeps until it approaches or surpasses GPTAQ under the same quantization format. These results establish ReQuant as a practical complementary stage for further improving existing PTQ pipelines.

</details>


### [177] [ZIPBrain: Can EEG Foundation Models Be Faster, Locally Deployable, but Accurate?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07033)
*Lingwei Li,Yirong Kan,Peng Chen,Xu Cao,Zheng Chen,Yasuhiko Nakashima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出ZIPBrain，一种基于冗余感知的EEG token池化模块，利用EEG低信噪比特性有效压缩token数量，在不损失精度的前提下显著加速EEG基础模型的推理，实现32.7%的推理时间减少（CUDA Graph下最高41.8%）及1.3%-10.5%的平均精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型（EFMs）虽然能提供强大的通用表征能力，但其计算开销随输入长度呈二次方增长，难以部署在资源受限场景（尤其是实时临床监测）。由于EEG信号具有低信噪比（low-SNR）特性，许多token是冗余且可压缩的，这为实现高效的token压缩提供了契机。

Method: ZIPBrain是一个冗余感知的EEG token池化模块，其核心流程为：给定token序列后，将token划分为冗余组和独特组两类；然后将每个冗余token与其在独特组中最相似的对应token进行合并。ZIPBrain是训练无关（training-free）的即插即用模块，可无缝集成到标准Transformer编码器中，且计算开销可忽略不计。

Result: 在多个EEG基础模型上的广泛实验表明，ZIPBrain具备较强的通用性：相比基线实现了1.3%-10.5%的平均精度提升，同时将端到端推理时间减少了32.7%（使用CUDA Graph时最高可达41.8%）。

Conclusion: ZIPBrain证明了利用EEG低信噪比特性进行token级冗余压缩是可行且有效的，能够在保持甚至提升精度的同时显著加速EEG基础模型的本地化实时推理，为资源受限的临床监控场景提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: This work investigates whether Electroencephalograph (EEG) foundation models (EFMs) can be made faster and locally deployable without sacrificing accuracy. EEG foundation models are a major trend, offering strong general-purpose representations. However, their computational burden grows quadratically with input length, hindering deployment on resource-constrained scenario, particularly for real-time clinical monitoring. EEG's low SNR further suggests many of these tokens are redundant and compressible with little accuracy cost. We propose ZIPBrain, a novel redundancy-aware EEG token pooling module that leverages this low-SNR characteristic to reduce token count. Given a token sequence, ZIPBrain partitions tokens into redundant and unique groups, then merges each redundant token with its most similar counterpart in the unique group. Furthermore, ZIPBrain serves as a training-free, plug-and-play module that seamlessly integrates into standard Transformer encoders with negligible computational overhead. Extensive experiments across multiple EEG foundation models show ZIPBrain's strong versatility, achieving 1.3%-10.5% average improvement over baselines, while reducing wall-clock inference time by 32.7% (up to 41.8% with CUDA Graph) compared to the original EEG foundation models.

</details>


### [178] [Not All Problems Are Best Modeled as MILP: A DSL-Centric Framework for Flexible and Accurate Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07040)
*Shaofeng Zhang,Hongyuan Su,Qingwen Peng,Zefang Zong,Shengcai Liu,Ke Tang,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OptiDSL框架，将组合优化问题（COP）的建模从传统的混合整数线性规划（MILP）范式转变为领域特定语言（DSL）表示。利用LLM将自然语言映射到标准化的领域结构上，将问题建模与求解执行解耦，从而支持多样化的专用求解器。在44种COP类型的综合基准上，OptiDSL相比MILP流程在建模准确率上提升51.66%，建模时间减少91.71%，并在现有基准上实现23.09%的建模准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的自动化优化建模框架过度依赖刚性的MILP范式，但并非所有问题都适合用MILP建模——将复杂的问题域强制转化为线性约束会带来高昂的建模复杂度，并严重限制求解器的灵活性。因此需要一种更灵活、更贴近问题本质的建模范式。

Method: OptiDSL框架核心思想是用DSL表示取代MILP建模。利用LLM将自然语言问题描述映射到标准化、领域公认的DSL结构上，实现问题公式化与求解执行的解耦；该范式可无缝集成多种专用求解器库，涵盖传统启发式方法和现代基于学习的求解方法。

Result: 在44种COP类型的综合基准上，OptiDSL相比MILP流程实现51.66%的建模准确率提升和91.71%的建模时间降低；在现有基准上，相较MILP流程获得23.09%更高的建模准确率。

Conclusion: OptiDSL证明了以领域特定语言而非刚性的MILP范式来表达组合优化问题是更有效的建模路径。通过解耦建模与求解，该框架既降低了建模复杂度与时间成本，又显著提升了建模准确性，为自动化优化建模提供了新的范式方向。

Abstract: Solving combinatorial optimization problems (COPs) requires not only efficient algorithms but also carefully crafted formulations. While recent works have leveraged LLMs to automate optimization modeling, current frameworks predominantly rely on a rigid mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) paradigm. In this paper, we argue that not all problems are best modeled as MILP, as forcing complex domains into linear constraints can induce prohibitive modeling complexity and severely restrict solver flexibility. To address this, we propose OptiDSL, a framework that shifts the focus from rigid MILP formulations to domain-specific language (DSL) representations. By utilizing LLMs to map natural language onto standardized, domain-accepted structures, OptiDSL decouples problem formulation from execution. This paradigm enables seamless integration with a diverse library of specialized solvers, ranging from traditional heuristics to modern learning-based methods. Experimental results on the comprehensive benchmark of 44 COP types show that OptiDSL significantly surpasses MILP-based pipelines, yielding a 51.66% gain in formulation accuracy and a 91.71% decrease in modeling time. Notably, it also outperforms MILP-based pipelines on the existing benchmark, achieving a 23.09% higher formulation accuracy. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/OptiDSL.

</details>


### [179] [Unsupervised Adaptation of PDE Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07053)
*Ziye Song,Zhao Wei,Xin Yu,Ivor Tsang,Yueming Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Pretrained partial differential equation (PDE) foundation models can generalize across different equations, but adapting them to unseen PDE systems typically requires dense solution data, which is often expensive or unavailable. To address this limitation, we propose an unsupervised PDE-based finetuning framework that eliminates the need for ground-truth solutions. We first pretrain a neighborhood attention Transformer on diverse time-dependent PDEs spanning varying spatial scales, yielding transferable representations across heterogeneous equations. In the adaptation stage, we construct a physics-based objective using the PDE residual and boundary conditions, and finetune the model on unseen equations via low-rank adaptation (LoRA). To address the uneven learning across physical quantities in standard LoRA, we introduce NSLoRA, a Newton-Schulz orthogonalized variant that rebalances adaptation. Our method achieves performance comparable to supervised LoRA finetuning without requiring any ground-truth solutions, while consistently outperforming competitive neural operator baselines and recent PDE foundation models across heterogeneous PDE benchmarks spanning multiple spatial dimensions.

</details>


### [180] [BONSAI: Evolvability-Guided Tree Search over Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07056)
*Yash Priya Shastri,Anand Eswaran,Adnan Qidwai,Pankaj Thorat,Sachin Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BONSAI是一个新的技能(skill)优化框架，它将技能视为引导冻结代理的自然语言文档，并通过"可进化性"(evolvability)而非单纯的当前适应度来优化文本。该方法将技能文档空间建模为蒙特卡洛搜索树，用上置信界(UCB)规则平衡节点自身适应度与突变邻域的适应度，从而专注于那些能在持续突变下仍产生有价值变体的文档空间区域。在30B冻结代理和三个基准上，BONSAI将保留准确率提升23.13个百分点，分别比GEPA和SkillOpt高出3.87和3.97个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的技能优化方法以"保留任何能提高验证集分数的编辑"为准则，但单一分数无法区分"窄过拟合尖峰"与"宽平台"——只有后者才能被继续改进。作者希望借鉴生物学中"可进化性"与"当前适应度"相分离的概念，寻找能持续产生高价值变体的文档区域，而非仅在分数上局部上升。

Method: BONSAI将技能文档空间构建为蒙特卡洛搜索树(MCTS)：每个子文档是父文档的一次突变；搜索采用上置信界(UCB)选择规则，其利用项(exploitation)混合了节点自身的适应度和其突变邻域的适应度。由于每个子节点都是突变产生的，节点下记录的平均分数无需额外成本即可估计该邻域的可进化性，从而将预算集中在持续改进的区域，同时探索项保持弱势分支的竞争力。最终输出搜索中找到的最佳评分文档，其成本等同于传统的accept-if-better循环。

Result: 使用冻结的30B代理，在三个基准上平均：BONSAI比无技能代理的保留准确率提升23.13个百分点；比两个预算匹配的基线GEPA提升3.87个百分点，比SkillOpt提升3.97个百分点。

Conclusion: BONSAI证明了通过"可进化性"而非单一当前分数指导技能优化是可行且有效的，能够以与传统简单基线相同的代价，更充分地开发文档空间中的高潜力区域，并为技能优化提供了一种生物学启发的替代范式。

Abstract: A skill is a naturallanguage document that steers a frozen agent whose weights cannot be updated so any capability the agent lacks must be supplied in prose Optimising a skill is therefore optimising text against a score and the standard recipe which keeps any edit that raises a heldout score is blind in a specific way a single score cannot tell a document perched on a narrow overfit spike from one resting on a broad plateau even though only the second can still be improved We introduce BONSAI a novel skilloptimisation framework that steers instead by evolvability the capacity of a region of documentspace to keep producing viable variation under further mutation a property biology treats as separate from present fitness BONSAI grows skills as a MonteCarlo search tree in which every child document is a mutation of its parent and descends it under an upperconfidence selection rule whose exploitation term blends a skills own fitness with the fitness of its mutational neighbourhood Because every child is a mutation the mean score recorded beneath a node estimates that neighbourhoods evolvability at no extra cost so the rule concentrates budget on regions that keep improving while its exploration term keeps a currently weak branch in contention BONSAI ships the single bestscoring document it finds at no cost beyond the acceptifbetter loop it replaces With a frozen 30B agent and averaged over three benchmarks BONSAI lifts heldout accuracy over the skillfree agent by 2313 points and improves on two budgetmatched baselines GEPA and SkillOpt by 387 and 397 points respectively

</details>


### [181] [PTQ4SNN: Membrane-Aware Post-Training Quantization for Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07066)
*Hui Xie,Tong Shi,Haotong Qin,Aishan Liu,Xiaode Liu,Jinyang Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PTQ4SNN是一个膜感知的后训练量化框架，可联合量化脉冲神经网络的权重和循环膜状态，在仅需少量校准集的情况下，通过通道级统一尺度桥和混合精度位分配，在W4量化和约4位膜精度下保持模型精度，适用于卷积SNN和脉冲驱动Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN能够实现稀疏和事件驱动计算，但其低比特部署尚不完整——即使权重已量化，循环膜状态仍常以浮点存留。膜状态量化面临两大挑战：其分布在不同通道间及与前序权重间存在差异；且靠近发放阈值的小扰动可能改变脉冲决策并随时间累积。现有方法难以应对这些挑战，因此需要一种联合量化权重与膜状态的框架。

Method: PTQ4SNN包含两个核心组件：(1) 通道级统一尺度桥（Unified Scale Bridge），将膜状态尺度约束为s_mem,c = s_w,c * 2^k_c，自适应膜分布的同时支持移位兼容的尺度转换；(2) 混合精度位分配（Mixed-Precision Bit Allocation），根据发放活动和量化敏感性，在平均位预算下为膜通道分配2/4/8比特精度。框架基于可复用的投影-LIF对运行，无需骨干网络重训练，同时支持卷积SNN和脉冲驱动Transformer。

Result: 在静态和事件驱动的分类任务以及语义分割任务上的实验表明，PTQ4SNN在W4量化和约4位膜精度条件下能有效保持模型精度。

Conclusion: PTQ4SNN证明了膜状态可以在后训练阶段实现联合低比特量化而不显著损失精度，推动了SNN全链路低比特部署的可行性，且框架具有跨越不同SNN骨干架构的泛化能力。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) enable sparse and event-driven computation, but their low-bit deployment remains incomplete because recurrent membrane states are commonly retained in floating point even after weight quantization. Quantizing these states is challenging because their distributions differ across channels and from the preceding weights, while small perturbations near the firing threshold may alter spike decisions and accumulate over time. We propose PTQ4SNN, a membrane-aware post-training quantization framework that jointly quantizes weights and recurrent membrane states using only a small calibration set. First, a channel-wise Unified Scale Bridge constrains the membrane scale as s_mem,c = s_w,c * 2^k_c, adapting to membrane distributions while enabling shift-compatible scale conversion. Second, Mixed-Precision Bit Allocation assigns 2/4/8-bit precision to membrane channels according to firing activity and quantization sensitivity under an average-bit budget. The framework operates on reusable projection-LIF pairs and supports both convolutional SNNs and spike-driven Transformers without backbone retraining. Experiments on static and event-based classification and semantic segmentation show that PTQ4SNN effectively preserves model accuracy under W4 quantization and approximately 4-bit membrane precision.

</details>


### [182] [DocMemo: Dynamic Evidence Discovery via Probabilistic Memory-Guided Retrieval for Multi-Modal Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07067)
*Hanshu Yao,Janfeng Zhong,Niu Lian,Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DocMemo是一个基于记忆引导框架的长文档推理方法，通过三级检索状态（文档模式记忆、页面信念记忆、问题情节记忆）实现动态证据探索，利用贝叶斯页面信念更新和Thompson采样等机制克服传统静态检索和脆弱跨轮记忆的局限，在3个基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长文档理解系统存在两大局限：单轮方法在一开始就固定top-k页面集合，难以从早期检索错误中恢复；迭代方法虽支持多轮证据获取，但未研究跨轮状态的传播机制，难以跟踪页面相关性的动态变化。

Method: DocMemo维护三级检索状态：文档模式记忆（结构先验）、页面信念记忆（动态相关性估计）、问题情节记忆（查询特定推理轨迹）。推理过程中通过贝叶斯页面信念更新结合Thompson采样进行连续性页面选择优化，利用空间邻近性传播和结构感知的自适应粒度证据访问，并以细粒度视觉区域补充页面级证据。

Result: 在3个基准数据集上取得最先进性能，实验结果验证了结构化记忆和动态页面信念更新的有效性。

Conclusion: DocMemo通过将长文档推理建模为动态证据探索，并以结构化记忆跟踪跨轮状态，有效解决了静态检索的固有缺陷和跨轮信息传播不透明的问题，展示了动态检索策略与记忆机制相结合的优越性。

Abstract: Long-document understanding requires locating sparse and heterogeneous evidence across hundreds of pages, yet existing systems remain limited by static retrieval and fragile cross-round memory. Mainstream single-round methods commit to a fixed top-$k$ page set at the outset and struggle to recover from early retrieval errors; recent iterative approaches allow multi-round evidence acquisition, but they do not investigate the propagation mechanism of cross-round states, making it difficult to track the dynamic changes in page relevance. To address these limitations, we propose DocMemo, a memory-guided framework that formulates long-document reasoning as dynamic evidence exploration. DocMemo maintains a tri-level retrieval state consisting of Document Schema Memory, Page Belief Memory, and Question Episodic Memory, which respectively capture structural priors, dynamic relevance estimation, and query-specific reasoning trajectories. During reasoning, DocMemo continuously refines cross-round page selection through Bayesian page belief updating with Thompson sampling, spatial proximity propagation, and structure-aware adaptive-granularity evidence access, while supplementing page-level evidence with fine-grained visual regions. Experiments on 3 benchmarks show that DocMemo achieves state-of-the-art performance and validate the efficacy of structured memory and dynamic page belief updating. Code is available at https://github.com/Harrygof/DocMemo.

</details>


### [183] [MemOPD: On-Policy Distillation through Memory State Alignment for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07068)
*Zhiyuan Liu,Tinghong Ye,Chenghao Liu,Yizhuo Li,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemOPD是一种面向长时程智能体的记忆对齐在线策略蒸馏方法，通过记录并重建每次模型调用的输入输出，恢复原始token位置和因果可见性，确保教师在评分学生动作时处于与学生rollout相同的状态，从而提供密集的教师监督信号，解决传统PPO稀疏奖励和持久化历史评分导致的状态不对齐问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体在交互过程中累积不断增长的上下文，导致性能下降和稳定性受损。虽然紧凑记忆通过压缩和重写历史来缓解该问题，但传统的PPO仅依赖最终任务奖励进行学习，稀疏奖励无法为单个记忆更新提供足够指导。在线策略蒸馏(OPD)可以提供密集教师监督，但记忆压缩过程中的上下文重写可能破坏教师评分与学生行动状态之间的对齐——当采样响应被保留并重新编码用于后续调用时，展平历史可能导致教师在学生从未访问过的状态下评分动作，使动作按来源是on-policy但按状态不是。

Method: MemOPD记录每次模型调用的输入和采样输出，恢复其原始token位置和因果可见性，并将重建的调用打包以进行高效的教师评分。教师在采样动作位置提供全词表监督，同时PPO保持最终任务目标。该方法通过状态对齐确保蒸馏监督的有效性，并通过打包机制提升训练效率。

Result: 实验验证了多次上下文更新中的状态对齐，结果显示相比持久化历史教师评分的匹配对照，F1提升7.0%。MemOPD-3B相比PPO在F1上最高提升416.2%，打包机制在训练期间实现高达1.63倍的actor计算加速。

Conclusion: MemOPD通过记忆对齐的在线策略蒸馏有效解决了长时程智能体中教师监督状态不对齐的问题，在性能上大幅超越PPO基线，同时保持了训练效率，验证了保持状态对齐对于蒸馏监督有效性的关键作用。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents accumulate growing contexts during interaction, impairing performance and stability. Compact memory mitigates this problem by compressing and rewriting the history retained between model invocations. Learning what to retain typically relies on proximal policy optimization (PPO) with final task rewards, but sparse rewards provide little guidance for individual memory updates. This limitation motivates on-policy distillation (OPD), which supplies dense teacher supervision on student rollouts. For such supervision to be valid, the teacher must evaluate each sampled action under the same state in which it was generated. However, the context rewriting performed during memory compression can break this alignment. When sampled responses are retained and re-encoded for later invocations, flattening the interaction into a persistent history may cause the teacher to score the action under a state that the student never visited during rollout. The action therefore remains on-policy by provenance, but not necessarily by state. We therefore propose Memory-Aligned On-Policy Distillation (MemOPD). MemOPD records the inputs and sampled outputs of each model invocation, restores its original token positions and causal visibility, and packs the reconstructed invocations for efficient teacher scoring. The teacher provides full-vocabulary supervision at the sampled action positions, while PPO preserves the final task objective. Experiments verify state alignment across several context updates and show that it improves F1 by 7.0% over persistent-history teacher scoring in a matched control. Overall, MemOPD-3B improves F1 over PPO by up to 416.2%, while packing yields up to a 1.63x speedup in actor computation during training. The code for this work is publicly available at: https://github.com/TPssp/MemOPD.

</details>


### [184] [Transformers Struggle to Use Their Emergent World Models: Revisiting the Tower of Hanoi, and the Illusion of Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07077)
*Devin Pereira,Willem Zuidema*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文深入研究了小型Transformer和大型推理模型（LRM）在汉诺塔平塔变体任务上的表现。小模型通过可解释性技术被发现发展出一种涌现的世界模型——可线性解码、几何保真的状态空间（谢尔宾斯基三角形）表征。而大型推理模型（Qwen3.6-27B和DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-32B）在提示结束时几乎完美地编码了谢尔宾斯基世界模型，却在3环以上任务中大量失败，其根源在于规划过程中世界模型表征的衰退。通过注入提示时刻的表征可以部分恢复性能，表明失败的本质是表征维持能力的缺失而非表征本身的缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型推理模型虽能解决标准汉诺塔问题，但在平塔变体上仍表现挣扎。已有工作仅报告了性能崩溃现象，但缺乏对失败机制的深入理解。作者希望从机制层面揭示LRM在解决此类规划任务时内部究竟发生了什么，从而解释为什么模型能够建立世界模型却仍然失败。

Method: 作者采用双轨研究策略：（1）从零训练小型Transformer于预计算的解轨迹上，运用多种可解释性技术（线性探测、因果干预等）分析其内部表征，证明其发展了涌现的世界模型（谢尔宾斯基三角形）；(2)将相同技术应用于两个前沿推理模型Qwen3.6-27B和DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-32B，在思维链的不同阶段探测世界模型表征，并通过在推理时注入提示时刻的表征来建立因果性。

Result: 小型Transformer展现出几何保真的谢尔宾斯基三角形世界模型，且该模型因果参与了解题过程。大型推理模型在提示结束时近乎完美地编码了谢尔宾斯基世界模型，但在超过3个环的多数任务中失败。失败根源定位于规划过程中表征的持续衰减。通过注入提示时刻的表征，性能获得改善，证明因果关系成立。

Conclusion: 大型推理模型在规划任务中失败的本质不是无法构建世界模型，而是无法在长程推理过程中维持该表征。这重新框定了先前文献中报告的性能崩溃现象——模型构建了世界模型，然后在推理过程中丧失它。性能通过表征注入至少可以部分恢复，为改进LRM的规划能力提供了新思路。

Abstract: The Tower of Hanoi is a simple planning puzzle that in prior work has proven challenging for large reasoning models (LRMs). Current models solve the standard formulation of the puzzle, but still struggle with the flat-to-flat variant (where initial and goal states are not restricted to have all rings on a single peg). This paper presents an in-depth study of how both small, in-house Transformers and large, third-party LRMs solve this task. To understand the failures mechanistically, we first train small Transformers from scratch on precomputed solution traces. Using a variety of interpretability techniques, we show that these Transformers develop an emergent world model: a linearly decodable, geometrically faithful representation of the puzzle's state space (the Sierpinski triangle), that is causally involved in solving the puzzles. Second, we return to the large LLMs and apply our techniques to two frontier reasoning models, Qwen3.6-27B and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-32B, that attempt to solve the task through extended chain-of-thought. Surprisingly, we find that both models encode the Sierpinski world model near-perfectly at the end of the prompt, and yet fail at the majority of tasks when there are more than 3 rings. We locate the source of this failure in the decaying representation of the world model. We probe for the representation at different stages during planning, and establish causality by showing that performance can be improved by injecting the prompt-time representation at inference. The failure of the models is thus one of maintenance of the required representations, not their absence, and performance is at least partially recoverable. These results thus reframe the reported collapse in performance from prior work: current Large Reasoning Models build a world model, and then lose it.

</details>


### [185] [MemWM: Memory-Augmented Text-Based World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07107)
*Yujun Wang,Tao Zhang,Jinhe Bi,Aniri,Wenxuan Ye,Boliang Liu,Sikuan Yan,Shuning Wang,Xuebing Zhou,Sören Pirk,Hinrich Schütze,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MemWM，一种记忆增强的文本世界模型，通过世界记忆（过渡规则、状态缓存和难预测事实）来调节下一状态预测，并结合策略侧的世界技能（任务级技能和逐步纠错指导）提升下游规划性能。在ALFWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld上，相对于SFT世界模型代理，记忆增强代理最多获得65.4%的相对成功率提升，事实状态保持度SSF最高提升206.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型虽能流畅预测下一状态，但常遗漏任务关键事实、破坏产品属性或应用错误的过渡规则，即存在系统性的预测错误。此类错误会严重影响基于世界模型的智能体规划能力，因此需要一种能够缓解这些系统错误的方法。

Method: MemWM包含两个核心组件：(1) 世界记忆：一个精心构建的记忆库，包含过渡规则、状态缓存和难以预测的事实，用于条件化下一状态想象（next-state imagination）；(2) 策略侧世界技能：保持策略模型冻结，检索任务级技能和逐步纠错指导来辅助动作选择。同时提出Structured State Fidelity (SSF)评估指标，通过基准特定的事实和字段对预测状态进行评分。

Result: 记忆增强训练相比SFT在SSF上最多提升206.3%；在完整规划设置下，记忆增强代理在ALFWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld上相比SFT训练的世界模型代理，下游成功率最高获得65.4%的相对提升。敏感性分析进一步表明检索记忆在不同记忆和动作预算设置下均能提升任务成功率和效率。

Conclusion: 记忆增强的世界模型能够有效缓解系统性的状态预测错误，显著提升事实状态保真度和下游任务成功率；将世界记忆与策略侧世界技能结合，即使策略模型保持冻结也能带来显著的性能增益，验证了外部记忆作为世界模型和策略增强手段的有效性。

Abstract: World models are increasingly used to support planning in agents by predicting how environment states evolve in response to agent actions. Yet fluent next-state predictions can still omit task-critical facts, corrupt product attributes, or apply incorrect transition rules. To address such systematic prediction errors, we introduce MemWM, a memory-augmented text-based world model. MemWM uses world memory, a curated memory bank of transition rules, state caches, and hard-to-predict facts, to condition next-state imagination. We evaluate factual state preservation with Structured State Fidelity (SSF), which scores predicted states through benchmark-specific facts and fields. Compared with SFT, memory-augmented training improves SSF by up to 206.3%. In the full planning setting, we keep the policy model frozen and provide policy-side world skill: retrieved task-level skills and step-wise corrective guidance for action selection. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld, memory-augmented agents improve downstream success over an SFT-trained world-model agent, with up to a 65.4% relative gain. Sensitivity analyses further show that retrieved memory improves task success and efficiency under different memory and action-budget settings.

</details>


### [186] [How Much, Then Where: Credit-Conserving Action-to-Token Allocation for Multi-Turn Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07118)
*Lichao Ma,Yang Sun,Shuaitao Zhao,Yangyi Fang,Cong Qin,Xiaoliang Fu,Yuhang Tian,Yuchen Wei,Junbo Zhu,Yang Wei,Lu Pan,Jiaye Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FACTOR提出了一种多轮智能体强化学习中分离轨迹级信用分配与token级信用分配的新方法，使用检查点校准的TD残差分配动作信用、反馈条件化的师生似然差距分配token信用，并在多个环境上一致优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮智能体强化学习中的信用分配存在两个层次：将轨迹级信用分配给每个动作，以及将每个动作的信用进一步分配到其各token上。此前方法未将这两个决策明确分离，导致信用分配不精确，影响学习效率。

Method: FACTOR将信用分配分解为两个独立步骤：(1) 使用检查点校准的时间差分(TD)残差为每个动作分配信用，使其望远镜式求和等于轨迹优势；(2) 通过反馈条件化的师生似然差距将动作信用分配到各实现token。采用动作级归一化保持动作平均系数、防止token级符号翻转，并使用动作均值归约消除动作标量替代权重对token长度的隐式依赖。在裁剪前，每个动作的内部动作均值替代等于其TD信用。

Result: FACTOR在ALFWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld上一致优于竞争基线，所有环境-种子组合均倾向FACTOR，且在最长horizon环境中取得最大增益。超参数无需重新调优即可迁移到更大的骨干网络和不同模型家族。消融实验表明TD动作信用是改进的主要驱动因素，事后token分配提供互补增益。

Conclusion: FACTOR通过显式分离轨迹级与token级信用分配，提供了一种更精确、更稳定的多轮智能体强化学习信用分配机制，具有良好的一致性、可迁移性和可解释性，为长horizon多轮交互任务的RL训练提供了实质改进。

Abstract: Credit assignment in multi-turn agent reinforcement learning operates at two levels: assigning trajectory-level credit to actions and distributing each action's credit across its tokens. In this paper, we introduce FACTOR, which separates these decisions. FACTOR uses checkpoint-calibrated TD residuals to assign per-action credits that telescope to the trajectory advantage, and feedback-conditioned teacher-student likelihood gaps to allocate each credit across the realized action tokens. Per-action normalization preserves the action-average coefficient and prevents token-level sign flips. We pair this construction with an action-mean reduction, removing the implicit dependence of an action's scalar surrogate weight on its token length. At the behavior policy and before clipping, each action's inner action-mean surrogate equals its TD credit. FACTOR consistently improves over competitive baselines across ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld, with every environment-seed comparison favoring FACTOR and the largest gains emerging on the longest-horizon environment. The same hyperparameters transfer without retuning to a larger backbone and to a different model family. Ablations identify TD action credit as the dominant driver of the improvement, with hindsight token allocation contributing complementary gains.

</details>


### [187] [DiDPO: Diff-in-Diff Policy Optimization for Coding Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07147)
*Xucong Wang,Zhe Zhao,Liheng Yu,Di Wu,Xiaofeng Cao,Pengkun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DiDPO（Diff-in-Diff Policy Optimization），一种无评论家的RL方法，通过从代码diff结构中构建细粒度信用单元（anchor），将diff级优势回传到单个token，以解决编码智能体RLVR中的细粒度信用分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体中每个步骤的编码操作同时改变代码版本的多个区域，导致独立变更的贡献无法区分。现有的RLVR方法主要使用结果奖励或步骤级奖励，无法深入代码diff内部，使编码任务的独特属性对训练不可见。

Method: DiDPO将多轮编码交互组织为多个思考-动作步骤，在采样轨迹中挖掘代码diff。通过"groupability score"将每个完整diff拆分为高度相似的子diff，并据此选择anchors，形成advantage groups，最后将diff级优势投影回单个响应token，实现细粒度信用分配。

Result: 在长时程编码和推理基准上，DiDPO显著超越强agentic RL基线。在Qwen2.5-7B-Coder上超过可比方法10%以上，缩小了与更大模型的差距。同时开源了verl-code代码库。

Conclusion: DiDPO为编码智能体训练提供了原则性的细粒度信用分配框架，证明从diff结构构造信用单元比现有的结果奖励或步骤级奖励更有效。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with Verifiable Reward (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for training coding agents, where the execution feedback from compilation and tests provides objective verification. However, unlike agent tasks, coding agents face a unique and finer-grained credit assignment challenge: at each step, coding actions simultaneously pack varying changes into different regions of a code version, which makes the contribution of independent change indistinguishable. Existing RLVR methods mostly leverage the outcome reward or step-level reward, which fails to dive into a code diff and makes unique properties of coding actions invisible to training. In this paper, we propose Diff-in-Diff Policy Optimization (DiDPO), a critic-free RL method that constructs fine-grained credit units directly from the structure of code diffs. DiDPO organizes multi-turn coding interactions into multiple thought--action steps and discovers code diffs across sampled trajectories. It then selects anchors by aggregating highly similar sub-diffs split from each whole diff by our ``groupability score'', which provides the splitting schema that optimally balances the semantic scope of anchors and the group mass they may form. Finally these anchors form advantage groups and project the diff-level advantage back to individual response tokens. Experiments on long-horizon coding and reasoning benchmarks show that DiDPO significantly outperforms strong agentic RL baselines. On Qwen2.5-7B-Coder, DiDPO exceeds comparable methods by over 10\% and narrows the gap with far larger models, offering a principled framework for fine-grained credit assignment in coding agent training. We also open-source verl-code, an agentic rl codebase that supports various RL methods and coding benchmarks.

</details>


### [188] [A MARL Centered Reference Architecture for Large Language Model Augmentation in Smart Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07148)
*Fouad Bahrpeyma,Dirk Reichelt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以多智能体强化学习（MARL）为中心，探讨大语言模型（LLM）在制造业自适应控制中的增强与替代角色，提出一个三层参考架构与分类法，并基于证据评估各LLM附著点的能力与部署就绪度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代制造业对自适应控制提出了六重耦合需求（局部决策全局影响、部分可观测、非平稳、快速反射与长时程效应、延迟弥散结果、难以显式建模的动态）。合作式MARL（Dec-POMDP框架下的集中训练分散执行）是契合这些需求的自然形式化工具，但需要厘清LLM在其中的定位——是在哪些环节增强、接口、训练，还是在最激烈的竞争情形下取代协调核心。

Method: 提出一种分类法，通过四个LLM附着点（策略、奖励设计、智能体间通信、分层规划）组织文献；建立"条件能力画像"（条件能力画像），区分原生机制、报告性能、形式化保证与工程成熟度；辅以部署就绪度分析识别各角色背后的证据；据此构建三层MARL中心参考架构（语义推理、自适应协同控制、独立保证执行），并提出LLM-Augmented Dec-POMDP作为描述性对比记法，记录四种附着选择而不引入新决策过程类别或算法。

Result: 证据综述表明：传统MARL更适合任务特定训练后的高频、结构化、去中心化协调；LLM组件则适用于语义解释、奖励起草、人机交互及较慢的监督级规划。当前的纯LLM制造控制器在严格实时、去中心化、安全关键的控控场景尚未建立等价性。

Conclusion: LLM与MARL应分工协作而非全盘替代：LLM擅长高层语义与慢速监督推理，常规MARL擅长底层快速协调控制。该结论受现有证据约束，并不否认LLM未来在该场景的可能性。

Abstract: Modern manufacturing imposes six coupled demands on adaptive control: local decisions with global consequences, partial observability, nonstationarity, reflex speed response with long horizon effects, delayed and diffuse outcomes, and dynamics that resist explicit modeling. Cooperative multiagent reinforcement learning (MARL), posed as a Dec-POMDP under centralized training with decentralized execution, is a particularly natural formalism for these demands. This paper adopts a MARL centered scope and asks where large language models (LLMs) should augment, interface with, train, or, in the strongest competitive case, replace that coordination core. A taxonomy organizes the literature through four LLM attachment points: policy, reward design, communication between agents, and hierarchical planning. A conditional capability profile separates native mechanism, reported performance, formal guarantee, and engineering maturity, and a deployment readiness analysis identifies the evidence behind each role. These stages yield the principal contribution: a three layer MARL centered reference architecture, grounded in evidence, for semantic reasoning, adaptive cooperative control, and independently assured execution. The LLM-Augmented Dec-POMDP is a descriptive comparative notation for that architecture, recording four attachment choices without introducing a new decision process class or algorithm. Under the reviewed evidence, conventional MARL is better suited to frequent, structured, decentralized coordination after task specific training, whereas LLM components are promising for semantic interpretation, reward drafting, human interaction, and slower supervisory planning. Current LLM only manufacturing controllers do not yet establish equivalence for strict real time, decentralized, safety critical control; this conclusion is bounded by the available evidence and does not assert impossibility.

</details>


### [189] [Recipes for Creativity: Iterative Generation and Evaluation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07243)
*Rens Anderson,Tessa Verhoef,Amirhossein Zohrehvand*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该试点研究将FunSearch方法适配到2024年Pillsbury Bake-Off食谱生成任务中，检验迭代生成-筛选机制能否提升LLM创造力。结果显示迭代式生成-选择可产出与人类基准相当的创意食谱，但单纯增加迭代次数并无益；循环内评分器模型大小是影响创造力的首要因素，较小评分器在多数TTCT维度上显著更优，温度除原创性外影响有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式模型的评估通常基于单一产物，而人类创造力往往通过迭代生成、评估与精炼的过程涌现。现有研究缺乏对迭代搜索能否提升LLM创造力的系统验证，因此作者希望通过将FunSearch（进化式搜索方法）适配到开放式食谱生成任务中，探讨迭代搜索策略对创造力的影响。

Method: 采用两轮实验设计：(1) 将FunSearch方法适配到2024年Pillsbury Bake-Off食谱生成任务；2) 使用基于TTCT（托兰斯创造性思维测验）的LLM评估框架，将生成结果与人类基准对比。实验中系统性地操控三个变量：迭代轮数、生成器温度、以及循环内选择评分器的模型大小。

Result: 迭代式生成-选择流程能产出与人类基准创造力评分相当的食谱；但仅增加迭代次数并不能提升创造力分数。循环内评分器模型大小是最关键的设计变量——较小的选择评分器在大多数TTCT维度上产生显著更高的分数；温度参数除原创性维度外影响有限。

Conclusion: 在主观创造性搜索任务中，评估器（scorer）的设计是第一阶设计变量，而非迭代次数或生成器温度。这一发现为生成式模型中"评估驱动搜索"的研究路径提供了重要启示，提示设计者应优先关注评估器的选择与设计。

Abstract: Generative models are often evaluated through singular artifacts, whereas human creativity typically emerges through iterative generation, appraisal, and refinement. This pilot study examines whether iterative search improves LLM creativity by adapting FunSearch to recipe generation for the 2024 Pillsbury Bake-Off and evaluating outputs against human benchmarks using TTCT-based LLM evaluation. Across two experiments, we test iteration count, generator temperature, and in-loop selection-scorer model size. Results show that iterative generation-selection can produce recipes with creativity scores comparable to human benchmarks, but additional iterations alone do not improve creativity. The in-loop evaluator matters most: a smaller selection scorer yields significantly higher scores across most TTCT dimensions, while temperature has limited effects except for originality. These findings suggest that evaluator design is a first-order design variable in subjective creative search.

</details>


### [190] [NiyamAI - An Intent-Bound AI Agent with Cryptographically Verifiable Guardrails using Zero-Knowledge Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07167)
*Aditya Katkar,Om Karkele,Kartik Mandhane,Manisha More,Yash Kashid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Niyam-AI是一个使LLM智能体安全执行可证明（provable）的框架：在会话开始时，允许的工具和约束通过SHA-256锁定为意图合约（Intent Contract），每次工具调用由隔离的Judge模型验证，通过后生成zk-SNARK证明，工具仅在证明验证后才执行。在Agent-SafetyBench的2000个真实场景上，F1分数达88.5%，FP率为1.1%，显著优于NeMo Guardrails、Llama Prompt Guard 2和GPT-OSS-Safeguard（McNemar检验p<0.0001）。证明生成平均耗时2260.6ms，验证耗时53.1ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主LLM智能体面临提示注入、幻觉推理和不安全工具调用等主要攻击面。现有防御依赖系统提示或策略过滤器等软件检查，且与被攻击对象运行在同一台机器上，无法提供可验证的执行证明（verifiable proof of execution）。作者希望构建框架，使安全执行不仅有效，而且可以被第三方数学性地验证。

Method: Niyam-AI框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 会话开始时将允许工具和约束通过SHA-256提交为Intent Contract；(2) 每个工具调用由隔离的Judge模型拦截并验证；(3) 验证通过后经EZKL生成zk-SNARK证明，工具在执行前需完成证明验证。评估方法上，在Agent-SafetyBench的2000个场景上进行5折分层交叉验证，并使用bootstrap 95% CI和McNemar精确配对检验。

Result: 在Agent-SafetyBench上，Niyam-AI达到88.5%的F1分数和1.1%的假阳性率（bootstrap 95% CI: [85.19%, 91.88%], N=1000）。McNemar配对检验显示：Niyam-AI对NeMo胜390败20，对Prompt Guard 2胜115败13，对GPT-OSS-Safeguard胜384败19，所有p<0.0001。证明生成约为2260.6±218.4ms/次，验证约53.1±11.8ms。

Conclusion: Niyam-AI提供了一种既高准确率又数学上可验证的安全护栏方案，将零知识证明与LLM安全防护相结合。作者也坦诚指出了局限性：其分类器适应于Agent-SafetyBench，且基线为零样本设置，这一差异在Section IV.C中讨论。

Abstract: Giving an AI agent the ability to send emails, query databases, or execute commands is useful--until the agent is tricked into doing something it shouldn't. Prompt injection, hallucinated reasoning, and unsafe tool calls form the primary attack surface for autonomous LLM agents. Existing defenses rely on software checks like system prompts or policy filters running on the same machine the attacker targets, offering no verifiable proof of execution. We introduce Niyam-AI, a framework that makes safety enforcement provable. At session start, permitted tools and constraints are locked into an Intent Contract committed via SHA-256. Every tool call is intercepted and validated by an isolated Judge model; upon passing, a zk-SNARK proof is generated via EZKL.
  The tool executes only after proof verification, allowing third parties to confirm enforcement without accessing Judge model weights. Evaluating Niyam-AI on 2,000 real-world scenarios from Agent-SafetyBench against NeMo Guardrails, Meta's Llama Prompt Guard 2, and OpenAI's GPT-OSS-Safeguard using 5-fold stratified cross-validation yields an F1 score of 88.5% with a 1.1% false-positive rate (bootstrap 95% CI: [85.19%, 91.88%], N=1000). McNemar's exact paired test confirms significant improvement: Niyam-AI wins 390 discordant scenarios against NeMo (vs 20 losses), 115 against Prompt Guard 2 (vs 13), and 384 against GPT-OSS-Safeguard (vs 19) with p < 0.0001 in all cases.
  Proof generation adds 2260.6 +/- 218.4 ms per approved action, while verification takes 53.1 +/- 11.8 ms. Niyam-AI provides a guardrail that is both highly accurate and mathematically verifiable--though this reflects a classifier adapted to Agent-SafetyBench evaluated against zero-shot baselines, a distinction discussed in Section IV.C.

</details>


### [191] [Agent Memory Distillation: Empowering Small LLM Agents with Hierarchical Teacher Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07169)
*Taeil Kim,Kangsan Kim,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Agent Memory Distillation (AMD)，一种无需训练的框架，通过分层记忆将大型教师agent的结构化知识蒸馏到小型学生agent中，提升小模型在工具使用任务上的表现，在三个benchmark上平均提升27.2%p、11.2%p和3.4%p。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆系统在提升agent性能方面有潜力，但针对小语言模型的研究尚不充分。小模型难以自行生成足够的成功轨迹，导致其性能受限，因此需要从大模型中转移知识来增强小模型能力。

Method: AMD是一种训练free的框架，从大型教师agent的成功轨迹中构建三种互补的记忆类型：Workflow memory编码任务级策略，Subtask memory提供中间粒度的具体行为示例，Function memory捕获每个函数调用约定和常见陷阱。Workflow和Subtask记忆在任务开始时主动注入，Function记忆则在工具调用出错时被动检索。

Result: 在AppWorld、BFCL V3和ToolSandbox三个工具使用benchmark上，使用四个4B-8B参数的学生模型和GPT-5-mini作为教师，分别获得27.2%p、11.2%p和3.4%p的平均准确率提升，持续优于现有基于记忆的基线方法。分析表明Subtask记忆贡献最大，教师有效性取决于教师能力和学生兼容性，4B规模学生受益最多。

Conclusion: AMD通过分层记忆蒸馏有效增强小语言模型的工具使用能力，是一种无需训练的成本效益方案，且在不同模型规模和基准上表现出一致的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Memory systems have shown promise for improving agent performance, but their potential remains largely unexplored for small language models, which struggle to generate sufficient successful trajectories on their own. We propose Agent Memory Distillation (AMD), a training-free framework that transfers structured knowledge from a large teacher agent to a small student agent through hierarchical memory. AMD constructs three complementary memory types from successful teacher trajectories: Workflow memory encodes task-level strategies, Subtask memory provides concrete behavioral examples at an intermediate granularity, and Function memory captures per-function calling conventions and common pitfalls. Workflow and Subtask memories are injected proactively at the start of each task, while Function memory is retrieved reactively upon tool-calling errors. We evaluate AMD on three tool-use benchmarks using four student models (4B-8B parameters) with GPT-5-mini as the teacher, achieving average accuracy gains of 27.2%p, 11.2%p, and 3.4%p on AppWorld, BFCL V3, and ToolSandbox, while consistently outperforming existing memory-based baselines. Further analysis shows that Subtask memory contributes the largest gains, teacher effectiveness depends on both teacher capability and student compatibility, and 4B-sized students benefit most from AMD.

</details>


### [192] [GeoBenchLLM: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLMs on Geo-Related Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07411)
*Rodrigo Ferreira Rodrigues,Karim Radouane,Jose G Moreno,Lynda Tamine*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了GeoBenchLLM，一个综合性的地理相关大语言模型基准测试平台，整合了12个来自不同地理任务和领域的公开数据集，用于评估LLM在空间和时间理解方面的泛化能力。实验结果表明推理能力和模型规模对整体性能有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对地理数据的大语言模型研究通常在单一同质环境下进行，这极大地限制了对LLM泛化能力的深入理解。作者认为需要构建一个全面的基准来系统地评估LLM在地理领域的表现。

Method: 从多个地理相关任务和领域中精心挑选了12个公开数据集，构建了GeoBenchLLM基准，并对一组LLM在地理空间和时间理解能力上进行了系统评估。

Result: 研究发现推理能力和模型规模对整体性能有强烈影响。

Conclusion: GeoBenchLLM提供了评估LLM地理能力的关键洞察，即推理能力和模型大小是决定性能的主导因素，该基准已公开供社区使用。

Abstract: In the context of geodata, existing Large Language Models have often been studied in a homogeneous setting, which has considerably limited insights into their generalization capabilities. In this paper, we present \benchName, a comprehensive benchmark for probing LLMs on geo-related tasks. We leverage a careful selection of twelve publicly available datasets from diverse geo-related tasks and domains, and evaluate a set of LLMs on geo-spatial and temporal understanding using our benchmark. Our results show that reasoning and size have a strong impact on overall performance. GeoBenchLLM is publicly available at https://github.com/Rfr2003/GeoBenchLLM.

</details>


### [193] [SetEasy: A Multi-Modal Classroom Engagement Assessment and Seating Optimization Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07188)
*Zhihao Xie,Hongye Yang,Shien Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SetEasy 系统通过融合多模态感知（腕带生理数据、4K视频、环境数据）与基于改进 ISEQ 的 v-Gage 模型，实现课堂参与度预测与最优座位方案生成。在23名学生、331节课、四周的部署实验中，v-Gage 的 RMSE 从0.75降至0.53，优化后平均参与度从0.30提升至0.70，超过三分之二的座位达到高参与水平，后排低活动模式显著减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定座位网格的课堂环境中，学生参与度往往因座位位置而分布不均（如后排低参与问题），但缺乏系统化的数据驱动手段来优化座位安排以提升整体参与度。

Method: 采用"评估+优化"双阶段框架：1）多模态感知融合腕带生理信号、4K视频与环境数据；2）基于修订版 ISEQ 训练 v-Gage 模型预测两周参与度趋势；3）将预测结果映射为学生-座位效用矩阵；4）使用 CP-SAT 求解器在视觉可达与社会动态约束下生成最优座位方案。

Result: 四周部署中（23名学生、331节课），v-Gage 在情感、行为、认知及总体维度上均收敛，RMSE 从0.75降至0.53；优化后平均参与度从0.30提升至0.70，超三分之二座位达到高参与水平，后排低活动模式显著减少。

Conclusion: 无需硬件改造，仅通过可解释的、数据驱动的座位策略即可显著提升课堂参与度。该多模态"评估+优化"范式为文化响应式、差异化的空间设计提供了可迁移、可持续的路径。

Abstract: SetEasy optimizes classroom engagement in fixed seating grids. It fuses multimodal sensing (wristband physiology, 4K video, environmental data) and trains a v-Gage model grounded in a revised ISEQ. Each week, two-week engagement forecasts are mapped to a student-seat utility matrix, and CP-SAT generates seating plans under visual-access and social-dynamics constraints. In a four-week deployment (23 students, 331 classes), v-Gage converged across affective, behavioral, cognitive, and overall dimensions, cutting RMSE from 0.75 to 0.53. Optimization raised mean engagement from 0.30 to 0.70, with over two-thirds of seats reaching high engagement and back-row low-activity patterns markedly reduced. These results show that, without hardware changes, interpretable, data-driven seating strategies can substantially enhance engagement. The multimodal "assessment + optimization" paradigm offers a transferable, sustainable path to culturally responsive, differentiated spatial design amid global homogenization.

</details>


### [194] [ResidencyRL: Reinforcement Learning in Simulated Clinical Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07418)
*Valentin Liévin,Samuel Schmidgall,Tim Strother,Alex Bijamov,Akshay Goel,Anil Palepu,Chunjong Park,Vahid Balazadeh,Min Woo Sun,Marius Guerard,Justin Chen,Dave Steiner,Vikram Dhillon,Ibrahim Azar,Akhil Mehta,Nicholas Spetsieris,Shilpan Shah,Maen Abdelrahim,Amit Dahiya,Yun Liu,Katherine Chou,Yossi Matias,Avinatan Hassidim,Dale R. Webster,Quoc V. Le,Raia Hadsell,Joelle Barral,Carey Radebaugh,Aleksandra Faust,Shekoofeh Azizi,Mike Schaekermann,Po-Hsuan Cameron Chen,Tao Tu,David Racz,Lin Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ResidencyRL，一种通过模拟多轮临床对话（最多60轮对话和8次工具调用）训练临床AI智能体的强化学习方法。该方法将策略智能体与能表现出复杂对抗行为的LLM模拟器配对，根据诊断准确性、管理质量、沟通、文档和安全性等进行结构化奖励训练。在对抗条件下诊断准确率提升7.0%（88.0% vs 81.0%），漏诊红旗警示率降低31%，盲法专家在87.6%的对比中偏好训练后的智能体。该方法的程序性能力可迁移至未见基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在静态医疗基准上表现出色，但优化完整临床决策序列（从病史采集、诊断假设细化到治疗决策）的方法仍不成熟。临床推理本质上依赖于医患对话这一多轮交互过程，现有静态基准无法充分评估和训练这类序贯决策能力，因此需要一种能够通过模拟多轮临床对话来优化临床决策的方法。

Method: ResidencyRL采用强化学习方法，将策略智能体与能模拟复杂对抗行为的LLM模拟器配对，在模拟的多轮临床会谈（每轨迹最多60轮对话及8次工具调用）中进行训练。训练使用与诊断准确性、管理质量、沟通、文档记录和安全相关的结构化奖励信号，模拟住院医师通过数千次患者接触逐步建立临床专业知识的训练过程。

Result: 在对抗条件下诊断准确率提高7.0%（88.0%对81.0%）；漏诊红旗警示率降低31%，体现出对过早下结论的有力缓解。盲法临床专家验证了这些改进，在87.6%的并排比较中偏好训练后的智能体。程序性能力可迁移：在AMIE多访问基准的六个临床维度上均优于基础模型，并在AgentClinic和CRAFT-MD上表现出持续的方向性改进。

Conclusion: 序贯临床决策可以通过模拟环境中的多轮强化学习得到有效训练，从而产生稳健且可泛化的能力，为临床掌握铺平道路。但需要在真实世界工作流程中进行前瞻性验证以确定临床效用。

Abstract: In medical education, physicians convert academic knowledge into clinical expertise through residency: years of training across thousands of encounters, with diverse sources of feedback and progressively greater autonomy. Much of clinical reasoning relies on the patient encounter, a dialogue in which a clinician elicits history, refines diagnostic hypotheses, and decides management under uncertainty.
