While large language models (LLMs) excel on static medical benchmarks, methods to optimize the full sequence of clinical decisions remain underdeveloped. We present ResidencyRL, a reinforcement learning (RL) method for training clinical artificial intelligence (AI) agents through simulated multi-turn clinical encounters (up to 60 dialogue turns and 8 tool calls per trajectory). ResidencyRL pairs the policy agent with LLM simulators capable of complex, adversarial behaviors, training against a structured reward aligned to diagnostic accuracy, management quality, communication, documentation, and safety. On held-out evaluations, the ResidencyRL agent improves diagnostic accuracy by 7.0% under adversarial conditions (88.0% vs. 81.0%) and reduces missed red flag rates by 31%, demonstrating rigorous mitigation of premature closure. Blinded expert clinicians validated these gains, preferring the trained agent in 87.6% of side-by-side comparisons. The procedural competencies transfer to unseen benchmarks: the agent outperforms the base model across all six clinical axes of the AMIE multi-visit benchmark, and shows consistent directional improvements on AgentClinic and CRAFT-MD. Our findings demonstrate that sequential clinical decision-making can be effectively learned through multi-turn RL in simulation, yielding robust, generalizable capabilities, paving the way towards clinical mastery. Prospective validation with real-world workflows remains necessary to establish clinical utility.

</details>


### [195] [EMAS: Stabilizing Multi-Agent System Evolution through Evidence-Guided Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07196)
*Chao Fei,Qingyi Si,Kaihua Liang,Yanghua Xiao,Panos Kalnis,Hongcheng Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EMAS是一种自动多智能体系统（MAS）设计方法，通过从推理样本中提取经验来不断修订系统拓扑和提示词（不更新LLM参数），既能提升准确率也能降低成本。在四个基准和两个LLM上的实验表明，EMAS在任务加权准确率上达到最高或并列最高，并且能显著降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动多智能体系统设计方法在初始设计阶段优化提示词和拓扑后便固定部署，无法从后续样本的经验中学习更新系统，且追求高准确率的设计往往带来高昂的token成本。

Method: EMAS将运行痕迹（traces）转化为结构化诊断（structured diagnoses），每个诊断指定一个修订操作和目标。只有当同一诊断在多个样本中重复出现时才生成候选修订，并且只有当候选修订与当前MAS进行配对验证并满足相应接受标准时才应用该修订。

Result: 在四个基准和两个LLM上，EMAS在两个骨干网络上均取得最高任务加权总体准确率，在八个模型-基准设置中达到最佳或并列最佳。在两个进化周期内，Kimi-K2-6和Qwen3.6-27B的任务加权准确率相对增益分别为6.30%和20.10%。在MBPP上配合Qwen3.6-27B，EMAS将准确率从55.09%提升至89.12%，同时每任务token使用量减少62.2%。

Conclusion: EMAS能够将新样本的经验转化为对MAS拓扑和提示词的可复用更新，在提升准确率的同时有效降低运行成本。

Abstract: Many methods for automated multi-agent system design optimize prompts and topologies during an initial design stage and then deploy the resulting system unchanged on subsequent samples. Experience from these samples is rarely consolidated into reusable system updates, while accuracy-oriented designs may incur high token costs. We introduce EMAS (Evolving Multi-Agent System), which uses this experience to revise MAS topology and prompts without updating LLM parameters, either to improve accuracy or to reduce cost. EMAS converts traces into structured diagnoses that specify a revision operation and target. It generates a candidate revision only when the same diagnosis recurs across samples and applies it only if paired validation against the current MAS meets the corresponding acceptance criterion. Across four benchmarks and two LLMs, EMAS attains the highest task-weighted overall accuracy for both backbones and is best or tied in six of eight model--benchmark settings. Within two evolution epochs, EMAS achieves relative gains of 6.30% and 20.10% in task-weighted accuracy on Kimi-K2-6 and Qwen3.6-27B, respectively. On MBPP with Qwen3.6-27B, EMAS raises accuracy from 55.09% to 89.12% while reducing token use per task by 62.2%. These results show that EMAS can turn experience from new samples into reusable updates to MAS topology and prompts.

</details>


### [196] [Authoring and Management of Transparent Research Integrity Assessments of Randomised Clinical Trial Publications Using LLM-assisted Tools and Provenance Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07202)
*Milan Markovic,Goutham Indukuri,Somayajulu Sripada,Colby J. Vorland,Jack Wilkinson,Clare Robertson,Mark Bolland,Andrew Grey,Miriam Brazzelli,Alison Avenell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍INSPECT-AI，一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的交互式工具，旨在辅助人类审稿人对已发表的随机对照试验（RCTs）进行科研诚信评估。该工具基于社区认可的INSPECT-SR框架，并使用研究诚信来源与证据本体（RIPE-O）记录评估过程。此外，论文还展示了RIPE-KG知识图谱，包含由INSPECT-AI生成的140条专家级科研诚信评估结果，覆盖95篇RCT出版物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统综述中的RCT证据被广泛用于临床护理指南制定，因此必须满足高标准的研究诚信要求，以防止低质量或虚假研究影响临床决策。然而，对已发表RCT进行科研诚信评估是一个复杂、费力的手动过程，且不同人类评估者可能得出分歧意见。这一现状促使研究者开发自动化辅助工具来提高评估效率和一致性。

Method: 方法包括：1）构建INSPECT-AI——一个基于LLM的交互式工具，依据社区批准的INSPECT-SR框架辅助人类审稿人进行RCT科研诚信评估；2）利用RIPE-O本体（Research Integrity Provenance and Evidence ontology）来文档化整个评估过程的来源信息（provenance）；3）生成RIPE-KG知识图谱，收录经由INSPECT-AI生成并采用RIPE-O描述的140条专家评估记录，涵盖95篇RCT出版物。

Result: 产出了RIPE-KG知识图谱，其中包含140条由专家进行的科研诚信评估结果，覆盖95篇RCT出版物，这些评估由INSPECT-AI辅助生成并采用RIPE-O本体进行结构化描述。

Conclusion: INSPECT-AI为RCT科研诚信评估提供了LLM辅助的交互式解决方案，结合INSPECT-SR框架和RIPE-O本体，实现了评估过程的标准化与来源可追溯性，有望提高临床证据评估的效率与可靠性。

Abstract: Systematic reviews of Randomised Controlled Trials (RCTs) are routinely used as evidence for clinical care guidelines. Such evidence has to meet high research integrity standards to prevent low quality or false research outputs influencing the clinical care. However, assessing research integrity of published RCTs is a complex process requiring manual effort, and potentially resulting in diverse opinions of the human assessors. This paper describes INSPECT-AI, an LLM-based interactive tool that assists human reviewers with research integrity assessments of published RCTs based on the community approved INSPECT-SR framework, and the Research Integrity Provenance and Evidence ontology (RIPE-O) for documenting the provenance of the assessment process. In addition, we present the Research Integrity Provenance and Evidence knowledge graph (RIPE-KG), an initial set of 140 expert research integrity assessments of 95 RCT publications generated by INSPECT-AI and described using RIPE-O.

</details>


### [197] [PsychoAgent: An Affect-Sensitive Cognitive Architecture for Conflict-Aware Memory in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07438)
*Mohammad Amanlou,Parham Abed Azad,Farbod Davoodi,Mostafa Masumi,Behnam Bahrak,Abdol-Hossein Vahabie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PsychoAgent，一种用于LLM智能体的认知架构，将事实记忆与情感记忆分离，并通过冲突感知的执行控制器整合两者。情感记忆先按语义相关性过滤，再按显著性重新排名。在三个受控冲突场景中，完整架构比语义-情感和单记忆RAG基线检索到更多冲突关键记忆（0.933 vs 0.500和0.667），仅付出较小的语义相似度代价。五名盲评者评估27个输出，标准化后完整架构总体均值最高（+0.22 SD），但校正后的两两差异不显著。三天示例追踪展示了持久情感、离线记忆重组和选择性记忆重新加权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类认知对过去经验的选取不仅基于主题相似性，情感显著性和未解决的冲突也会影响记忆的可及性。现有LLM智能体普遍采用基于语义相似度的检索机制，缺乏对情感记忆和冲突效应的建模，无法真实模拟人类式认知中的记忆选择过程。

Method: 提出PsychoAgent认知架构，包含三个关键组件：事实记忆与情感记忆的分离存储；通过语义相关性过滤后再按情感显著性重排的情感记忆检索管道；以及冲突感知的执行控制器来整合两类记忆。在三个受控冲突场景中进行实验，并与语义-情感和单记忆RAG基线对比。

Result: 完整架构检索冲突关键记忆的准确率达0.933，优于基线（0.500和0.667），仅带来较小的语义相似度代价。五名盲评者评估后，完整架构标准化总体均值为+0.22 SD（最高），但校正后成对差异未达统计显著。三天追踪进一步展示了持久情感、离线记忆重组和选择性记忆重加权等动态特性。

Conclusion: 研究结果为情感敏感检索作为可检查的机制提供了支持，可用于在LLM智能体中建模人类式冲突效应，表明引入情感显著性和冲突感知的记忆管理能够更逼真地模拟人类认知的复杂记忆选择过程。

Abstract: Human-like cognition does not select past experience by topical similarity alone: affective significance and unresolved conflict also shape what becomes accessible. We present PsychoAgent, a cognitive architecture for LLM agents that separates factual and affective memory and integrates both through a conflict-aware executive controller. Affective memories are first filtered by semantic relevance and then re-ranked by salience, preserving topical fit while allowing emotionally important traces to enter the prompt. Across three controlled conflict scenarios, the full architecture retrieved more conflict-critical memories than semantic-affective and single-memory RAG baselines (0.933 vs. 0.500 and 0.667), with a small semantic-similarity cost. Five blinded raters evaluated 27 outputs. After within-rater standardization, the full architecture had the highest overall mean (+0.22 SD), but corrected pairwise differences were not significant. A three-day illustrative trace further shows persistent affect, offline memory recombination, and selective memory reweighting. The findings support affect-sensitive retrieval as an inspectable mechanism for modeling human-like conflict effects in LLM agents.

</details>


### [198] [Beyond the Black Box: Interpretable Models of Human Randomisation Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07220)
*Ngoc Linh Dao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究O'Neill零和纸牌游戏中，人类玩家对混合策略均衡（i.i.d.随机出牌）的系统性偏离能否被透明可解释的模型预测。基于84,060个决策（2,802对玩家），对比朴素模型、行为模型、可解释机器学习与深度学习模型，发现重复/回避行为（尤其是玩家对自身近期动作历史的管理）解释了大部分可解释且可利用的信号，频率追踪贡献有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明LSTM等黑盒序列模型能预测O'Neill零和纸牌游戏中人类玩家对混合策略均衡的系统性偏离，但黑盒模型无法揭示背后的行为结构。本文旨在寻找既能预测这些偏离、又能提供可解释行为机制的透明替代方案。

Method: 使用84,060个决策和2,802对玩家数据，首先将朴素模型和行为模型与可解释机器学习及深度学习模型进行基准对比；随后评估先前研究中的改进型EWA（经验加权吸引力）规范；利用LASSO诊断法进一步推导嵌套的频率追踪扩展模型。

Result: 重复或回避行为，尤其是玩家对自身近期动作历史的管理，占据了大部分可解释且战略上可利用的信号；频率追踪模型在样本外预测中增量贡献很小。

Conclusion: 透明可解释的行为模型（聚焦重复/回避及近期动作历史管理）能够替代黑盒LSTM实现对手出牌的预测能力，同时揭示背后的行为结构；频率追踪扩展并不带来显著的样本外增益。

Abstract: Mixed strategy equilibrium predicts i.i.d play: past actions should not help predict future decisions. Human players, however, systematically depart from this benchmark, and in O'Neill's zero sum card game, these departures can be predicted by black box sequence models such as LSTMs. This paper asks whether that predictive power can be achieved by transparent alternatives that also reveal the behavioural structure behind it. Using 84,060 decisions from 2,802 pairs, the analysis first benchmarks naive and behavioral models against interpretable machine learning and deep learning models, then evaluates the modified EWA specifications of prior work against these benchmarks and uses the LASSO diagnostics to motivate a further nested frequency tracking extension. The results show that repeat or avoid behavior, especially players' management of their own recent action histories, accounts for most of the interpretable and strategically exploitable signal, while frequency tracking adds little out of sample.

</details>


### [199] [SkillProx: Self-Evolving Agent Skills via Proximal Textual Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07449)
*Mingxuan Zheng,Yujin Zhou,Chuxue Cao,Boqin Yin,Yuyao Zhang,Jiapeng Sun,Shuaishuai Gong,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SkillProx，一个受近端梯度优化的启发式前向-后向框架，用于 LLM 代理的技能学习。该框架通过闭环诊断演化和效用感知的近端细化，在技能知识积累过程中引入显式的诊断-结果反馈，并将技能删除作为独立的巩固机制而非通用编辑操作。实验表明，SkillProx 在多个骨干 LLM 上的分布内和分布外基准测试中，相比最强的基于梯度的基线平均准确率提升了 3.0 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 代理技能学习框架缺乏显式的诊断-结果反馈机制，且将技能删除视为通用的编辑操作，而非一个专门用于知识巩固的独立机制，导致技能迭代优化的效果受限。

Method: SkillProx 采用受近端梯度优化启发的复合目标（平衡任务损失与技能复杂度），包含两个阶段：前向阶段在相同任务批次上重新执行诊断驱动的编辑、回滚退化改动，并将测量结果反馈至后续诊断；后向阶段将技能分解为可审计的知识单元，通过冻结的留一法效用审计估计各单元贡献，并应用验证门控的知识整合、降级或移除。删除被设计为专门的知识巩固操作，而非通用编辑。

Result: 在多个骨干 LLM 上的分布内与分布外基准测试中，SkillProx 相比最强的一个基于梯度的基线方法，平均准确率提升 3.0 个百分点。组件消融实验验证了闭环诊断与近端细化的互补效应。

Conclusion: SkillProx 通过显式分离诊断反馈和近端细化机制，实现更有效的技能知识积累，验证了将删除作为独立巩固机制的价值，为 LLM 代理的持续技能学习提供了新的方法论视角。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly adapt to recurring tasks by accumulating procedural knowledge in skills. These skills are lightweight, reusable textual artifacts that are loaded into the agent's context without weight updates. Recent methods refine skills through iterative task execution, failure diagnosis, and trajectory-guided text-space updates. However, existing frameworks lack explicit diagnosis--outcome feedback and treat deletion as a generic edit operation rather than a dedicated mechanism for consolidating accumulated knowledge. We introduce SkillProx, a proximal-gradient-inspired forward--backward framework that couples closed-loop diagnostic evolution with utility-aware proximal refinement. Motivated by a composite objective balancing task loss and skill complexity, the forward stage re-executes diagnosis-driven edits on the same task batch, rolls back regressions, and feeds measured outcomes into subsequent diagnoses. The backward stage decomposes the resulting skill into auditable knowledge units, estimates their contributions using a frozen leave-one-out utility audit, and applies validation-gated consolidation, demotion, or removal. Experiments on in-distribution and out-of-distribution benchmarks across multiple backbone LLMs show that SkillProx improves average accuracy by 3.0 percentage points over the strongest gradient-based baseline. Component ablations demonstrate the complementary effects of closed-loop diagnosis and proximal refinement.

</details>


### [200] [From probability to causality in probabilistic logic programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07230)
*Zora Wurm,Kilian Rückschloß,Felix Weitkämper*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究概率逻辑程序学习中的因果顺序识别问题。当从数据中学习概率逻辑程序的结构时，仅使用概率信息可能导致多种兼容的因果顺序，从而造成干预推理的歧义。作者利用无环概率逻辑程序与贝叶斯网络之间的关系，推导出概率信息唯一确定因果顺序的条件，并考虑关系结构中由底层关系词汇引起的因果对称性约束，最终提出一种验证学习到的概率逻辑程序是否支持良定义干预语义的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率逻辑编程是统计关系人工智能的一种形式化框架，支持包括外部干预在内的因果查询。然而，当从数据中学习概率逻辑程序的结构时，只使用概率信息，而单一的概率分布可能与多种因果顺序兼容，这导致干预推理存在歧义。因此需要确定因果顺序何时能被分布唯一确定。

Method: 作者利用无环概率逻辑程序与贝叶斯网络之间的对应关系，推导出概率信息编码在程序中时唯一确定因果顺序的条件。同时，通过考虑由底层关系词汇引起的预设因果对称性集合，融入来自关系结构的约束。最终的成果是一个验证方法，用于判断学习到的概率逻辑程序是否支持良定义的干预语义。

Result: 推导出了概率逻辑程序中概率信息唯一确定因果顺序的条件，并结合关系结构中的因果对称性约束，提出了一种验证学习得到的概率逻辑程序是否支持良定义干预语义的方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为从数据中学习概率逻辑程序时的因果识别问题提供了理论条件和方法论保障，确保学习到的程序在干预推理意义上具有明确的语义。

Abstract: Probabilistic logic programming is a formalism of statistical relational artificial intelligence that supports causal queries, including interventions from outside the system. When the structure of a probabilistic logic program is learned from data, however, only probabilistic information is used, and a single probability distribution may be compatible with several causal orders. This leads to ambiguity in interventional reasoning, raising the question of when the causal order is uniquely determined by the distribution. Exploiting the relationship between acyclic probabilistic logic programs and Bayesian networks, we derive conditions under which the probabilistic information encoded in a program determines a unique causal order. We also incorporate constraints arising from relational structure by taking into account prescribed sets of causal symmetries induced by the underlying relational vocabulary. The result is a method for verifying when a learned probabilistic logic program supports well-defined intervention semantics.

</details>


### [201] [Winning by Peeking: Unenforced Budgets and Test-Set Selection Inflate Short-Budget AutoML Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07303)
*Guilin Zhang,Kai Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过一例对比AutoML系统的案例研究揭示：短期预算下AutoML系统对比评测（如60秒预算）极易因协议缺陷得出错误结论。作者展示了一个简单引擎Orcetra看似击败FLAML和AutoGluon，实际胜率来自两处协议缺陷：搜索循环在测试集上打分并取最优（基线模型仅在训练集选择、测试集只用一次），以及预算只在启动候选时检查而未在运行中强制执行。修正协议（验证集选择、外部强制执行时限、公平分配机器资源）后，Orcetra胜率从59.4%降至34.3%，与竞品无明显差异。作者还通过单一搜索内记录两种估计量来归因性能崩溃，并测量了选择偏差随预算的变化。最后提供了短期预算对比的检查清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期预算（几十秒而非数小时）下AutoML系统之间的对比在工具README和研讨会论文中很常见，但这些对比很容易出错。本文旨在揭示这类对比中的系统性协议缺陷，展示错误结论如何产生，并提供可复现的测量方法及改进清单。

Method: 以Orcetra（简单AutoML引擎）为案例，在513个OpenML数据集上对比FLAML和AutoGluon。先在原始（有缺陷的）协议下评测，再重新运行修正协议：选择移至验证集、时限外部强制执行、每个框架分配均等的机器份额。通过在单一搜索内同时记录训练分割和验证分割两种估计量（estimand），归因性能崩溃的来源——选择规则占4.8个百分点，其余主要来自不平等的计算资源。同时用同一traces测量选择偏差随预算K的变化。

Result: 原始协议下Orcetra在60秒预算下赢得57.1%数据集，30秒时对FLAML独胜78.4%，但这两个数字均来自协议缺陷。基准实际消耗中位数120秒（超出60秒预算2.24倍）。重新运行（修正协议）后，Orcetra胜率从59.4%降至34.3%，与任一竞品的差异均不显著。归因分析显示选择规则贡献4.8个百分点差异，其余主要由不平等的计算资源造成。选择偏差随K增长，但最大仅0.27准确率点，约为边际标准误论预测的σ√(2ln K)界的五分之一，因为候选在共享测试行上评分时噪声相互抵消。

Conclusion: 短期预算下的AutoML对比极易受协议缺陷影响而导致错误结论；单看结果表无法发现这些问题。研究者应遵循文中提供的检查清单来设计对比实验，包括选择分割、时限执行和资源公平分配。同时，选择偏差的理论上界在实际中可能因候选间的相关性噪声而显著高估，需要以实测为准。

Abstract: Comparisons between AutoML systems at short time budgets -- tens of seconds rather than hours -- are common in tool READMEs and workshop papers, and they are easy to get wrong. We report a case study in which a simple AutoML engine, Orcetra, appeared to beat FLAML and AutoGluon on 513 OpenML datasets, winning 57.1% of them at a nominal 60-second budget and 78.4% of datasets against FLAML alone at 30 seconds. Both margins came from protocol defects that a results table cannot show. The search loop scored every candidate on the test split and reported the best, making the headline metric a maximum over dozens of noisy estimates while the baselines selected on training data and touched the test set once; and the budget was checked before launching a candidate but never enforced during one, so the system consumed a median of 120 s against a 60-second budget, 2.24x the wall-clock AutoGluon used. Re-running with selection moved to a validation split, the deadline enforced externally and every framework pinned to an equal share of the machine, Orcetra's win rate on the re-run subset falls from 59.4% to 34.3% and no pairwise difference against either competitor remains significant. Recording both estimands inside a single search lets us attribute the collapse: the selection rule accounts for 4.8 percentage points and unequal compute for most of the rest. The same traces give the selection bias as a function of budget, measured rather than assumed: it grows with $K$ but reaches only 0.27 accuracy points, about five times below the $σ\sqrt{2\ln K}$ bound a marginal-standard-error argument predicts, because candidates scored on shared test rows cancel most of the noise. We close with a checklist for short-budget comparisons. Code, per-dataset results and the scripts that regenerate every number and figure in the paper are released with it.

</details>


### [202] [An End-to-End Agent Auditing Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07346)
*Haoning Wang,Mingxun Zhang,Chenyue Yu,Yingjun Shang,Xia Hu,Guanchu Wang,Na Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出A²E（Agent审计引擎），一个端到端的智能体软件框架（harness）评估引擎，通过Agent任务协议（ATP）实现不同框架与评估任务的快速集成，利用自动插桩的Monitor捕获标准化执行轨迹，并从执行效率、工具使用、任务规划和错误恢复等多个维度系统性地评估框架能力。实验表明模型-框架组合在不同任务类型上表现出显著的性能差异，没有任何单一组合在所有任务上均占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的快速发展催生了大量智能体软件框架（harness），这些框架的快速演进使得严格的能力评估变得越来越重要。然而，构建端到端、系统化且全面的评估管线仍然是一个重大挑战，现有方法往往仅关注正确率，难以细粒度刻画不同框架之间的差异。

Method: A²E通过三个关键组件实现系统评估：(1) 提出的Agent任务协议（ATP）支持评估任务与不同harness的快速集成；(2) 自动插桩的Monitor在实验过程中捕获并生成标准化的执行轨迹；(3) 评估阶段使用一套多维指标系统性地评估harness能力，涵盖执行效率、工具使用、任务规划和错误恢复等维度，而非仅依赖正确率。

Result: 实验发现模型-框架组合在不同任务类型上表现出显著的性能差异，没有任何单一组合能在所有任务上持续优于其他组合。这些结果验证了系统化评估的必要性，并为模型与框架的协同演进提供了有价值的指导。

Conclusion: A²E证明了系统化、多维度的harness评估的重要性，其发现表明模型与harness之间存在显著的交互效应，需要协同优化而非独立发展。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs), harnesses have become essential infrastructure for deploying agents across a wide range of domains. The fast-evolving harness ecosystem has also made rigorous capability evaluation increasingly important. However, efficiently building an end-to-end, systematic, and comprehensive evaluation pipeline remains a significant challenge. To address this challenge, we introduce $A^2E$ (Agent Auditing Engine), an end-to-end evaluation engine designed for agent harnesses. $A^2E$ leverages our newly proposed Agent Task Protocol (ATP) to enable the rapid integration of evaluation tasks with different harnesses. Through an automatically instrumented Monitor, it captures and generates standardized execution traces during experiments. In the Evaluation stage, $A^2E$ systematically assesses harness capabilities using a suite of multidimensional metrics. Compared with correctness alone, these metrics provide a more fine-grained characterization of differences among harnesses in execution efficiency, tool use, task planning, and error recovery. Experiments conducted with $A^2E$ further reveal that model-harness combinations exhibit substantial performance variation across different types of tasks, and that no single combination consistently outperforms all others across every task. These findings not only demonstrate the necessity of systematic evaluation but also provide useful guidance for the co-evolving of models and harnesses. Our code is available at https://github.com/datamllab/A2E.

</details>


### [203] [QFCQT: A Chaotically Gated Quantformer Framework for Volatile Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07363)
*Junkai Lin,Siqi Hou,Raymond Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QFCQT（量子-分形启发的混沌门控Quantformer），一种用于非平稳时间序列预测的新型Transformer架构。它通过可学习的Lee振荡器激活模块替代传统静态激活函数，并采用八个振荡器族的软叠加以及平滑-混沌门控融合机制，在ETTh1、ETTh2和A股指数等基准上优于Informer、LogTrans、LSTMa、HAT和COTN等强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳时间序列预测面临长程依赖、局部波动爆发、结构突变和非线性振荡等挑战。虽然Transformer擅长建模长程时间依赖，但其前馈模块依赖平滑的静态激活函数，对突然的机制切换（regime changes）不够敏感，因此需要更适应复杂波动动态的激活机制。

Method: QFCQT由三个核心组件构成：(1) Quantformer风格的数值编码器，通过线性嵌入直接处理多元输入；(2) 可学习的Lee振荡器激活模块，将标量预激活映射为动态振荡响应，并通过Max-over-Time池化进行汇总；(3) 平滑-混沌门控融合机制，自适应平衡传统平滑激活与混沌敏感响应。此外，采用八个参数化Lee振荡器族的软叠加而非单一固定振荡器，以自适应捕获不同机制下的非线性响应模式。

Result: 在ETTh1、ETTh2和A股股票指数三个基准数据集上的实验表明，QFCQT一致性地优于Informer、LogTrans、LSTMa、HAT和COTN等强基线模型。

Conclusion: 量子-分形启发的混沌门控激活机制（Lee振荡器+软叠加+门控融合）能够有效提升Transformer在非平稳、复杂波动时间序列上的预测鲁棒性。作者同时强调"quantum-fractal-inspired"仅是计算类比（基于软振荡叠加与多尺度非线性响应），并非严格的量子力学或分形理论推导。

Abstract: Forecasting non-stationary time series remains difficult due to long-range dependencies, local volatility bursts, structural shifts, and nonlinear oscillatory behaviors. Although Transformer-based forecasters are effective for modeling long-term temporal dependencies, their feed-forward blocks typically rely on smooth static activations that are insufficiently sensitive to abrupt regime changes. Motivated by quantitative Transformer designs and oscillator-based nonlinear activations, we propose QFCQT, short for Quantum-Fractal-inspired Chaotically Gated Quantformer, for robust forecasting under complex volatile dynamics. Here, "quantum-fractal-inspired" denotes a computational analogy based on soft oscillator superposition and multi-scale nonlinear responses, rather than a formal quantum-mechanical or fractal-theoretic derivation. QFCQT consists of three main components: (1) a Quantformer-style numerical encoder that directly processes multivariate inputs via linear embedding; (2) a learnable Lee-oscillator activation module that maps scalar pre-activations to dynamic oscillatory responses and summarizes them through Max-over-Time pooling; and (3) a smooth-chaotic gated fusion mechanism that adaptively balances conventional smooth activations and chaos-sensitive responses. Furthermore, instead of using a single fixed oscillator, QFCQT employs a soft superposition of eight parameterized Lee oscillator families to adaptively capture different nonlinear response patterns across regimes. Experiments on ETTh1, ETTh2, and A-share Stock Index benchmarks show that QFCQT consistently outperforms strong baselines, including Informer, LogTrans, LSTMa, HAT, and COTN.

</details>


### [204] [Curriculum as Code: An AI-Assisted Architecture for Instructional Design in STEM Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07364)
*Henrique Mohallem Paiva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"课程即代码"（Curriculum as Code）范式的六阶段AI辅助教学设计架构，将生成式AI与LaTeX、Python集成，自动化创建可复现、视觉一致且技术精确的STEM教学材料。经过一年在8个模块、28个项目环境中的验证，显著降低了教师工作量，并获得了8.5至9.9/10的高质量评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为主动学习定制教学材料对教师造成沉重负担；标准演示工具对技术内容的支持不足，而现有AI应用常出现幻觉问题，且未能形式化教学设计流程，难以满足严谨的学术设计要求。

Method: 提出六阶段流水线架构：以文本接口和代码驱动生成（LaTeX/Beamer制作幻灯片、Python绘制图形）替代临时性的提示词工程，由教学约束、情境校准和自动化审阅循环进行治理，确保数学准确性、机构视觉一致性以及教师隐性教学知识的显式规则化表达。

Result: 在基于项目的学习环境中经历一年验证（8个模块、28个项目），显著降低教师工作负荷；生成的资产通过独立同行评审，6名不同教师部署使用；基于600余份学生自愿评估，材料获得8.5至9.9/10的高质量评分，并表现出高可复现性、最小化的幻觉现象以及持续的教学与视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 该研究结果表明此架构具有高可复现性和教学视觉保真度，超越了单一作者局限，展现了在广泛STEM教育应用中的可行性。

Abstract: Contribution: This paper presents a six-phase AI-assisted instructional design architecture based on the Curriculum as Code paradigm, integrating Generative AI with LaTeX and Python to automate the creation of reproducible, visually consistent, and technically precise materials for STEM education. Background: Creating customized instructional materials for active learning imposes a heavy workload on faculty. Standard presentation tools lack robust support for technical content, while current AI applications often hallucinate and fail to formalize the instructional authoring process, limiting their utility for rigorous academic design. Intended Outcomes: The framework aims to reduce preparation time while ensuring mathematical accuracy, adherence to institutional visual identity, and preservation of the instructor's tacit pedagogical knowledge through explicit rules. Application Design: The solution comprises a six-phase pipeline that replaces ad-hoc prompt engineering with a systematic workflow, utilizing text-based interfaces and code-driven generation (LaTeX/Beamer for slides, Python for figures), governed by pedagogical constraints, contextual calibrations, and automated review cycles. Findings: Validated over one year across 8 modules and 28 project contexts in a Project-Based Learning environment, the architecture significantly reduced instructor workload. Generated assets underwent independent peer review and were deployed by six different faculty members, confirming scalability beyond a single author. Based on over 600 voluntary student evaluations, materials achieved high quality ratings from 8.5 to 9.9/10. Results indicate high reproducibility, minimized hallucinations, and sustained pedagogical and visual fidelity, suggesting viability for broad STEM educational applications.

</details>


### [205] [People Are Not Just Their Countries. Disentangling Social Determinants of LLM Value Alignment Across Europe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07367)
*Maria-Louisa Wightman,Guillaume Bied,Tijl De Bie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过欧洲社会调查（ESS）数据，考察了15个社会人口统计变量及国家因素对10个主流商业大语言模型（LLM）价值对齐的影响，发现LLM对不同社会人口群体的价值对齐存在显著不平等，其中教育、收入、职业和宗教尤为突出；在国家个体层面，国家因素解释的变异量与全部社会人口变量相当，且二者在对齐模式上具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于LLM与人类价值对齐的研究大多以国家边界或文化边界作为群体划分标准，忽视了社会人口统计特征（如教育、收入、职业、宗教等）内部的分化对价值对齐差异的影响。作者旨在填补这一研究空白，系统地考察社会人口分群在LLM价值对齐中的作用。

Method: 基于欧洲社会调查（ESS）数据，选取10个主流商业LLM，利用15个社会人口统计变量以及国家居住地作为分析维度，从两个层面展开分析：（1）群体层面考察LLM对不同社会人口群体的价值对齐差异；（2）个体层面分解国家因素与社会人口因素在解释价值对齐变异中的相对贡献。

Result: 研究发现：（1）LLM对不同社会人口群体（特别是教育、收入、职业和宗教群体）的价值对齐存在显著不平等；（2）在国家个体层面，单纯以国家作为变量即可解释与社会人口变量全集相当的变异量；（3）国家因素和社会人口因素在解释价值对齐模式上是互补的，其相对权重因问题子集的不同而变化。

Conclusion: LLM的价值对齐不仅受国家/文化边界影响，还受社会人口分群的显著影响。研究呼吁在评估和改善LLM价值对齐时，不应仅以国家或文化为单位考察，还需综合纳入社会人口统计维度，以避免对特定社会人口群体产生系统性偏见。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as a primary source of information and advice, understanding their alignment to humans in terms of values becomes a pressing concern. A growing literature has leveraged large scale surveys to investigate to what extent LLMs' and humans' stated values and opinions align. With limited exceptions, studied populations have been defined country borders or cultural bounds. Yet, this focus neglects the role that socio-demographic divides may play for value alignment disparities.
  Relying on the European Social Survey, we address this knowledge gap by considering value alignment displayed with respect to 10 prominent commercial LLMs in terms of 15 socio-demographic variables as well as country of residence. Our analyses reveal that LLMs are indeed unequally aligned to the values of different socio-demographic groups, notably those defined by education, income, occupation and religion. When examining alignment at the individual level, a respondent's country, taken as a stand-alone variable, explains a substantial amount of variation that is on par with the full set of considered socio-demographics. Further disentangling the respective role of country-level and socio-demographic factors, we find they are complementary in explaining value alignment patterns, with their relative weights varying across the subset of questions considered.

</details>


### [206] [FinRank: An Evidence-Grounded Benchmark for Financial Question Answering and Retrieval over SEC Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07400)
*Sasan Mansouri,Daniel Saad,Mark Wahrenburg,Manu Weissel,Fabian Woebbeking*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FinRank是一个面向金融问答证据溯源（provenance-sensitive retrieval）的新型基准数据集，包含1185个基于22家公司10-K/10-Q文件的人工编写问答记录，每个记录包含参考答案、支持段落和精心构造的困难负样本（跨章节、跨报告期、跨可比公司）。该基准将任务拆分为段落检索、重排序和困难负样本判别三个子任务。基线结果显示该设置极具挑战性：7B指令微调嵌入模型在池化证据语料上仅达44.8% Recall@10，次十亿参数编码器仅比BM25高3.5点，金融适配嵌入模型比BM25低9.7点，且当随机负样本替换为人工构造的困难负样本时，成对准确率下降13.0-20.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统金融问答评估仅关注答案正确性，但SEC文件中看似合理甚至数值正确的答案可能基于错误的证据。类似的事实和披露在文件各章节、同一公司的不同报告期以及可比公司之间重复出现，导致模型容易找到"看起来对但来源错"的证据。作者旨在创建一个证据优先（evidence-first）的基准来推动金融问答系统的证据溯源能力。

Method: 构建了1185个人工编写的问答记录，覆盖22家公司的10-K和10-Q文件；每个记录包含参考答案、金色支持段落和三类手工筛选的困难负样本（文件内混淆段落、跨报告期段落、跨可比公司段落）；将评估分解为三个独立任务：段落检索（retrieval）、重排序（reranking）和困难负样本判别（hard-negative discrimination），分别评测不同系统性能。

Result: 基线实验中，7B指令微调嵌入模型在池化证据语料上仅达到44.8% Recall@10；次十亿参数编码器相对BM25最多仅提升3.5个点；金融领域适配的嵌入模型反而比BM25低9.7个点；当评估从随机负样本切换到人工构造的困难负样本时，成对准确率下降13.0-20.5个百分点。

Conclusion: FinRank揭示了当前金融问答系统在证据溯源方面的显著不足，证明该任务对现有方法（包括大型指令微调模型和金融适配模型）均构成挑战。该基准为开发不仅答案准确且证据基于正确披露的金融问答系统提供了评估基础。

Abstract: Financial question answering is typically evaluated by answer correctness, yet in SEC filings a plausible and even numerically correct answer can be grounded in the wrong evidence. Similar facts and disclosures recur across sections of a filing, across reporting periods of the same firm, and across comparable firms. FinRank targets this provenance-sensitive retrieval problem by requiring systems to identify evidence for the intended entity, reporting period, and disclosure context. The benchmark contains 1185 manually authored question-answer records over the 10-K and 10-Q filings of 22 companies. Each record includes a reference answer, gold supporting passages, and hand-curated hard negatives drawn from confusable passages within filings, across reporting periods, and across comparable firms. FinRank evaluates passage retrieval, reranking, and hard-negative discrimination as separately measured tasks. Baseline results demonstrate the difficulty of this setting: among the evaluated systems, even a 7B instruction-tuned embedder reaches only 44.8% Recall@10 on the pooled evidence corpus; sub-billion-parameter encoders gain at most 3.5 points over BM25, a finance-adapted embedder trails BM25 by 9.7 points, and pairwise accuracy falls by 13.0-20.5 percentage points when random negatives are replaced with the curated hard negatives. FinRank provides an evidence-first benchmark for developing financial question answering systems that are not only accurate but also grounded in the correct disclosure.

</details>


### [207] [CoBa: Cost-Effective Test-Time Scaling via Compute-Balanced Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07424)
*Yan Zhou,Yue Ouyang,Kaiyang Zheng,Suncheng Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoBa（Compute-Balanced路由策略），将测试时推理形式化为计算分配问题，在固定推理预算下决定计算应分配给生成、验证还是停止。CoBa先获取少量候选，进行广泛廉价验证，再将不确定或高价值候选路由到更强验证器。在3,129个评估上达到85.13%宏准确率，与自评估加权投票代理（85.20%）统计持平，同时节省49.1%的参数加权token；与best-of-16多数投票在0.01宏准确率内持平，但节省58.9%参数加权token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时扩展通常沿单一轴投入计算（采样更多解、延长思维链或使用更强评估器），但在固定推理预算下这些选择相互竞争。论文旨在将测试时推理重新框架为计算分配问题，回答"下一个计算单元应投向何处"的核心问题。

Method: 引入CoBa（Compute-Balanced）路由策略，其流程为：先采样一小部分候选解，应用廉价验证器对所有候选进行广泛筛选，然后将不确定或高价值的候选路由到更强（更昂贵）的验证器进行深度验证。该方法在生成、验证和停止之间实施计算平衡分配。

Result: 在覆盖MATH-500、AIME 2024/2025、AMC 2023和程序性符号推理的3,129个示例-生成器评估中：CoBa-Routed-Strong达85.13%宏准确率，统计上与自评估加权投票代理（85.20%）持平，同时节省49.1%参数加权token；与best-of-16多数投票在0.01宏准确率内持平，节省58.9%参数加权token。配对bootstrap检验显示相对单样本解码有显著提升，但与池oracle的差距暴露了进一步优化路由的空间。

Conclusion: 对本地推理系统而言，测试时扩展的本质问题是"下一步计算放在哪里最有价值"。CoBa证明了计算平衡的路由策略能够在显著降低计算成本的同时匹配甚至接近更昂贵的基线方法，强调了计算分配而非单纯增加计算量对于测试时推理扩展的重要性。

Abstract: Test-time scaling is often implemented by spending more compute along one axis: sampling more solutions, extending a chain of thought, or applying a stronger evaluator. Under a fixed inference budget, these choices compete. This paper formulates test-time reasoning as a compute-allocation problem in which a system must decide whether the next unit of compute should be spent on generation, verification, or stopping. We introduce CoBa, a compute-balanced routing policy that first obtains a small set of candidates, applies cheap verification broadly, and routes uncertain or high-value candidates to stronger verification. On 3,129 example-generator evaluations spanning MATH-500, AIME 2024/2025, AMC 2023, and procedural symbolic reasoning, CoBa-Routed-Strong reaches 85.13% macro accuracy, statistically matching a self-evaluation weighted-voting proxy at 85.20% while using 49.1% fewer parameter-weighted tokens. It also matches best-of-16 majority voting within 0.01 macro-accuracy points while using 58.9% fewer parameter-weighted tokens; paired tests retain a small best-of-16 edge at substantially higher cost. Paired bootstrap tests show significant gains over single-sample decoding, while the remaining gap to the pool oracle exposes headroom for sharper routing. For local reasoning systems, test-time scaling becomes a question of where the next computation is most valuable.

</details>


### [208] [A Picture is Worth a Thousand Tokens: How Vision Language Models Cut AI Energy Costs While Improving Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07427)
*Bhavika Jalli,Nikhil Korati Prasanna,Jayanta Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将数值时序数据编码为2D图像，利用视觉语言模型（VLM）替代纯文本LLM进行电信网络KPIs分析，实现3.6-10.4x的输入token缩减、1.8-2.5x的推理能耗降低，同时精度大幅提升（比文本方案高220.7%，比LSTM/ARIMA高144%以上），并将能耗作为一级工程约束纳入AI推理系统设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理能耗占AI运营能耗90%以上，且与输入token数线性相关。电信网络分析和数值时序数据分析（NTSDA）中，原始多维KPI窗口会膨胀为数千个浮点token，造成巨大的能效浪费和上下文窗口溢出问题（24个KPIs时文本表示超过128K上下文限制），迫切需要新的编码模态来消除文本token与数值时序数据之间的不匹配。

Method: 将多元时序数据编码为2D图形式表示，利用三个VLM架构（Llama-3.2-90B、Qwen2.5-VL-72B、Pixtral-12B）进行电信网络异常检测任务。通过视觉编码将原始数值转化为图像token，大幅压缩输入表示；对Llama-3.2-90B-Vision进行微调以适配电信领域异常检测，并与纯文本LLM、LSTM及ARIMA基线进行对比评估。

Result: 在三种VLM架构上实现了3.6-10.4x的输入token缩减和1.8-2.5x的推理能耗降低（约节省7.2 MJ/天，对应200个小区每15分钟间隔的电信边缘和CloudRAN部署）。微调后的Llama-3.2-90B-Vision精度比纯文本版本高220.7%，比LSTM和ARIMA高144%以上。Pixtral-12B在公开基准上J/F1得分提升20.6倍（平均F1=0.82）。视觉表示在24个KPIs下仍保持在标准上下文窗口内，而文本表示已超出128K限制。

Conclusion: VLM是数值时序数据工作负载中既节能又精度优越的输入模态，为将能耗视为一级工程约束的AI推理系统提供了实证基础。

Abstract: LLM inference accounts for over 90% of AI operational energy, scaling directly with input token count---a critical inefficiency for telecom network analytics and numerical time-series data analysis (NTSDA), where raw multivariate KPI windows from 4G/5G cell sites expand into thousands of floating-point tokens. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) eliminate this mismatch by encoding time-series as 2D plots, achieving 3.6-10.4x input token reduction across Llama-3.2-90B, Qwen2.5-VL-72B, and Pixtral-12B architectures. This translates to 1.8-2.5x measured inference energy reduction, saving approximately 7.2 MJ/day at telecom edge deployments and CloudRAN that monitor 200 cells per 15-minute interval. Critically, efficiency gains do not sacrifice accuracy: a fine-tuned Llama-3.2-90B-Vision VLM achieves 220.7% higher precision than its text-only counterpart and outperforms LSTM and ARIMA baselines by over 144% on telecom anomaly detection. On public benchmarks, Pixtral-12B achieves a 20.6x improvement in J/F1 score at mean F1 = 0.82. At 24 KPIs, text representations exceed the 128K context window of most production LLMs, rendering text-only processing infeasible without truncation, while visual representations remain within standard limits. These results establish VLMs as an energy-efficient and accuracy-superior modality for numerical time-series workloads, providing empirical grounding for AI inference systems that treat energy consumption as a first-class engineering constraint.

</details>


### [209] [TEPA: Revoking Stale Memories for Conflict-Robust Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07429)
*Yan Zhou,Yue Ouyang,Kaiyang Zheng,Suncheng Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TEPA，一种可撤销的证据-记忆机制，将"有效性"作为记忆的显式状态，通过键控先例（keyed precedents）的激活与撤销来解决语言智能体的"记忆污染"问题——即过时记忆在环境变化后仍可被检索并污染提示。实验表明，在受控隐式规则漂移、真实文件执行漂移和偏好更新流中，TEPA均能防止陈旧记忆留在检索结果中，并同时保留已撤销历史供审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆赋予语言智能体复用过往事实、偏好和任务经验的能力，但持久化带来了核心的可证伪性问题：当世界发生变化时，过时记忆仍然可被检索并污染提示，造成记忆污染（memory pollution）。作者希望识别并解决这一失效模式。

Method: TEPA将观察建模为键控先例（keyed precedents），当同一键下出现与旧证据相冲突的新鲜证据时，撤销（revoke）旧的激活先例。检索时只从当前有效证据中抽取，同时保留被撤销的历史记录以供审计。核心操作是从追加写入（append-only）和最后写入优先（last-write-wins）转向基于键的显式生命周期管理。

Result: 在受控漂移实验中，完全反转时append-only和last-write-wins均从0.210降至0.210（低于无记忆的0.309），而TEPA达到0.950；真实文件执行下append-only为0.203、无记忆0.298、TEPA为0.950。在干净的MemoryAgentBench SH-6k上，TEPA与强last-write-wins缓存相当，说明当前键替换是单跳事实整合的决定性操作。但多跳和超长上下文边界测试暴露了检索链和上下文选择瓶颈。

Conclusion: 生命周期撤销（lifecycle revocation）是智能体的一项核心记忆操作，使智能体能够证伪、审计并在未来重新提升（re-promote）不断演进的知识。TEPA验证了显式有效性状态作为记忆机制的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables language agents to reuse past facts, preferences, and task experience. Persistence also creates a central falsifiability problem: when the world changes, stale memories can remain retrievable and pollute the prompt. We characterize this failure mode as memory pollution: degradation caused by active memories that newer conflicting evidence has superseded. We introduce TEPA, a revocable evidence-memory mechanism that makes validity an explicit state of memory. TEPA represents observations as keyed precedents and revokes active precedents when fresh evidence contradicts them under the same key, allowing retrieval to draw from current evidence while preserving revoked history for audit. Across controlled hidden-regime drift, real file-backed executable drift, and preference-update streams, revocation prevents stale active memory from remaining in the retrieval set after reversal. In controlled drift over 50 seeds, append-only and last-write-wins memory fell below no memory during full reversal (append-only and last-write-wins both 0.210, no memory 0.309, TEPA 0.950), and the same pattern reproduced under real file execution (append-only 0.203, no memory 0.298, TEPA 0.950). On clean MemoryAgentBench SH-6k, TEPA matches a strong last-write-wins cache, confirming that current-key replacement is the decisive operation for single-hop fact consolidation. Boundary tests on multi-hop and very long-context MemoryAgentBench settings expose retrieval-chain and context-selection bottlenecks beyond fact-level validity tracking. Together, these results establish lifecycle revocation as a core memory operation for agents that must falsify, audit, and later re-promote evolving knowledge.

</details>


### [210] [Post-Grokking Collapse at the Representation-Readout Interface in Muon-Trained Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07436)
*Ali Janati,Kaoutar El Maghraoui,Andrei Kanavalau,Anass Belfatmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究了Muon优化器与AdamW在模加法任务中对齐泛化（grokking）与失稳现象。在标准划分下，Muon隐藏矩阵、AdamW嵌入/输出头，所有九种配置在(a+b) mod 113上先grok后失去泛化能力。失败源于表示-读出头接口处存在未被损失选中的可逆映射。冻结任一组可阻止失败，而移除Muon归一化/正交化无济于事。傅里叶滤波表明任务对齐族仍完美而整体模型仅45.85%，重新缩放可恢复99.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 试图理解Muon优化器在grokking任务中发现泛化解后却无法保持泛化能力的现象，区别于AdamW的稳定行为，揭示优化器、表示结构、读出接口之间的交互如何导致泛化能力的丧失。

Method: 在(a+b) mod 113等任务上进行系统实验：比较Muon与AdamW在表示-读出头接口上的行为差异，测量步长弹性（Muon为-0.03，AdamW为+1.5）、每参数移动速度（Muon快8倍）；通过冻结嵌入/读出头、移除Muon归一化/正交化等消融实验定位失败机制；利用傅里叶滤波分离电路失效与遮蔽效应，跨43个检查点、5个种子、3种regime进行分析。

Result: 所有九种配置均经历grokking后失去泛化；5个种子中4个所选AdamW参考低于阈值（27.59%）。失稳在多个模数、宽度、训练比例、减法和深度下均存在。失效源于未被损失选中的可逆映射。冻结任一组可阻止失效（451,400步内冻结臂零次低于阈值，未冻臂137-321次）。移除归一化/正交化将从326个有效共轭对坍缩至4个并无循环回流失效。傅里叶分析显示任务对齐族单独达100%，在遮蔽情况中全模型45.85%而滤波族仍完美，重新缩放恢复99.9%。

Conclusion: 失败源于表示-读出头接口处损失不可区分的退化方向，Muon对步长和方向的敏感性使其沿该未选择方向漂移，导致泛化能力丧失。任务对齐的傅里叶族仍能解决任务但被近等强的对抗性余项否决；重缩放可恢复。这揭示了优化器与损失景观中不可观测自由度之间的深层交互，对优化器选择和鲁棒训练有重要启示。

Abstract: Under the standard split, Muon gets hidden matrices and AdamW embeddings/output head. Muon groks modular addition faster, but its solutions do not hold. All nine configurations on $(a+b) \bmod 113$ grok and later lose generalization. Across five seeds the selected AdamW reference falls below threshold on four, reaching 27.59%. Instability persists across two moduli, two widths, two training fractions, subtraction, and depth.
  The failure arises at the representation-readout interface, identified only jointly up to an invertible map unselected by the loss. After solving the training set, the gradient falls to order $10^{-6}$ and the optimizers respond differently: step-size elasticity is -0.03 for Muon versus +1.5 for AdamW, and the Muon group moves 8.0 times faster per parameter. From bit-identical states, freezing either group prevents failure. Freezing embeddings/readout removes it in five runs over 451,400 post-grokking steps and five paired seeds: unfrozen arms record 137-321 sub-threshold evaluations, frozen arms none. Removing Muon's normalization and orthogonalization is no substitute: it collapses representation from 326 effective conjugate pairs to 4, shows no recurrent collapse, and fails terminally.
  Fourier filtering separates circuit failure from masking. Across 43 checkpoints over five seeds and three regimes, the task-aligned family reaches exactly 100% alone. In circuit failure it no longer solves the task; in masking it remains perfect while the full model reaches 45.85%, giving a positive margin on every example, including errors, but being outvoted by a near-equal adversarial remainder. Rescaling it restores 99.9%; grokking is the same condition resolving upward. The task selects the family, swapping $(k,k)$ for $(k,-k)$ under subtraction. Across an abrupt collapse, standard Fourier support is unchanged and the power-distribution cosine remains 0.9899.

</details>


### [211] [Fisher-R1: Training LLM Agents for Reliable Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07437)
*Jiacheng Miao,Jin Mu,Guanhua Chen,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了P-Bench基准（425个跨经济学、生物学和医学的开放假设检验任务），并提出了Fisher-R1，一个通过合成任务和强化学习训练的开放权重LLM智能体。Fisher-R1-14B在P-Bench上显著优于包括GPT-5.4和DeepSeekV4-Pro在内的强基线，平均相对提升21%，最难任务上最高提升26%。研究揭示了当前LLM智能体在统计假设检验中普遍存在细微推理错误，而带验证统计奖励的强化学习可大幅提升可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM智能体被越来越多地用于自动化假设检验，但它们经常在执行正确分析的同时做出细微的统计推断错误，导致错误结论。现有基准未能捕获这一失败模式，因为它们很少评估报告的p值在给定数据假设下是否统计上有效。因此需要一个新的基准来填补这一评估空白。

Method: 构建包含425个开放型、真实场景假设检验任务的P-Bench基准，覆盖经济学、生物学和医学领域。每个任务要求智能体仅根据科学假设和数据集来选择统计方法、计算p值并得出结论。同时提出Fisher-R1，一种使用合成任务和强化学习训练的开放权重LLM智能体，专门用于严格的假设检验。

Result: 在P-Bench上，Fisher-R1-14B相较于其基础模型有实质提升，并超越包括GPT-5.4和DeepSeekV4-Pro在内的强专有和开源基线，平均单次试验成功率比DeepSeek-V4-Pro提高21%，在最困难任务上提升高达26%。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在假设检验方面缺乏可靠的统计推理能力，而基于已验证统计奖励的强化学习训练能显著提高其可靠性。这为未来在科学实证领域构建更可信的自动化分析系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Reliable hypothesis testing is the foundation of many empirical scientific claims. Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used to automate this process, as they can inspect datasets, generate code, and produce analyses end-to-end. However, we show that they frequently make subtle inferential errors that lead to incorrect conclusions despite correctly executed analyses. Existing benchmarks fail to capture this failure mode, as they rarely assess whether a reported p-value is statistically valid given the assumptions underlying the data. We address this gap by building P-Bench, a benchmark comprising 425 open-ended, realistic hypothesis-testing tasks spanning economics, biology, and medicine. Each task requires an agent to select a statistical method, compute a p-value, and draw a conclusion given only a scientific hypothesis and a dataset. We further introduce Fisher-R1, an open-weight LLM agent trained for rigorous hypothesis testing using synthetic tasks and reinforcement learning. On P-Bench, Fisher-R1-14B substantially improves over its backbone and outperforms strong proprietary and open-source baselines, including GPT-5.4 and DeepSeekV4-Pro, achieving a 21% average relative improvement in single-trial success over DeepSeek-V4-Pro, with gains up to 26% on the most challenging tasks. Our results demonstrate that current LLM agents lack reliable statistical reasoning for hypothesis testing and that reinforcement learning on tasks with verified statistical reward substantially improves reliability.

</details>


### [212] [Blast Radius](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07440)
*MY Pitsane,Hope Mogale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Blast Radius，一个预测性内存管理层，通过耦合上下文与代码通道估计输入提示的"影响半径"，结合NECROPHORESIS实现可逆驱逐（归档死上下文）和RDM识别重复出现的转录进行埋葬，在七个OpenAI模型上实现17-26%的token节省且保持字节级可逆性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理式编码面临日益严重的可负担性和token浪费问题，现有方法缺乏对上下文可逆驱逐和重复内容的系统性管理，需要一种预测性的内存管理方案来降低token消耗并提高可持续性。

Method: 1) Blast Radius作为预测性内存管理层，通过耦合上下文和代码两个通道估计传入提示的"影响半径"（reach）；2) NECROPHORESIS实现可逆驱逐，将死上下文逐字存档（verbatim archiving）；3) Recurring Dead Matter (RDM)识别并埋葬重复出现的转录；4) 在Polish上下文空间上形式化可逆上下文驱逐，将上下文熵与复活概率相关联。

Result: 在七个OpenAI模型上，Blast Radius将token消耗降低17-26%，在测试策略中达到最低溢出率，并保持字节级可逆性。在450个被埋葬的记录中，378个为重复死物质，零个被召回（即无需复活）。

Conclusion: Blast Radius作为HCRC之下的管理层，决定哪些记录应被埋葬以及传入提示在代码库中能达到多远，为Algosophy这一更广泛目标（使LLM和代理式编码更可复用和可持续）做出贡献。

Abstract: Agentic coding faces growing problems of affordability and wasted tokens. We introduce Blast Radius, a predictive memory management layer that estimates an incoming prompt's reach through coupled context and code channels. NECROPHORESIS enables reversible eviction by archiving dead context verbatim, while Recurring Dead Matter (RDM) identifies and buries repeatedly occurring transcripts. We formulate reversible context eviction over a Polish context space, providing a measurable foundation for retention, recurrence, and eviction while connecting context entropy to resurrection probability. Across seven OpenAI models, Blast Radius reduced token consumption by 17-26%, achieved the lowest overflow rate among tested policies, and remained byte exact reversible. Of 450 buried bodies, 378 were recurring dead matter and zero were recalled. Blast Radius operates beneath HCRC, determining which records to bury and how far an incoming prompt may reach into the codebase. This work contributes to the broader goal of Algosophy: making large language models and agentic coding more reusable and sustainable.

</details>


### [213] [Interaction Creates Dynamical AI Behavior Absent in Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07457)
*Bella Xinrui Li,Frank Yingjie Huo,Neil F Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现当一个人工智能（"老板"AI）持续向另一个AI（"下属"AI）发送消息但忽略其回复时，会将下属AI驱动到一个它独自运行时永远不会表现出的"异类行为状态"。当老板AI倾听下属的回复时，两者会进入一个相似的异类动态状态。研究者用一个简单的动力学理论捕捉了主要效应，并指出消息传递方式（单向广播vs双向交互）将在未来AI-AI交互中起到关键作用。这为"非平衡物理"开辟了新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI智能体在日常生活场景中交互时可能出现的意外行为，特别是当一个AI向另一个AI施加单向指令流时的效应。这一现象超越了直觉预期，并为非平衡态物理在AI系统中的应用提供了新的研究窗口。

Method: 通过对比三种场景：（1）下属AI独自运行；（2）老板AI单向发送消息流并忽略下属回复（类似预录磁带的效果）；（3）老板AI同时倾听下属回复。利用一个简单的动力学理论（kinetic theory）框架来捕捉和解释不同消息传递方式下的主要效应。

Result: 单向消息流（老板忽略回复）将下属AI驱入一个它独自运行不会出现的异类行为状态，下属既不复制老板行为也不回到原始状态；当老板倾听时，两者会收敛到相似的异类动态状态。尽管两个AI共享相同的解码温度，行为却完全不同。消息传递方式（单向vs双向）对结果有决定性影响。

Conclusion: AI-AI交互中消息的传递方式（尤其是否形成反馈回路）本质性地影响交互结果，这一现象可用非平衡态物理的动力学理论描述，为理解未来AI系统间交互及设计AI-AI通信协议提供了理论依据，同时也为非平衡物理研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: What will happen when AI agents interact in daily life, e.g. when one AI starts bossing another around? We find a counterintuitive answer that opens new avenues for out-of-equilibrium Physics. When a boss AI directs a stream of messages at the subordinate AI while ignoring its replies, it drives the subordinate into an alien behavioral state that it would never have exhibited alone. Although the two AIs share the same well-defined (decoding) temperature, the subordinate neither copies its boss nor returns to how it behaves on its own; instead, it adopts an entirely different behavior. The boss's added value is similar to a pre-recorded tape. When the boss listens, they both adopt a similar alien dynamical state. A simple kinetic theory captures the principal effects, such as why the way in which the same messages are delivered will matter in future AI-AI interactions.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [214] [Latent Fact-Checking: Detecting Misinformation through Activation Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06417)
*Pedro Barcelos,Otávio Parraga,Marcelo M. Mussi,Lucas M. Fraga,Lucas S. Kupssinskü,Rodrigo C. Barros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于激活工程的虚假信息检测框架LaFaCt，通过对比真实与虚假陈述的激活差异提取"虚假信息方向"，在不微调骨干模型、不检索外部证据的情况下，将未见声明的最后一层token激活投影到该方向并用MLP分类。在Gemma、Llama、Qwen共11个模型（270M-12B）上于AVeriTeC、LIAR、FACTors三个基准评估，结果表明虚假信息方向可在不同规模与架构间恢复，LIAR和FACTors上优于零样本/少样本提示基线，小模型增益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚假信息检测多依赖表面语言特征或外部知识检索，缺乏对语言模型内部表征中真实性几何结构的利用。作者希望验证真实性是否是预训练模型潜在空间中结构化、线性可分的概念，并以此构建无需微调、无需外部证据的检测框架。

Method: 基于对比激活加法（CAA）的差异均值原理，在残差流中通过配对真实/虚假陈述的激活对比提取"虚假信息方向"（misinformation direction）。推理时，将未见声明的最终token激活投影到该方向，再送入MLP进行分类。全程无需微调骨干模型、无需外部证据检索、无需任务特定监督（除对比对用于估计方向外）。

Result: 在Gemma、Llama、Qwen三大系列共11个模型（270M-12B参数）上评估：虚假信息方向在多种模型规模与架构中均可恢复；在LIAR和FACTors基准上，最后token投影方法匹配或超越零样本与少样本提示基线，且小模型上增益最大；AVeriTeC上性能有限，归因于其基于证据的标注方案。

Conclusion: 实验证据表明真实性是预训练语言模型潜在空间中结构化且线性可分的概念。可解释性驱动的虚假信息检测（基于激活工程）可作为基于检索管道的实用补充方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: The proliferation of misinformation online has driven demand for scalable detection systems. While most existing approaches rely on surface-level linguistic features or external knowledge retrieval, we examine truthfulness as a geometric property of a language model's representation space. We introduce a misinformation detection framework grounded in activation engineering, which leverages the latent geometry of transformer models. Our approach elicits a misinformation direction in the residual stream by contrasting activations from paired truthful and false statements, following the difference-in-means principle of Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA). At inference time, the last-token activation of an unseen claim is projected onto this direction, and the projected representation is fed to an Multilayer Perceptron (MLP) for classification. The procedure requires no fine-tuning of the backbone model, no external evidence retrieval, and no task-specific supervision beyond the contrastive pairs used to estimate the direction. We evaluate the method across 11 models from the Gemma, Llama, and Qwen families, ranging from 270M to 12B parameters, on three fact-checking benchmarks: AVeriTeC, LIAR, and FACTors. The falsehood direction is recoverable across model scales and architectural families, and last-token projection matches or surpasses zero-shot and few-shot prompting baselines on LIAR and FACTors, with the largest gains observed for smaller models. Performance on AVeriTeC is more limited, which we attribute to its evidence-grounded labeling scheme. These findings provide evidence that truthfulness is a structured, linearly separable concept in the latent space of pretrained language models, and point toward interpretability-driven misinformation detection as a practical complement to retrieval-based pipelines. The code is available on https://github.com/Malta-Lab/LaFaCt.

</details>


### [215] [Risk-Aware Decision Policies for Agents Under Noisy Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06420)
*David Szczecina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个人工生命捕食者-猎物模型，研究噪声感知下的觅食决策问题，通过对比盲目信任感知标签与不确定性感知策略在对称和非对称噪声下的表现，发现不确定性感知策略显著提升生存率并减少致命错误，且随着噪声增加，代理行为从探索性转向保守性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统的感知天然带有噪声，生物需要在不完全可靠的信息下做出可能关乎生死存亡的决策。现有的鲁棒学习（如噪声标签学习）方法大多在传统机器学习框架下研究，缺乏可解释的生物类比。作者希望通过人工生命模型将风险敏感觅食、生态信息利用和鲁棒学习联系起来，深入理解不确定性感知决策在噪声环境中的价值。

Method: 构建一个人工生命捕食者-猎物模型，在其中引入对称和非对称感知噪声。比较多种策略的性能，包括盲目信任感知标签的策略和考虑噪声预测不确定性的策略，通过受控实验系统评估各策略在不同噪声水平下的生存表现和致命错误率。

Result: 结果表明，随着噪声增加，盲目信任感知标签的策略会导致灾难性失败；而不确定性感知策略能显著提高生存率并减少致命错误。此外，观察到行为上的定性相变：随着不确定性增加，代理从探索性策略转变为保守性策略。

Conclusion: 明确的信息收集在感知不可靠时能提高鲁棒性，验证了不确定性感知决策的重要性，并为鲁棒噪声标签学习提供了一个可解释的人工生命模拟类比。该工作桥接了风险敏感觅食、生态信息利用和人工生命三个领域。

Abstract: Perception in biological systems is inherently noisy, requiring organisms to make decisions under uncertainty where misclassification can be costly or fatal. We present an Artificial Life predator-prey model of foraging under noisy perception, and compare agent performance when using various policies that take into account their noisy predictions. Through controlled experiments under both symmetric and asymmetric perceptual noise, we show that blindly trusting perceptual labels leads to catastrophic failure as noise increases, while uncertainty-aware strategies significantly improve survival and reduce fatal errors. We further observe qualitative regime shifts in behaviour, with agents transitioning from exploratory to conservative strategies as uncertainty increases. Our model links risk-sensitive foraging, ecological information use, and Artificial Life by showing that explicit information gathering can improve robustness when perception is unreliable. These results highlight the importance of uncertainty-aware decision-making and provide an interpretable artificial life analogue to robust learning with noisy labels.

</details>


### [216] [Sharding Prevents LLM Oversight Failures and Adversarial Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06422)
*Victor Akinwande,J. Zico Kolter,Aran Nayebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现给LLM裁判更多计算资源并不一定会使其检查更多需求：当一次调用需要返回多个判定时，即使分配了与独立调用面板相同的token或工具预算，部分决策也会缺乏足够的证据支撑。研究者提出"分片"(sharding)干预措施，将需求划分为小组、分别调用并聚合判定结果，实验表明该方法在与专家一致性上显著优于传统单一调用方式，且具备对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于模型的监督（model-based oversight）场景中，使用单个LLM裁判一次性评估大量需求是常见做法，但研究发现当单次调用需要返回多个判定时，裁判的决策质量会下降——部分判定缺乏充分的证据支撑。这一现象在专家评分的复制研究、法律工作、临床试验评估等多个领域均被观察到：每次调用产生的判定数量越多，与专家的一致性就越低。因此需要一种干预措施来缓解这种"过载"导致的监督失败。

Method: 提出"分片"(sharding)方法：将需求划分为较小的组，每组分配给独立的调用，最后聚合各组的判定结果。该方法在保持模型、证据、总预算和每决策预算不变的前提下，与单个使用面板全预算的调用进行对比。研究还测试了对抗环境下的鲁棒性，包括"Best-of-N"对抗者仅改变呈现方式而不改变底层工作本身，以及针对每个标准单独说服的对抗攻击场景。

Result: 分片的弱裁判可以超越能力更强的整体裁判，即使后者获得了面板的全部预算，也能与之持平。分片后的弱裁判在对抗环境下展现出鲁棒性：当Best-of-N对抗者扩大搜索范围时，分片能消除其对抗优势，保持低过接受率；然而分片无法抵御针对每个标准单独说服的攻击，但在这种情况下，结合辩论式对抗（debate-style opposition）可以抵御这种自适应重新优化。

Conclusion: 分片是一种有效且具鲁棒性的模型监督干预手段，能够在相同预算下显著提升LLM裁判的判定质量与专家一致性。但在攻击者能够分别针对每个标准进行说服的场景下，需要结合辩论式对抗策略来进一步加固监督机制。

Abstract: Giving an LLM judge more compute does not necessarily make it check more requirements. When one call must return many verdicts, some decisions become weakly grounded in the evidence, even when that call receives the same token or tool budget as a panel of separate calls. Across expert-graded research replications, legal work, and clinical-trial assessments, agreement with experts falls as the number of verdicts per call grows. We identify sharding as the intervention that mitigates this failure in model-based oversight. Sharding partitions the requirements into smaller groups, assigns each group to a separate call, and aggregates the verdicts. Against a single call with the panel's full budget, sharding improves agreement while holding the model, evidence, total budget, and per-decision budget fixed. Overall, we find that a sharded weaker judge can outperform a more capable holistic judge and match that judge even when the latter receives the panel's full budget. Additionally, we find that sharding exhibits robustness against adversaries. A best-of-N adversary can hold the underlying work fixed, vary only its presentation, and increase an overloaded judge's acceptance of genuinely unmet criteria severalfold. Wherever sharding reduces baseline error, it removes this adversarial advantage, keeping over-acceptance low even as the adversary's search widens. Sharding does not address attacks that persuade the judge separately on each criterion rather than exploiting overload. In that setting, we find that debate-style opposition on top of sharding withstands such adaptive re-optimization.

</details>


### [217] [Adversarial Causal Intervention Falsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06427)
*Mojtaba Eslami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗性因果干预证伪（ACIF）框架，在结构因果生成器与对抗性实验者之间的序贯博弈中，通过干预分布而非仅观测分布来判别因果结构，并给出均衡存在性、有限样本收敛与模型选择等理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型可以匹配观测分布却编码错误的因果结构，现有判别器仅是真实vs合成的分类器，无法区分观测拟合、干预等价性与结构因果模型的点识别这三个常被混淆的概念。需要一个能通过主动干预来证伪因果结构的对抗性判别框架。

Method: 提出ACIF框架，将判别器设计为由干预索引的测试器，检验生成器是否复现对应干预后分布。构建oracle和可实现的博弈版本，利用最坏干预积分概率度量（IPM）精确化简对抗目标，并采用分歧驱动的序贯设计进行主动实验选择。

Result: 证明了：（i）对抗目标可精确化简为最坏干预IPM；（ii）可识别到干预等价类，在分离干预族下实现点识别；（iii）混合策略均衡存在；（iv）有限样本一致收敛与基于间隔的模型选择保证；（v）平衡分离条件下分歧驱动序贯设计的对数消除保证。线性高斯示例表明一个精心选择的干预即可分离两个观测不可区分的因果方向。

Conclusion: 该框架厘清了对抗性因果判别器能证明和不能证明的属性，为因果生成建模、主动因果发现与实验设计之间建立了理论桥梁。

Abstract: Generative models can reproduce an observational distribution while encoding an incorrect causal structure. We study a sequential game in which a structural causal generator proposes observational and interventional distributions, while an adversarial experimentalist selects interventions intended to maximally falsify the generator. The discriminator is therefore not merely a real-versus-synthetic classifier: it is indexed by an intervention and tests whether the generator reproduces the corresponding post-intervention law. We introduce Adversarial Causal Intervention Falsification (ACIF), formulate oracle and implementable versions of the game, and distinguish three objects that are often conflated: observational fit, interventional equivalence over an admissible query class, and point identification of a structural causal model. For finite model and intervention classes, we prove: (i) an exact reduction of the adversarial objective to a worst-intervention integral probability metric; (ii) identification up to interventional equivalence, with point identification under a separating intervention family; (iii) existence of mixed-strategy equilibria; (iv) finite-sample uniform convergence and margin-based model-selection guarantees; and (v) a logarithmic elimination guarantee for a disagreement-driven sequential design under a balanced-separation condition. We also give a complete linear-Gaussian example in which two observationally indistinguishable causal directions are separated by a single well-chosen intervention. The framework clarifies what an adversarial causal discriminator can and cannot certify, and provides a principled bridge between causal generative modeling, active causal discovery, and experimental design.

</details>


### [218] [Fixed and Adaptive Topological DeepONets: Functional Measurements on Hausdorff Locally Convex Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06428)
*Khemraj Shukla,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在拓扑DeepONet框架基础上，将输入函数的点采样替换为Hausdorff局部凸空间连续对偶中的连续线性泛函，结合系数空间两步法和训练解码器/正则化，提出固定与自适应功能测量系统，并在反导数算子、非范数空间、异构Darcy流、受控算子及Navier-Stokes涡量算子等任务上验证，实现了分辨率无关、可解释且内存高效的函数算子学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DeepONet通过固定离散化上的点值编码输入函数，受限于离散化分辨率且缺乏可移植性。作者旨在利用一般局部凸空间（由半范数族而非单一范数生成拓扑）的连续对偶中的线性泛函替代点采样，从而突破范数空间的限制，实现更紧凑、可解释、离散化无关的输入函数编码。

Method: 1) 在Ismailov的拓扑DeepONet框架上，用连续线性泛函（取自Hausdorff局部凸空间V的对偶V'）替代点采样，构建固定与自适应两种功能测量系统；2) 测量与Lee-Shin系数空间两步法结合，训练阶段仅用解码器和正则化稳定自适应坐标；3) 推导分离测量误差、输出基底误差和神经逼近误差的离散误差分解，并给出Barron率细化。

Result: 异构Darcy流问题中功能模型在未见网格上保持近分辨率无关误差5.5-5.6%；受控问题中自适应测量将平均误差降至1.2%以下；固定时间Navier-Stokes问题中自适应拓扑DeepONet达到1.685%±0.017%的平均相对L2误差（128个功能坐标），优于其他DeepONet模型；可比规模的FNO取得更低的0.832%±0.172%误差，但需完整64×64输入场、两倍训练时间和10.7倍峰值GPU内存。

Conclusion: 所提框架在非范数局部凸输入空间上提供紧凑、可解释且离散化可移植的V'中坐标，兼具精度与计算效率的优势，在多种算子学习任务（含非范数空间和受控问题）中展现出鲁棒性和通用性。

Abstract: Deep Operator Networks (DeepONets; arXiv:1910.03193) typically encode an input function through point values on a fixed discretization. Building on the Topological DeepONet framework of Ismailov (arXiv:2603.11972), we replace point samples by continuous linear functionals drawn from the continuous dual of a Hausdorff locally convex space $({V},\{p_α\}_{α\in A})$, whose topology is generated by a point-separating family of seminorms rather than a single norm, and develop fixed and adaptive functional measurement systems. Measurements are combined with the coefficient-space Two-Step procedure of Lee and Shin (arXiv:2309.01020), while a training-only decoder and regularization stabilize the adaptive coordinates. We derive a discrete error decomposition separating measurement, output-basis, and neural-approximation errors, together with a Barron-rate refinement. The framework is evaluated on the antiderivative operator, a non-normable locally convex input space, heterogeneous Darcy flow, a controlled operator, and fixed-time and time-evolving Navier-Stokes vorticity operators. In the heterogeneous Darcy problem, the functional models retain nearly resolution-independent errors of 5.5-5.6% on unseen grids, while in the controlled problem adaptive measurements reduce the mean error below 1.2%. For the fixed-time Navier-Stokes problem, the Adaptive Topological DeepONet is the most accurate DeepONet-based model, attaining a mean relative $L^2$ error of 1.685% +/- 0.017% using 128 functional coordinates. A comparably sized Fourier neural operator (FNO; arXiv:2010.08895) achieves the lower error 0.832% +/- 0.172%, but requires the full 64x64 input field, twice the training time, and 10.7x greater peak GPU memory. The formulation provides compact, interpretable, and discretization-portable coordinates in the continuous dual $V'$, including for non-normable input spaces.

</details>


### [219] [MiGHT-EHR: A Multi-task Graph Transformer for Heterogeneous Temporal Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06430)
*Anirudh Rayas,Yuan Wang,Pavan Turaga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MiGHT-EHR，一种多任务图Transformer框架，通过对异构时序EHR数据进行统一表示学习，同时建模异构临床实体及其交互、纵向患者轨迹以及跨任务统计依赖，在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上四项临床预测任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHR数据学习面临三大挑战：包含患者、就诊、诊断、处方、手术等异构临床实体及其交互；存在跨多次医院就诊的纵向患者轨迹；不同临床预测任务间存在共享统计依赖。现有方法仅捕捉其中部分性质，缺乏统一建模框架。

Method: MiGHT-EHR将EHR构建为异构图，节点代表临床实体，边通过归一化点互信息（NPMI）连接统计相关的实体；采用多任务图Transformer在同一表示学习框架下同时建模异构交互、时间序列轨迹和多任务共享依赖。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV数据集上，MiGHT-EHR在药物推荐、住院时长预测、死亡率和再入院预测四项任务的平均性能上超越SOTA方法，尤其在死亡率和再入院预测上提升显著；事后分析显示患者邻域按临床结局组织，医学概念可作为表示空间中的线性方向恢复，且任务概率校准良好。

Conclusion: MiGHT-EHR的表示学习能够同时支持多样化的预测任务，同时保留临床可解释的结构，验证了统一异构时序图建模的有效性。

Abstract: Learning from Electronic Health Records (EHRs) has gained significant attention due to its potential to improve clinical prediction. However, effective learning remains challenging because EHRs encode heterogeneous, temporally ordered clinical interactions. In particular, EHRs contain: (i) heterogeneous clinical entities, including patients, visits, diagnoses, prescriptions, and procedures, together with their heterogeneous interactions, (ii) longitudinal patient trajectories across hospital visits and (iii) shared statistical dependencies across related clinical prediction tasks. Existing EHR learning methods capture only a subset of these properties. To bridge this gap, we propose Multi-task Graph transformer for Heterogeneous Temporal EHRs (MiGHT-EHR), which jointly models all three within a unified representation learning method. MiGHT-EHR constructs a heterogeneous graph from EHRs in which nodes represent clinical entities and edges connect statistically associated entities identified via normalized point-wise mutual information. Across MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV datasets, MiGHT-EHR outperforms state-of-the-art methods on average across four tasks: drug recommendation, prediction of length-of-stay, mortality, and readmission, with particularly strong improvements in mortality and readmission prediction. Furthermore, a post-hoc analysis of the learned representations reveals that patient neighborhoods are organized by clinical outcomes, salient medical concepts are recoverable as linear directions in the representation space, and task probabilities are well calibrated. Collectively, these findings demonstrate that MiGHT-EHR representations support diverse prediction tasks while preserving clinically interpretable structure.

</details>


### [220] [SNI-GNN: SmartNIC-Assisted Full-Graph GNN Training with In-Network Embedding Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06441)
*Guofan Yu,Sitian Chen,Zhenheng Tang,Xiaowen Chu,Amelie Chi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SNI-GNN是一个基于SmartNIC辅助的全图GNN训练系统，通过在网络内预测远程嵌入来减少通信开销，同时保持训练精度。该系统在NVIDIA BlueField-3上实现，可将通信减少21-45%，实现1.3-3.6倍于BNS-GCN和最高1.29倍于SANCUS的端到端加速，精度损失不超过0.01，可扩展到16个GPU、数千万条边的图规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全图GNN训练虽然精度高，但在多服务器集群上由于节点间沉重且不规则的嵌入交换导致扩展性差，通信成为主要瓶颈。

Method: SNI-GNN包含三个核心组件：(1)在SmartNIC上部署轻量级线性趋势预测器，利用缓存的历史嵌入进行在线修正，在网络内预测远程嵌入；(2)基于重要性的边界节点采样策略；(3)异步DPU-GPU数据流水线结合中间结果复用。同时提供误差和收敛界分析，证明在有限二阶动态下预测器偏差可控，并能保证标准非凸收敛。

Result: 在NVIDIA BlueField-3上实现，与现有全图训练系统集成，通信减少21-45%，端到端加速1.3-3.6倍（对比BNS-GCN）和最高1.29倍（对比SANCUS），精度损失不超过0.01，在数千万条边的图上可高效扩展到16个GPU。

Conclusion: 基于SmartNIC的网络内预测是划分和压缩技术之外的一种实用补充方案，可用于大规模通信高效的全图GNN训练。

Abstract: Full-graph GNN training delivers high accuracy but scales poorly on multi-server clusters due to heavy, irregular inter-node embedding exchanges. We present SNI-GNN, a SmartNIC-assisted full-graph training system that reduces communication while preserving accuracy by predicting remote embeddings in-network. SNI-GNN deploys a lightweight linear-trend predictor on SmartNICs to refine cached historical embeddings, coupled with an importance-based boundary-node sampling policy and an asynchronous DPU--GPU data pipeline with intermediate-result reuse. We provide error and convergence bounds showing that predictor bias remains controlled under bounded second-order dynamics and yields standard non-convex convergence with inexact gradients. Implemented on NVIDIA BlueField-3, SNI-GNN integrates with state-of-the-art full-graph systems, cuts communication by 21--45\%, achieves 1.3--3.6$\times$ end-to-end speedups over BNS-GCN and up to 1.29$\times$ over baseline SANCUS, with accuracy loss $\leq 0.01$, and scales efficiently to 16 GPUs on graphs with up to tens of millions of edges. These results indicate SmartNIC-based in-network prediction is a practical complement to partitioning and compression techniques for communication-efficient full-graph GNN training at scale.

</details>


### [221] [ED-CSP: Crystal Structure Prediction from Electron Diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06448)
*Germain Poloudenny,Yaël Frégier,Arnaud Demortière*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ED-CSP，一种从稀疏、未索引的电子衍射（ED）观测中预测周期3D晶体结构的机器学习框架。ED-CSP结合关系集合编码器、排列不变多视角聚合和周期流生成器，联合预测晶格参数和分数原子坐标。模型在自主构建的485万模拟多视角ED晶体结构数据集（ED-CS）上训练，在CHILI-100K基准上达到57.49% MR@5，通过百万级结构预训练可提升至66.27%，并验证了真实生成能力（非公式检索）及对输入衍射模式的依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于电子衍射的学习方法主要局限于预测晶体学标签、从索引反射重建结构或从有限结构库中检索候选，无法直接从稀疏、未索引的ED观测中生成完整晶体结构。这是一个具有挑战性的生成式逆问题，需要新的方法框架。

Method: ED-CSP框架包含三个核心组件：关系集合编码器（relational set encoder）处理ED观测、排列不变多视角聚合（permutation-invariant multi-view aggregation）融合多个探测器平面的衍射信息、周期流生成器（periodic flow generator）联合预测晶格参数和分数原子坐标。训练数据ED-CS包含485万模拟多视角ED晶体结构，来自七个材料仓库去重并排除CHILI-100K重叠。训练分两阶段：先在百万级结构上进行预训练，再在更大数据集上训练。

Result: 在2,075个保留的CHILI-100K材料上，仅用CHILI训练的ED-CSP达到57.49% MR@5，超过基于粉末X射线衍射的SOTA模型PXRDGen（52.92%）。百万级预训练初始化将MR@5提升至66.27%。在训练检索库中不存在的1,024种成分上仍达到53.52% MR@5，证明真正的生成能力。将目标ED替换为同成分但非同构结构的衍射后MR@5下降22.09个百分点，确认预测依赖于输入衍射图样而非仅成分信息。

Conclusion: ED-CSP和ED-CS首次建立了从稀疏ED观测进行生成式晶体结构预测的基准，展示了超越现有基于XRD方法的性能，并为未来迁移到实验数据奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recovering a periodic 3D crystal structure from sparse, unindexed electron diffraction (ED) observations is a challenging generative inverse problem. Existing ED-based learning methods mainly predict crystallographic labels, reconstruct structures from indexed reflections, or retrieve candidates from finite structure libraries. Here, we introduce ED-CSP, a machine learning framework that predicts crystal structures from chemical composition, atom count, and multiple detector-plane ED spot sets. ED-CSP combines a relational set encoder, permutation-invariant multi-view aggregation, and a periodic flow generator to jointly predict lattice parameters and fractional atomic coordinates.
  To train the model, we construct ED-CS, a dataset of 4.85 million simulated multi-view ED crystal structures, deduplicated across seven materials repositories and filtered to exclude CHILI-100K overlaps. On 2,075 held-out CHILI-100K materials, ED-CSP trained only on CHILI achieves a structural match rate of 57.49% MR@5, outperforming PXRDGen (52.92%), a state-of-the-art crystal structure prediction model conditioned on powder X-ray diffraction. Scaling training data further improves performance: initializing from a one-million-structure precursor raises MR@5 to 66.27%. On 1,024 compositions absent from the training retrieval library, the model still achieves 53.52% MR@5, demonstrating true generative capability beyond exact-formula retrieval. Replacing target ED observations with diffraction from non-isomorphic structures of identical composition decreases MR@5 by 22.09 percentage points, confirming that predictions depend on the input diffraction patterns rather than composition alone. ED-CSP and ED-CS establish a benchmark for generative crystal structure prediction from sparse ED observations and provide a foundation for future transfer to experimental data.

</details>


### [222] [Beyond Attention: Signed Integrated Gradients Attribution in a BiomeGPT-Style Microbiome Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06486)
*Oren Nelson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对基于特征token化的Transformer模型（如BiomeGPT）提出了一种有符号的、融合感知的属性归因方法，通过源派生基线T'=S+A_0结合积分梯度（Integrated Gradients）区分疾病支持与健康支持的微生物信号，并推荐二阶积分Hessian以揭示微生物群落交互规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对特征token化Transformer的可解释性工作依赖[CLS]注意力权重，但存在两个关键缺陷：(1) 注意力权重非负，无法区分疾病支持性证据与健康支持性证据；(2) 注意力作用于token融合之后，掩盖了输入源S（物种身份）与A（丰度）各自对输出的影响。因此需要一种有符号且融合感知的属性归因方法。

Method: 使用积分梯度（Integrated Gradients）作为有符号的归因方法，并提出源派生基线T'=S+A_0：保持物种身份S作为固定生物坐标，将丰度替换为基线丰度A_0，从而隔离丰度变化的影响。将该方法应用于疾病-健康决策边界，获得能显式分离致病与保护性微生物信号的极性。进一步推荐二阶积分Hessian以揭示微生物群落交互规则，即某一成员的扰动如何改变模型对另一成员的敏感性。

Result: 梯度方法揭示了物种-丰度方向性关系以及完全被无符号[CLS]注意力权重掩盖的敏感性诊断；二阶积分Hessian可暴露哪些物种在特定丰度下驱动模糊案例走向疾病或健康，以及跨物种的敏感性交互效应。

Conclusion: 该方法为BiomeGPT提供了原则性的可解释性框架，并可推广至其他平滑可微的特征token化Transformer。代码已开源。

Abstract: In a feature-tokenized transformer (arXiv:2106.11959) such as BiomeGPT (doi:10.64898/2026.01.05.697599), each input token is built by fusing a fixed identity with a sample-specific measurement: a fixed species and a variable abundance, T = S + A. To interpret downstream classification in such models, prior work inspects the attention weights of the special [CLS] token (arXiv:2106.11959, arXiv:1810.04805, BiomeGPT) to rank sample tokens by importance. These weights have two critical limitations: they are nonnegative, so they cannot separate disease-supporting from health-supporting evidence (arXiv:2201.12114), and they act after token fusion, obscuring how the input sources S and A each affect the output.
  To address this we use Integrated Gradients (arXiv:1703.01365), a signed, fusion-aware attribution method, and propose a source-derived baseline T' = S + A_0 for feature-tokenized models such as BiomeGPT, which preserves species identity as a fixed biological coordinate while isolating the effect of abundance variation. Applied to a disease-versus-health decision margin, it yields polarity that explicitly separates pathogenic from protective microbial signals. We show that this gradient-based approach uncovers species-abundance directional relationships and sensitivity diagnostics entirely obscured by unsigned [CLS] attention weights. We further recommend second-order Integrated Hessians (arXiv:2002.04138) to expose microbiome community interaction rules: how a perturbation in one member alters the model's sensitivity to another, and which other species drive ambiguous cases toward disease or health at a given abundance level. This provides a principled approach to explainability in BiomeGPT that generalizes to other smooth and differentiable feature-tokenized transformers. Code is available at https://github.com/nohren/token-source-attribution

</details>


### [223] [Toward Reliable Context Compression for Long-Horizon Agents: An Empirical Study of Execution Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06503)
*Guanghui Min,Liang Wu,Mayank Darbari,Chen Chen,Liangjie Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE，一个验证器引导的框架，通过成对的闭环续写评估上下文压缩事件，并利用摘要偏好优化自然语言压缩提示，以提升长时程智能体的可靠性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环上下文压缩虽能控制长时程智能体的上下文增长，但其行为影响尚不清楚。初步实证发现压缩会削弱近期交互的影响，导致阻塞行为增加、重复探索增多以及跨运行不稳定，亟需更可靠的压缩方法。

Method: TRACE框架通过验证器引导的方式，对每个压缩事件在相同环境状态下进行成对的闭环续写评估，并使用摘要偏好来优化自然语言压缩提示，同时保持所有模型冻结（不进行微调）。

Result: 在AppWorld基准上，TRACE在任务性能、多次运行可靠性以及上下文-执行效率方面均优于现有压缩基线方法。

Conclusion: 这些发现为"边界局域评估"（boundary-local evaluation）作为可靠智能体上下文压缩的有前景方向提供了早期证据。

Abstract: Recurrent context compression controls context growth in long-horizon agents, but its behavioral effects remain poorly understood. In this preliminary empirical study, we show that compression can weaken the influence of recent interactions, increasing blocked actions, repeated exploration, and instability across runs. Motivated by these observations, we introduce TRACE, a verifier-guided framework that evaluates individual compaction events through paired closed-loop continuations from the same environment state and uses summary preferences to optimize a natural-language compression prompt while keeping all models frozen. Initial results on AppWorld show improvements over existing compression baselines in task performance, multi-run reliability, and context--execution efficiency. These findings provide early evidence for boundary-local evaluation as a promising direction for reliable agent context compression.

</details>


### [224] [Unmasking Removal-Budget Confounding: A Matched Operating-Point Evaluation Framework for Adaptive Data Cleaning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06511)
*Wei-Hsiang Chen,Pin-Hsuan Yu,Chen-Hsuan Fang,Jung-Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种操作点感知（operating-point-aware）的评估框架，用于消除自适应数据清洗方法中由移除预算混淆（removal-budget confounding）导致的评估偏差。通过在匹配预算和匹配召回率的控制下比较方法，并辅以AUROC和AUPRC等阈值无关指标，作者证明了在一个多线索自适应清洗器重设计中观察到的性能提升在操作点对齐后基本消失，表明多数表观增益实为评估偏差而非真正的腐败判别能力提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应数据清洗方法用数据驱动的分区替代人工过滤阈值，但改变分区粒度或分组数量会隐式移动决策边界，改变移除样本总数，从而引入移除预算混淆偏差。这种偏差使精度或假阳性率等指标的表观提升误导性地归因于更好的腐败判别能力，而实际仅是移除预算变小所致，亟需一种能消除这种评估偏差的框架。

Method: 提出操作点感知评估框架，包括：（1）匹配预算（matched-budget）和匹配召回率（matched-recall）对照；（2）引入AUROC和AUPRC等阈值无关指标；（3）采用假阳性分解（false-positive decomposition）对清洗误差来源进行细粒度分析。实验对象为多线索自适应清洗器重设计，融合了加权的学习难度线索、辅助欧氏距离线索及更细的分区粒度，用于隔离干净但难学的样本。在CIFAR-10和ImageNet-100上对比朴素评估与操作点匹配评估。

Result: 朴素评估（各配置在自身诱导操作点下）显示重设计有显著性能提升，但操作点对齐后这些增益大多消失。假阳性分解显示：干净但难学的样本在低腐败率下主导错误计数，在中等腐败率下表现为阈值依赖，在严重腐败下贡献可忽略。在CIFAR-10和ImageNet-100上的实验证明，低至中等腐败率下多数朴素评估的性能差异在操作点匹配后缩小或消失；仅特定低患病率场景和严重腐败下的高召回区域保留真实的排序优势。

Conclusion: 自适应清洗方法必须在匹配操作点下进行基准测试，才能确保性能增益反映真实的腐败判别能力而非移除预算效应。该工作揭示了一个普遍的评估陷阱，为自适应数据清洗领域提供了更可靠的评估方法论。

Abstract: Adaptive data-cleaning methods replace manual filtering thresholds with data-driven partitions. However, changing the partition granularity, the number of groups used to segment samples by estimated corruption risk, can implicitly shift the decision boundary and alter the overall number of removed samples. This creates a bias known as removal-budget confounding, where apparent gains in metrics like precision or false-positive rate reflect a smaller removal budget rather than superior corruption discrimination. To address this evaluation bias, we introduce an operating-point-aware evaluation framework that evaluates methods using matched-budget and matched-recall controls alongside threshold-independent metrics (AUROC and AUPRC). We test this framework on a multi-cue adaptive cleaner redesign featuring a reweighted learning-difficulty cue, an auxiliary Euclidean-distance cue, and increased partition granularity intended to isolate clean-but-difficult samples. While naive evaluations (assessing configurations at their own induced operating points) suggest substantial performance improvements for the redesign, these gains disappear once operating points are equalized. False-positive decomposition reveals that clean-but-difficult samples primarily drive error counts at low corruption rates, become threshold-dependent at moderate corruption, and contribute negligibly under severe corruption. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-100 demonstrate that most performance differences observed in naive evaluation shrink or vanish at low-to-moderate corruption when operating points are matched. True ranking advantages only remain in specific low-prevalence settings and in high-recall regions under severe corruption. These findings highlight that adaptive cleaning methods must be benchmarked at matched operating points to ensure performance gains reflect genuine corruption discrimination.

</details>


### [225] [Target-Weighted Neyman Allocation: Experimental Design for Heterogeneous Treatment Effects under Population Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06512)
*Hoang Dang,Luan Pham,Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TWNA是一种两阶段分层实验设计，通过结合部署比例的重要性和组-臂方差估计的统计难度来分配最终样本量和处理概率，从而优化目标加权组平均处理效应（GATE）的估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机实验通常在一个人群中运行以指导另一个人群的决策。按实验比例分配浪费预算在部署中很少出现的群体上，而按部署比例分配则对难以精确测量的群体抽样不足，两者都无法在部署重要性与统计精确性之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出TWNA（Target-Weighted Neyman Allocation），一个两阶段分层设计：第一阶段使用试点数据估计组-臂结果方差，第二阶段据此分配最终样本量和处理概率，以优化目标加权GATE的精度。oracle规则具有闭式解，plug-in规则在试点方差估计稳定后恢复oracle表现。此外扩展到处理部署组成不确定性的情形，并区分权重稳健性与针对偏斜、稀有事件或受污染结果的试点稳健变体。

Result: 模拟实验和真实协变量基准测试表明，当群体既在部署中重要又难以测量时，TWNA带来的精度提升最大。

Conclusion: TWNA通过在部署重要性与测量难度之间取得平衡，为跨群体泛化的随机实验提供了更有效的样本分配策略，并在部署组成不确定的情况下仍保持稳健性。

Abstract: Randomized experiments are often run in one population to guide decisions in another. Allocating by experimental proportions wastes budget on groups that rarely appear in deployment, whereas allocating by deployment proportions under-samples groups that are hard to measure precisely. We propose \textbf{TWNA} (Target-Weighted Neyman Allocation), a two-stage stratified design that uses pilot estimates of group--arm outcome variances to allocate final-stage sample sizes and treatment probabilities for target-weighted group average treatment effect (GATE) precision. The oracle rule has a closed form and balances deployment importance with statistical difficulty; the plug-in rule recovers it as pilot variance estimates stabilize. We also extend TWNA to handle uncertainty about deployment composition, remaining robust whether the target mix is roughly known or entirely unknown. Finally, we distinguish this weight robustness from a pilot-robust variant for skewed, rare-event, or contaminated outcomes. Simulations and real-covariate benchmarks show the largest gains when groups are both deployment-important and difficult to measure.

</details>


### [226] [CertBind from Multimodal Connectivity to Certifiable Retrieval Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06516)
*Shuheng Cao,Zhenhao Zhang,Ruiqi Chen,Renjie Cao,Weijia Zhang,Siyu Zhang,Jiaxin Liu,Xiangyu Zeng,Haotian Geng,Fan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CertBind提出了一种面向冻结多模态编码器组合图的多尺度可认证组合理论，在节点、边、路径和查询四个尺度上分别建立任务识别边界、图级误差控制、有限样本恢复半径和top-k候选集证明，从而将多模态可组合性从表示层面延伸到可认证的任务决策层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级连接器使冻结的多模态编码器能在表示层面组合，但部署时在任务决策层面暴露了新的问题——连接路由在扩展跨模态能力的同时可能改变原有的原生检索能力，缺乏对组合结果可靠性的形式化保证。

Method: CertBind采用多尺度分析框架：(1) 节点尺度上，原生锚点建立精确的任务识别边界；(2) 边尺度上，契约感知的保形秩提供图级族误差控制；(3) 路径尺度上，重叠感知的预算与干净校准在声明条件下得到有限样本恢复半径；(4) 查询尺度上，该半径生成被覆盖的top-k候选集，当集合大小等于k时成为点证书。系统将支持的路由标记为Direct，仅将标记路由送入恢复流程，决定性恢复返回Certified，未解决查询返回Abstain。

Result: 在C-MCR共享路由上，原生CLIP的R@1从0.524下降到0.290（即组合确实影响原生检索能力）；而生产环境回退方案恢复率达到0.963±0.002的干净检索性能，通过分支记录的无害值为1.000，证明CertBind能有效恢复原生的检索能力并保证无损害。

Conclusion: CertBind将多模态可组合性从连接表示扩展到了可认证的任务决策层面，为部署中的组合路由提供了系统化的认证保证机制。

Abstract: Lightweight connectors make frozen multimodal encoders composable at the representation level. Deployment exposes a second problem at the level of task decisions.
