Motivation: 深度学习的成功高度依赖高质量标注数据，但在实际应用中这一条件常难以满足。弱监督学习旨在利用不完整、不精确或不准确的监督信息训练准确模型，现有方法在大数生成过程的假设上过于严格，且缺乏公平的评估标准，亟需更实用、更符合真实场景的解决方案。

Method: 本文从三个维度展开：(1) 提出置信度差异分类这一新的弱监督二分类问题，并设计一致性（consistent）求解方法；(2) 针对互补标签学习，提出基于比现有方法更宽松的数据生成过程假设的一致性方法来解决多分类问题；(3) 为部分标签学习构建评估框架，以促进该领域算法公平且贴近实际的评测。

Result: 在置信度差异分类、互补标签学习以及部分标签学习三个弱监督学习方向上都取得了进展：提出了新的问题设置和一致性的求解算法，放宽了数据假设，并建立了更公平的评估体系。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入新的监督范式、放宽假设条件以及建立公平评估框架，系统推进了弱监督学习在真实场景中的实用性和理论一致性，为该领域提供了多角度的研究贡献。

Abstract: Deep learning has achieved great success in recent years thanks to the availability of high-quality, well-annotated training data. However, this requirement is often not met in real-world applications. Weakly supervised learning aims to train an accurate model with incomplete, inexact, or inaccurate supervision. In this chapter, we will discuss recent advances in this field, including new supervision paradigms, relaxed assumptions, and practical solutions. First, we introduce a new weakly supervised binary classification problem called confidence-difference classification and propose consistent approaches to solve it. Next, we investigate complementary-label learning, a weakly supervised multi-class classification problem. Our proposed approaches are based on more relaxed assumptions about the data generation process than existing consistent approaches. Lastly, we present an evaluation framework for partial-label learning, another popular multi-class weakly supervised learning problem, in order to promote fair and realistic evaluation of algorithms in this field.

</details>


### [260] [MiCoPro: End-to-End Mixed Precision HW/SW Co-design with HW-aware Proxy Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06916)
*Zijun Jiang,Yangdi Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MiCo框架，一个面向边缘AI的层间混合精度量化（MPQ）探索与部署框架，并扩展为MiCoPro，引入硬件感知代理（HAP）模型以增强预测精度和硬件通用性。该框架能直接从PyTorch模型部署到裸机C代码，在BitFusion加速器和SIMD扩展RISC-V处理器上实现高达40%的延迟降低，精度损失小于3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MPQ方案探索算法在灵活性和效率上受限，难以理解不同MPQ方案对后训练量化和量化感知训练的复杂影响，且缺乏端到端的MPQ模型优化与部署框架。

Method: 提出MiCo框架，采用新颖的优化算法在严格延迟约束下搜索精度最优的量化配置；扩展为MiCoPro，引入鲁棒的硬件感知代理（HAP）模型以提升预测精度和硬件通用性，通过目标特定的延迟建模实现从PyTorch模型到裸机C代码的快速探索和直接部署。

Result: 在BitFusion加速器和SIMD扩展的RISC-V处理器上验证了框架的通用性，实现高达40%的延迟降低，精度下降小于3%。

Conclusion: MiCo/MiCoPro框架提供了一个整体的端到端MPQ探索与部署解决方案，兼顾精度与硬件性能，弥补了现有工作在部署层面的缺失。

Abstract: Quantized Neural Networks~(QNN) with low-bitwidth data have proven promising in efficient storage and computation on edge devices. To mitigate accuracy degradation while maximizing speedup, layer-wise mixed-precision quantization~(MPQ) becomes a popular solution. However, existing algorithms for exploring MPQ schemes are limited in flexibility and efficiency. Comprehending the complex impacts of different MPQ schemes on post-training quantization and quantization-aware training results is a challenge for conventional methods. Furthermore, an end-to-end framework for the optimization and deployment of MPQ models is missing in existing work.
  To address these challenges, we propose the MiCo framework, a holistic MPQ exploration and deployment framework for edge AI applications. The framework adopts a novel optimization algorithm to search for accuracy-optimal quantization configurations under strict latency constraints. We further extended the framework to MiCoPro, which introduces a robust Hardware-Aware Proxy (HAP) model to enhance prediction accuracy and hardware versatility. By leveraging target-specific latency modeling, MiCoPro enables rapid exploration and direct deployment from PyTorch models to bare-metal C code. We demonstrate the versatility of our framework on both the BitFusion accelerator and SIMD-extended RISC-V processors, achieving up to 40\% of latency reduction with less than 3\% of accuracy drop.

</details>


### [261] [Walkable to Whom? Capturing Subjective Variability in Walkability Perception Using Multimodal Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06934)
*Moloud Damandeh,Meead Saberi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个用户条件多模态深度学习框架用于人行道可步行性感知预测，基于包含29,870条可步行性评分（来自1,196名受访者）的澳大利亚数据集。研究发现人行道视角图像比匹配的街景图像获得显著更高的可步行性评分，且用户条件模型相较于纯图像基线模型提升了65%的排序一致性（二次加权kappa从0.29提升至0.47），证明评估者身份特征对可步行性感知具有独立于图像内容的预测价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可步行性研究存在两大缺陷：一是将多样化的个体感知简化为聚合分数，隐含假设所有人感知一致；二是常用车载街景图像，无法反映行人的真实视觉体验。作者旨在解决这些局限，构建能表征不同用户感知差异的模型。

Method: 构建包含29,870条可步行性评分的数据集（1,196名受访者），结合人行道视角图像与个体评分者属性；提出首个用户条件多模态深度学习框架，融合视觉特征与受访者级表征；并通过视角对比研究比较人行道视角与街景图像的评分差异。

Result: 人行道视角图像获得的评分显著高于匹配的街景图像；用户条件模型在二次加权kappa上达到0.47，较纯图像基线（0.29）提升65%，表明用户信息对可步行性预测具有显著增益。

Conclusion: 研究支持从聚合的、观察者独立的可步行性评分向表征不同用户群体的模型转变，从而实现对行人环境更具包容性的评估，并强调图像来源（视角）是感知调查中的一个实质性设计决策。

Abstract: Visual perception of walkability varies substantially across individuals, reflecting differences in personal characteristics, experiences, and preferences. Existing studies, however, often reduce these diverse judgements to aggregated scores, implicitly assuming uniform perception, and commonly rely on vehicle-mounted street-view imagery that does not reflect the pedestrian's visual experience. This paper introduces a dataset of 29,870 walkability ratings from 1,196 respondents, linking sidewalk-view imagery across urban, suburban, and regional Australian environments with individual rater attributes, and proposes the first user-conditioned multimodal deep learning framework for walkability perception, fusing visual features with respondent-level representations. A viewpoint-comparison study shows that sidewalk-view images receive significantly higher walkability ratings than matched street-view images, indicating that imagery source is a substantive design decision in perception surveys. The user-conditioned model improves rank agreement with observed ratings by 65% over an image-only baseline (quadratic weighted kappa 0.47 vs. 0.29), demonstrating that who is evaluating an environment carries predictive indication beyond image content alone. These findings support moving from aggregated, observer-independent walkability scores toward models that represent diverse users, enabling more inclusive assessment of pedestrian environments.

</details>


### [262] [ELMZip: Onboard Satellite Image Compression via Extreme Learning Machines for Efficient Downlink](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06942)
*Woojin Cho,Junghwan Park,Sangcheol Sim,Steve Andreas Immanuel,Junhyuk Heo,Darongsae Kwon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ELMZip框架，基于极限学习机（ELM）和域分解策略，用于小卫星（如CubeSats）多光谱图像的高效、免分辨率（resolution-free）星上神经表示压缩。ELMZip将拟合过程表述为凸最小二乘问题，仅需传输紧凑的输出权重，相比传统神经表示方法显著降低了下行链路负载，同时保持高重建保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小卫星多光谱遥感图像数据量大、通信窗口受限，下行传输面临严重瓶颈。传统压缩方法难以适应多波段、多分辨率数据的非线性统计特性，而现有神经表示方法依赖迭代优化且需传输完整网络参数，计算开销和传输负载过高，不适合资源受限的星上平台。

Method: ELMZip结合极限学习机（ELM）与域分解策略：使用随机特征单层网络将拟合过程转化为凸最小二乘问题，无需反向传播；采用非对称传输协议，仅发送紧凑的输出权重而非完整网络参数，实现免分辨率（resolution-free）的星上神经表示与压缩。

Result: ELMZip在保持高重建保真度的同时显著提升了压缩效率，大幅降低了下行传输负载，使资源受限平台能够最大化数据回传量，并支持即时图像重建用于分析。

Conclusion: ELMZip为小卫星多光谱图像压缩提供了一种高效、免分辨率的神经表示方案，克服了传统方法对多波段非线性统计适应不足和计算成本高的问题，推动了实时AI驱动的对地观测应用的发展。

Abstract: The acquisition of multispectral imagery via small satellites (e.g., CubeSats) presents significant data downlink challenges due to high data volumes and restricted communication windows. While onboard image compression is critical to address this bottleneck, traditional methods often struggle to adapt to the nonlinear statistics of multi-band, multi-resolution data. To overcome these limitations, we propose ELMZip, a novel framework based on Extreme Learning Machines (ELM) and domain decomposition strategies for efficient, resolution-free onboard neural representation. ELMZip formulates the fitting process as a convex least-squares problem using random-feature single-layer networks, thereby eliminating the need for computationally expensive backpropagation. By adopting an asymmetric transmission protocol that sends only the compact output weights, the proposed method significantly reduces the downlink payload. Unlike previous neural representation approaches that rely on iterative optimization and require transmitting full network parameters, ELMZip achieves significant compression efficiency while maintaining high reconstruction fidelity. This capability enables immediate image reconstruction for analysis, allowing resource-constrained platforms to maximize data return and advancing real-time AI-powered Earth observation.

</details>


### [263] [A Rate Separation for Agnostic Direct Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06951)
*Mihir More,Aritra Das,Debayan Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究回答了Hanneke, Moran和Waknine提出的问题：agnostic PAC学习中直接和C^r的学习曲线如何依赖于单实例学习曲线和r。研究发现单实例学习速率并不能决定直接和的学习速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究agnostic PAC学习框架下，直接和（direct sum）的学习曲线与单实例学习曲线之间的关系，验证单实例学习速率是否足以决定直接和的速率。

Method: 通过构造两个具体的函数类F和G进行对比分析。F由两个常量二元函数组成，G由零函数和恒等函数组成。两个类的agnostic学习曲线阶数均为n^{-1/2}，通过比较它们的直接和C^r的学习速率来揭示差别。

Result: 证明了单实例学习速率不能决定直接和的学习速率，即使用具有相同单实例学习速率的两个类，其直接和的学习行为也可能存在本质差异。

Conclusion: 直接和学习速率的确定需要更多信息，而不仅仅是单实例学习曲线本身。

Abstract: Hanneke, Moran, and Waknine \cite{HannekeMoranWaknine2024} asked how the agnostic PAC learning curve of the direct sum $C^r$ depends on the single-instance learning curve $\epsagn(n\mid C)$ and on $r$. We show that the single-instance learning rate does not determine the direct-sum rate. Let $\F$ be the class of the two constant binary functions and let $\G$ consist of the zero function and the identity function. Both classes have agnostic learning curve of order $n^{-1/2}$.

</details>


### [264] [How Molecular Generative Models Organize Molecular Identity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06956)
*Raul Ortega-Ochoa,Tejs Vegge,Jens S. Bakander,Luis Mantilla Calderon,Alan Aspuru-Guzik,Tonio Buonassisi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了分子生成模型内部如何组织离散化学身份（分子结构），通过将分子身份映射拉回生成过程的隐空间，揭示模型内部的固定分区结构——即哪些分子（新颖或已知）可以被生成。在三种分子生成架构中发现，这种内部组织呈现分段常数区域，由粗细层次递进的边界分隔，且组织方式取决于多种因素。训练过程中局部化学组织趋于稳定，但邻域内不同分子身份的数量持续变化。因此，生成空间能否被视为化学可导航的，必须先经过内部组织的表征而非假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将生成模型视为输出表示的采样器，并利用其隐空间作为化学空间导航的代理，但对模型内部如何排布离散化学身份（单个分子）的机制知之甚少。本文旨在通过让分子身份明确化并将其拉回生成过程，来理解模型内部的组织结构。

Method: 将分子身份显式化，通过生成过程的"拉回"（pullback）操作，探测生成同一对象的隐空间区域，从而揭示训练模型的内部"曲目库"（repertoire）：一个固定分区，决定了模型能够产生的所有对象（无论新颖与否）。在三种分子生成架构上进行分析，考察隐空间中分段常数区域的排列方式及其边界结构。

Result: 发现隐空间被组织为分段常数区域，由重复出现的粗细递进（coarse-to-fine）边界分隔。该组织的具体形态取决于所探测的表示、身份约定、解码器随机性及坐标比较所用的度量。训练中，局部化学组织趋于稳定，但每个邻域内可表征的不同分子身份数量持续变化。

Conclusion: 必须在将生成空间视为化学可导航之前，对其内部组织进行显式表征而非假设。这一内部结构是模型行为的基础，决定了模型在化学空间中的覆盖范围与导航能力。

Abstract: Generative models for matter are often evaluated as samplers over output representations, and their latent spaces are commonly used as proxies for navigating chemical space. Much less is known about how these models internally arrange discrete chemical identities within those representations. We study this arrangement by making molecular identity explicit and pulling it back through the generative process. Through these pullbacks we probe the regions that generate the same object, exposing the trained model's internal repertoire: a fixed partition that determines which objects (novel or not) the model can produce.
  Across three molecular generative architectures, we find that this repertoire is arranged into piecewise-constant regions separated by recurring coarse-to-fine boundaries. Its organization depends on the representation probed, the identity convention, decoder stochasticity, and the metric used to compare coordinates. During training, local chemical organization stabilizes while the number of distinct molecular identities represented within each neighborhood continues to change. Internal organization must therefore be characterized, rather than assumed, before a generative space can be treated as chemically navigable.

</details>


### [265] [Density-aware Hierarchical Clustering Based on Element-Categorized Connection Subgraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06990)
*Yuning Yu,José Rodríguez-Piñeiro,Xuefeng Yin,Bin Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于元素分类连接子图的密度感知层次聚类方法（DHC-ECS），将层次聚类、密度聚类和图聚类有效集成，通过引入考虑距离、KNN连接子图中的元素分类、核密度估计和子簇内局部连通性的新型簇间相似度度量，在异构基准数据集上展现出优于AChameleon、RNN-DBSCAN、McDPC和G-RMS等基线方法的聚类精度和参数鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统层次聚类中簇间相似度的计算仅基于成对距离，忽视了密度变化和图结构连通性，导致聚类效果受限于数据分布特征，无法充分利用局部密度和结构信息。

Method: 提出DHC-ECS方法，融合层次聚类、密度聚类和图聚类三种代表性技术。核心创新在于：1）引入基于元素分类的新型簇间相似度度量，综合考虑距离、KNN连接子图中的元素分类、核密度估计以及子簇内局部连通性；2）利用顶点和边的对偶性捕捉图结构连通性。

Result: 在异构基准数据集上的大量实验表明，DHC-ECS在聚类精度和参数鲁棒性方面相较于AChameleon、RNN-DBSCAN、McDPC和G-RMS等基线方法表现出更优的整体性能。此外，该方法有望确定内在阈值，减少对手动参数调优的依赖。

Conclusion: DHC-ECS证明了通过结合局部密度和图结构连通性（顶点与边的对偶性），能够在低维数据集上发挥强大的聚类潜力，同时具备自动确定内在阈值、降低人工参数调优依赖的可能性。

Abstract: Clustering is a fundamental data mining technique for pattern recognition through unsupervised learning. Among various clustering methods, hierarchical clustering, density-based clustering, and graph clustering stand out as representative approaches. For hierarchical clustering, it can be categorized into agglomerative and divisive modes to construct clusters in a recursive manner. The key aspect of both modes is the calculation of inter-cluster similarity, which determines whether to merge the sub-clusters into one cluster or divide a current cluster into sub-clusters. Traditionally, the similarity is derived from pairwise distances, often overlooking density variations and structural connectivity in graphs. To address this, we propose a density-aware hierarchical clustering method based on element-categorized connection subgraphs (DHC-ECS), which effectively integrates the hierarchical clustering, density-based clustering, and graph clustering. Particularly, a novel inter-cluster similarity metric is introduced that considers not only distances but also the element categorization in the KNN connection subgraphs, kernel density estimation, and local connectivity within sub-clusters. Extensive evaluations on heterogeneous benchmark datasets demonstrate that DHC-ECS exhibits superior overall performance in terms of clustering accuracy and parameter robustness compared with the baseline methods (including AChameleon, RNN-DBSCAN, McDPC, and G-RMS). The work indicates the great potential of the proposed clustering algorithm for low-dimensional datasets by leveraging local density and graph-structured connectivity (i.e., the duality of vertices and edges), as well as the possibility to determine an intrinsic threshold, reducing the reliance on manual parameter tuning.

</details>


### [266] [Modular TTT: Rethinking Test-Time Training as Composable Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07110)
*Bohao Tang,Zhen Qin,Yuqi Pan,Zheng Li,Pengfei Liu,Ya Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Modular TTT框架，将序列建模中的测试时训练（TTT）内部学习器表示为有向无环图，将快速权重网络、损失函数、学习率、权重衰减和归一化作为显式设计维度，实现primitive-level规则的自动组合。通过系统消融发现小学习率初始化、权重衰减和单层非线性有益，而深层网络和归一化因导致激活过大会损害性能。基于此发现训练410M和1.45B参数模型，效果与Gated DeltaNet相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的TTT变体各自被单独硬编码实现，难以设计新的TTT方法，也难以隔离各组件的作用。需要一个统一的模块化框架来系统研究和比较TTT中的不同设计选择。

Method: 提出Modular TTT框架，将内部学习器表示为有向无环图（DAG），把快速权重网络、损失函数、学习率、权重衰减和归一化作为显式设计维度。框架自动将primitive级的train-view前向、train-view反向和因果query-view规则组合为完整的图级TTT计算，包括快速权重的状态转移。基于该框架对TTT各组件进行系统性消融分析。

Result: 系统性消融发现：小学习率初始化、权重衰减和单层非线性能提升性能；MSE和内积损失表现相似；更深层的快速权重网络和归一化因为导致激活过大而损害性能；残差连接和门控几乎没有可衡量的收益。基于这些发现训练的410M和1.45B参数模型在100B tokens上取得了与Gated DeltaNet相当的训练损失和基准性能。

Conclusion: Modular TTT提供了一个统一的模块化框架来设计和研究TTT方法，通过系统消融揭示了各组件对性能的实际影响，并验证了最优配置在大规模模型训练中的有效性，性能可与Gated DeltaNet相媲美。

Abstract: Test-time training (TTT) views sequence modeling as an online learning problem in which fast weights are updated by an internal learning rule. Despite the growing number of TTT variants, existing approaches typically hard-code each variant separately, which makes it difficult to design new TTT methods and to isolate the role of each component. To address this, we propose Modular TTT, a framework that represents the inner learner as a directed acyclic graph and exposes the fast-weight network, loss function, learning rate, weight decay, and normalization as explicit design dimensions. Modular TTT automatically composes primitive-level train-view forward, train-view backward, and causal query-view rules into the full graph-level TTT computation, including the fast-weight state transition. Using Modular TTT, we systematically ablate the components of TTT and find that small learning-rate initialization, weight decay, and a single-layer nonlinearity improve performance, while MSE and inner-product losses perform similarly. Deeper fast-weight networks and normalization tend to hurt performance because they induce excessively large activations, while residual connections and gating provide little measurable benefit. Guided by these findings, we train the best resulting variant as 410M- and 1.45B-parameter models on 100B tokens, and observe training loss and benchmark performance comparable to Gated DeltaNet.

</details>


### [267] [Beyond Foundation Models: Dimension-Aware Neural Architecture Search with Small-Data Representation Models for Cryocooler Lifetime Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06993)
*Gregor Molan,Grafika Jati,Francesco Barchi,Andrea Acquaviva,Aljaž Osterman,Martin Molan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FSD-RM范式，用于小数据、领域特定的遥测场景，通过容量受控表示学习和维度感知NAS，在不依赖大规模预训练的情况下实现具有竞争力的寿命预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练时间序列模型依赖丰富多样的数据，但工业和科学领域往往缺乏此类数据。因此需要一种面向有限、领域特定遥测数据的实用替代方案。

Method: 采用容量受控的表示学习方法，使用CNN1D、LSTM、GRU和Transformer等既有编码器架构，无监督训练于多元遥测数据，并集成到下游寿命预测的两阶段流水线中。通过维度感知神经网络架构搜索（NAS）联合优化模型容量和输入维度。

Result: 在低温冷却器遥测数据上的实验表明，该方法在实现具有竞争力预测性能的同时，降低了训练成本和模型复杂度。

Conclusion: 当施加适当的归纳偏置和容量控制时，无需大规模预训练也能实现有效的表示学习，为小数据领域提供了一种实用的替代框架。

Abstract: Large-scale pretrained time-series models achieve strong results through large-scale pretraining and task-agnostic representation learning, but they rely on abundant, diverse data that industrial and scientific domains often lack. We therefore propose the FSD-RM (Family of Small-Data Representation Models) paradigm as a practical alternative for limited, domain-specific telemetry. Rather than relying on large-scale pretraining, we focus on capacity-controlled representation learning using established encoder architectures (CNN1D, LSTM, GRU, Transformer), selected for their suitability in small-data settings and interpretability.
  These encoders are trained unsupervised on multivariate telemetry data and integrated into a two-stage pipeline for downstream lifetime prediction. To systematically examine architectural trade-offs under data constraints, we employ \textbf{dimension-aware neural architecture search (NAS)} to jointly optimize model capacity and input dimensionality.
  Experiments on cryocooler telemetry show that the proposed approach achieves competitive predictive performance while reducing training cost and model complexity. The contribution lies in combining established representation learning techniques within a coherent, NAS-driven framework tailored to small-data regimes, with explicitly defined parameter settings and design choices. The results indicate that effective representation learning can be achieved without large-scale pretraining when appropriate inductive bias and capacity control are applied.

</details>


### [268] [Every Cache Entry Earns Its Place: Global Allocation of Resolution and Coverage for KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07001)
*Haolin Tian,Yuzhe Liu,Tonghan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GraceKV，一种全局视角的KV缓存压缩方法，将压缩建模为固定缓存预算下的全局资源分配问题，通过原型树机制在层、头、槽位之间自由分配缓存资源，平衡局部分辨率与信息覆盖率。无需额外训练，全部在GPU上进行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM处理长上下文时，KV缓存的存储和重复访问成为主要瓶颈。现有压缩方法依赖预定义的固定规则，围绕token驱逐或合并单独设计，导致缓存资源无法跨层、跨头、跨上下文槽位自由流动，也无法联合分配以平衡局部分辨率和信息覆盖。

Method: GraceKV将每个层-头-槽位组合视为原子单元，构建原型树：叶节点对应token级KV条目，内部节点用一个原型压缩其子节点覆盖的KV空间。非重叠节点集合构成原子单元的表示。新增树的根节点扩展信息覆盖，分裂选定节点提升局部分辨率。所有候选动作在共享缓存预算下全局竞争，最终各树保留的节点形成压缩后的KV缓存。

Result: 在多种长上下文任务和压缩比例下，GraceKV在32个设置中的24个排名第一，且在高达128倍压缩下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了全局预算分配在协调信息覆盖与局部分辨率方面的有效性。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) process increasingly long contexts, KV cache storage and repeated access have become a major bottleneck. Existing KV cache compression methods rely on predefined, fixed compression rules and are typically developed around either token eviction or merging. As a result, cache resources can neither flow freely across layers, heads, and context slots, nor be jointly allocated to balance local resolution and information coverage. Therefore, we propose GraceKV, a global approach for the allocation of resolution and coverage in KV cache compression, and formulate the compression process as a global resource allocation problem under a fixed cache budget. GraceKV treats each layer-KV head-slot combination as an atomic unit and builds a prototype tree. Leaf nodes correspond to token-level KV entries, while each internal node uses a single prototype to compress the KV space covered by its children. A set of non-overlapping nodes in the tree forms the representation of an atomic unit. Adding the root of a new tree expands information coverage, whereas splitting a selected node improves local resolution. All candidate actions compete globally for a shared cache budget. Finally, the nodes retained across all trees form the compressed KV cache. This process adaptively determines the allocation of cache resources among atomic units globally and the balance between resolution and coverage. GraceKV requires no additional training, and the entire compression and inference process is performed on the GPU. Systematic experiments across diverse long-context tasks and compression ratios show that GraceKV ranks first in 24 of 32 settings and remains robust up to 128-fold compression. These results validate the effectiveness of global budget allocation in coordinating information coverage and local resolution.

</details>


### [269] [Trajectory-Relative Hindsight Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07371)
*Haoyu Zheng,Yun Zhu,Qing Wang,Wenqiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRIAL 是一种轨迹相对的后见之明蒸馏框架，通过统一的回合对齐评分协议，将完成轨迹中产生的密集后见信号按回合合理分配，在 WebShop 和 ALFWorld 上均优于 GRPO 及五种对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于智能体（agentic）的强化学习方法利用后见之明来补充稀疏的结果奖励，但一次完整轨迹可以产生大量这样的信号，如何将它们在各个回合之间进行恰当的分配尚不明确。

Method: TRIAD 提出轨迹相对的后见之明蒸馏框架，配备统一的回合对齐评分协议。对每个决策回合，提取该决策实际后果的结果视图，并在普通上下文和后见条件上下文下评估同一响应；通过对数概率差的符号决定标记级监督的方向与局部强度，回合级的幅度则基于已实现轨迹联合归一化。最终分配乘数以合格标记加权均值为一，在跨回合重新分配密集监督的同时固定其平均乘数。

Result: 在 WebShop 和 ALFWorld 上使用不同骨干网络，TRIAL 在全部八种骨干-环境-评估指标组合上优于 GRPO，并在六种组合上达到六种方法中的最佳或并列最佳性能。在 Qwen3-1.7B 的 WebShop 上，成功率从 56.4% 提升至 75.2%，任务得分从 78.7% 提升至 85.7%。受控消融实验表明轨迹相对的回合分配带来的增益远超单纯的密集后见蒸馏。

Conclusion: 轨迹相对的回合分配策略能更有效地利用后见信号，为智能体强化学习中的密集监督分配提供了一种统一且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent agentic reinforcement learning methods use hindsight to complement sparse outcome rewards. However, a completed rollout can yield many such signals, leaving their appropriate allocation across turns unclear. We introduce TRIAL, a trajectory-relative hindsight distillation framework with a unified turn-aligned scoring protocol. For each decision turn, TRIAL extracts an outcome view of that decision's realized consequence and evaluates the same response under ordinary and hindsight-conditioned contexts. The signed log-probability gap determines the direction and local strength of token-level supervision, while turn-level magnitudes are normalized jointly over the realized trajectory. The resulting allocation multipliers have an eligible-token-weighted mean of one, redistributing dense supervision across turns while fixing its average multiplier. Experiments on WebShop and ALFWorld with different backbones show that TRIAL outperforms GRPO across all eight combinations of backbone, environment, and evaluation metric, while achieving the best or tied-best performance among six methods on six of them. On WebShop with Qwen3-1.7B, TRIAL improves the success rate from 56.4% to 75.2% and the task score from 78.7% to 85.7%. Controlled ablations further show that trajectory-relative turn allocation provides substantial gains beyond those of dense hindsight distillation alone.

</details>


### [270] [Hyperbolic Graph Embedders for Link Prediction and Topology Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07029)
*Robert Jankowski,Maksim Kitsak,Dorota Celińska-Kopczyńska*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在统一协议下对13种无监督双曲图嵌入方法进行系统基准测试，涵盖链接预测和拓扑重建任务，发现最大似然与表示学习方法（含混合变体）整体表现最优，但无单一方法在所有任务和结构区间中占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲嵌入为复杂网络提供了紧凑的几何表示，但机器学习、网络科学和算法学等领域开发的方法之间缺乏系统性比较，导致方法选择缺乏依据。

Method: 在合成网络和实证网络上，采用统一的基准协议对13种无监督双曲图嵌入方法进行评估，协议同时涵盖缺失链接恢复以及局部与全局网络结构的保持，并依据嵌入范式和学科来源对性能进行归因分析。

Result: 最大似然方法和基于表示学习的方法（包括混合变体）整体性能最强，但没有方法在所有任务和结构区间中完全占优；性能更多与嵌入范式相关而非学科来源；研究识别了不同范式在不同网络区间中的成功与失败模式。

Conclusion: 论文为下游应用中双曲嵌入方法的选择提供了实用指导，并揭示了不同嵌入范式在不同网络结构区间中的适用边界。

Abstract: Hyperbolic embeddings provide compact geometric representations of complex networks in hyperbolic spaces, but systematic comparisons of methods developed in machine learning, network science, and algorithmics remain rare. We benchmark 13 unsupervised hyperbolic graph embedders under a unified protocol for link prediction and topology reconstruction on synthetic and empirical networks. The protocol captures both missing-link recovery and the preservation of local and global network structure. Maximum-likelihood and representation-learning-based approaches, including hybrid variants, achieve the strongest overall performance, although no method dominates across all tasks and structural regimes. Performance is more strongly associated with embedding paradigm than with disciplinary origin. We identify the network regimes in which different paradigms succeed or fail and provide practical guidance for method selection in downstream applications.

</details>


### [271] [Accounting Graph Transformer for Short-History Multi-KPI Forecasting in Small Businesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07037)
*Shrutendra Harsola,Vignesh Subrahmaniam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出会计图变换器（AGT），将71个月度分类账序列作为掩码标记，通过会计关系图的类型化注意力交换信息，结合门控三个月近因路径，实现13个KPI的12个月联合预测。在1,060家未见公司、11,993个预测起点上，AGT取得0.6990样本加权KPI宏平均MAE，优于最强基线LightGBM（0.7378），并在全部13个KPI上胜过LightGBM、TimeMixer和SOFTS。最终模型仅5.3M参数，无需公司特定拟合即可生成156个对齐预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中小企业通常只有12-24个月的会计历史数据，但规划和风险工作流需要跨财务报表的协调预测。现有方法难以在数据稀缺的情况下同时预测利润表、资产负债表、现金流量表和营运资本中的多个KPI，需要一种能利用会计领域结构知识、在小样本条件下进行多KPI联合预测的方法。

Method: AGT将71条月度分类账序列表示为掩码标记，通过固定在会计关系图上的类型化注意力机制在标记间交换信息，池化目标特定上下文，并用门控的三月近因路径融合信息。模型以端到端方式联合预测13个KPI未来12个月的值，无需公司特定微调。

Result: 在1,060家未见公司、11,993个预测起点上，AGT取得样本加权KPI宏平均MAE 0.6990±0.0013（三个独立种子），优于LightGBM的0.7378±0.0014。种子42配对公司聚类bootstrap显示AGT较LightGBM改善0.0395（95% CI [0.0350,0.0439]）。AGT在全部13个KPI上均优于基线；在7,094家额外未见公司（2025年1-5月起源）上获0.7548 MAE，优于SOFTS的0.7694。消融实验证实关系注意力、会计拓扑和近因路径均有效。

Conclusion: AGT以单一5.3M参数模型在不需公司特定拟合的情况下生成156个对齐预测，为综合规划、流动性和营运资本分析提供统一的预测层，在中小企业数据稀缺场景下显著优于强基线。

Abstract: Small businesses often have only 12-24 months of accounting history, yet planning and risk workflows require coordinated forecasts across financial statements. We study joint 12-month forecasting of 13 income-statement, balance-sheet, cash-flow, and working-capital key performance indicators (KPIs) from 71 monthly ledger series. We introduce the Accounting Graph Transformer (AGT), which represents each ledger series as a masked token, exchanges information through typed attention on a fixed accounting-relation graph, pools target-specific context, and fuses it with a gated three-month recency path. Across 11,993 forecast origins from 1,060 unseen companies, AGT achieves sample-weighted KPI-macro mean absolute error (MAE) $0.6990 \pm 0.0013$ over three independent seeds, compared with $0.7378 \pm 0.0014$ for the strongest baseline, LightGBM. At the pre-specified seed 42, a paired company-clustered bootstrap gives a LightGBM-minus-AGT difference of 0.0395 with 95% confidence interval (CI) $[0.0350,0.0439]$. AGT is best on all 13 KPIs against LightGBM, TimeMixer, and SOFTS in the matched seed-42 comparison, while final-architecture ablations show that relational attention, accounting topology, and the recency path each improve validation and test accuracy. On 7,094 additional unseen companies with origins sampled from January-May 2025, AGT obtains 0.7548 MAE versus 0.7694 for SOFTS. A single 5.3M-parameter model produces 156 aligned forecasts without company-specific fitting, providing one forecasting layer for integrated planning, liquidity, and working-capital analysis.

</details>


### [272] [Beyond Isolation: Unlocking Reinforcement Learning Component Synergy for Sample-Efficient Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07086)
*Qi Zhao,Guozheng Ma,Yilun Kong,Lu Li,Haoyu Wang,Zilin Wang,Tiantian Zhang,Yuxing Wang,Jian Sha,Yongzhe Chang,Xueqian Wang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了强化学习系统中各算法组件之间的功能依赖关系，发现组件功效具有显著的任务依赖性，简单堆叠先进技术反而会引发复合非平稳性等问题。基于这些发现，作者提出ROSER框架，协调了模型表示、优化稳定性与经验回放三个关键维度，在连续控制基准上相比朴素堆叠取得了17.60%的性能提升，强调了RL系统设计需要整体性视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习系统因其固有属性比传统机器学习范式复杂得多，系统设计需要联合考虑众多紧密耦合的因素。尽管单个算法组件已有长足进展，但它们之间的功能依赖关系仍未被充分探索：组件之间究竟是相互协同还是产生干扰？这一核心问题尚未得到系统性回答。

Method: 作者首先进行系统性的实证调查，探究不同RL算法组件（如模型表示、优化策略、经验回放等）之间的功能交互关系，揭示其任务依赖性以及朴素堆叠引发的复合非平稳性问题。基于调查所得洞见，提出ROSER框架，协调三个关键维度：基于模型的表示（Model-based Representation）、优化稳定性（Optimization Stability）和经验回放（Experience Replay）。

Result: 在多样化的连续控制基准上，ROSER一致性地优于vanilla基线，相比朴素堆叠方法取得了17.60%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了采用整体性视角进行RL系统设计的必要性，为开发样本高效的智能体铺平了道路。组件间的协调而非简单堆叠才是提升RL系统性能的关键。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning systems are significantly more complex than other machine learning paradigms due to inherent properties, causing RL system design to jointly account for many tightly coupled factors. Despite advances in individual algorithmic components, their functional interdependencies remain underexplored: do they exhibit mutual synergy or counterproductive interference? To bridge this gap, we conduct a systematic investigation and find that the efficacy of different components exhibits significant task-dependency, and naively stacking state-of-the-art techniques does not necessarily yield performance gains; instead, it often triggers emergent challenges, such as compounded non-stationarity. Building upon these findings, we distill a suite of actionable insights into the principled coordination of these components. Guided by these insights, we propose ROSER, an RL framework that coordinates three critical dimensions: Model-based Representation, Optimization Stability, and Experience Replay. Across diverse continuous-control benchmarks, ROSER consistently outperforms vanilla baselines and achieves 17.60% gains over naive stack. Our findings underscore the necessity of a holistic perspective in RL system design and paves the way for developing sample-efficient agents.

</details>


### [273] [Online Conformal Prediction Beyond Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07139)
*Joar Skalse,Edoardo Pona,Osvaldo Simeone,Nicola Paoletti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种超越反馈的在线共形预测（OCP）设置，其中学习者每轮要么输出预测集，要么查询真实标签，但不能两者兼得。作者将问题归约为部分监控博弈，提出OCPQ算法，在T轮中实现O(T^(2/3))的期望遗憾和β-O(T^(-1/3))的期望覆盖率，同时仅需查询T^(-1/3)比例轮次，与基于bandit的方法覆盖率相当但无需部署预测集的任何反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线共形预测依赖之前部署预测的反馈来更新预测集，但在许多实际安全关键场景中，部署的预测结果无法直接获得评估反馈（例如预测集输出后不会立即被验证正确性）。作者希望探索一个更严格的设置——学习者要么输出预测集要么查询标签，但不能同时进行——在此约束下仍能保证理论上有原则的不确定性量化。

Method: 作者将问题归约为一个部分监控博弈：预测动作不返回任何观测，单独的查询动作才返回标签。通过构造奖励函数鼓励学习者输出小预测集同时确保覆盖率。基于Cesa-Bianchi等人（2004）的label efficient forecaster，提出OCPQ算法，在每个轮次中决定是输出预测集还是查询标签，并通过查询获得的反馈来更新内部模型。

Result: 对于任意黑盒分类器和非独立同分布的无遗漏（oblivious）数据流，OCPQ在T轮中实现O(T^(2/3))期望遗憾，期望覆盖率至少为β-O(T^(-1/3))，且仅需查询期望T^(-1/3)比例的轮次。在真实数据集上的实验验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: OCPQ提供了与基于bandit的OCP方法相当的覆盖率保证，但不需要部署预测集的任何反馈，拓宽了在线共形预测在无法获得部署反馈场景中的适用性。这为安全关键应用中的不确定性量化提供了更灵活的框架。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification is essential when deploying machine learning models in safety-critical applications. Online conformal prediction (OCP) provides theoretically principled uncertainty quantification for arbitrary black-box classifiers and non-i.i.d. data streams by constructing prediction sets that are guaranteed to contain the true label at a user-specified frequency. OCP usually updates prediction sets using feedback from previously deployed predictions. We instead study an OCP setting beyond feedback: on each round, the learner can either output a prediction set or query the correct label, but not both. Thus, no deployed prediction is ever evaluated directly. We reduce this problem to a partial monitoring game in which prediction actions return no observation and a separate query action reveals the label. The reward function is constructed in a way that encourages the learner to output small prediction sets while ensuring that the correct label is covered with a sufficiently high probability. To solve this game, we develop OCP with queries (OCPQ) by adapting the label efficient forecaster of Cesa-Bianchi, Lugosi, and Stoltz (2004) to our setting. For any black box classifier and any (non-i.i.d.) oblivious data stream of length $T$, OCPQ has $O(T^{2/3})$ expected regret and expected coverage at least $β-O(T^{-1/3})$ for a user-defined $β$, while querying only an expected $T^{-1/3}$ fraction of rounds. This provides coverage comparable to bandit-based OCP methods while requiring no feedback from deployed prediction sets. Experiments on real-world datasets further demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [274] [Interpretable reinforcement learning with decision-tree pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07151)
*Mark Leon Ringer,Michel Tokic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对基于决策树规则策略的剪枝流程，通过定义结构性和使用感知的操作符，在保持任务性能的同时简化策略、提升可解释性，并使策略编辑可审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习策略难以检查，但可解释性是可信赖的前提。将训练好的策略转换为显式决策树规则虽提升了透明度，但生成的规则往往过于复杂，人类难以理解，因此需要进一步简化。

Method: 提出一个剪枝流程，定义了一小组结构性和使用感知的操作符（structural and usage-aware operators），通过重新执行策略来评估候选编辑，以衡量返回值和可解释性代理指标，从而将复杂的规则策略逐步转化为紧凑结构。

Result: 在经典控制和MuJoCo基准测试上，剪枝轨迹显示一致的可解释性提升，同时保持高性能。

Conclusion: 该剪枝流程能够将复杂的规则策略转化为紧凑的决策树策略，在保持任务性能的同时显著提升可解释性，并提供了可审计的策略编辑过程，有助于提升强化学习策略的可信度。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning policies are difficult to inspect, but interpreting them is a prerequisite for trustworthiness. Converting a trained policy into explicit decision-tree rules improves transparency and the resulting artifacts often remain too complex for human understanding. We present a pruning process that simplifies such rule-based policies while preserving task performance and making edits to the policy auditable. The process defines a small set of structural and usage-aware operators and evaluates candidate edits by re-executing the policy to measure return and interpretability proxies. This exposes an transformation process from complex to compact policy structures. We investigate this approach on classic control and MuJoCo benchmarks, where pruning traces reveal consistent interpretability improvements while maintaining high performance.

</details>


### [275] [Machine Learning-Based Inter-Crystal Scatter Recovery for Ultra-High Resolution PET Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07155)
*Alexandre Bernier,Roger Lecomte,Jean-Baptiste Michaud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种前馈神经网络方法，用于推断超高分辨率PET中晶体间散射（ICS）事件的首次康普顿相互作用的响应线，从而在不损失空间分辨率的前提下将灵敏度提升70%至106%，并通过蒙特卡罗模拟和实验数据验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UHR-PET中晶体尺寸缩小和读出分段化使得ICS事件成为重大挑战，传统方法要么拒绝这些事件降低灵敏度，要么用次优定位算法接受它们导致图像分辨率下降，亟需一种既能恢复ICS事件又能保持高空间分辨率的方法。

Method: 采用前馈神经网络（feed forward neural network）来推断第一次康普顿相互作用所属的响应线（LOR），从而恢复ICS事件。使用蒙特卡罗模拟和基于LabPET-II的全像素化临床前及脑部UHR-PET扫描仪的实验数据进行验证。

Result: 与传统方法相比，灵敏度提升了70%至106%，同时保持亚毫米级空间可分辨性（低至1.6mm）。该方法有效补偿了小尺寸像素化探测器在UHR-PET中较低的探测效率。

Conclusion: 该ICS恢复方法是UHR-PET中处理小尺寸像素化探测器检测效率不足的有效解决方案，可在大幅保持图像质量的同时缩短扫描时间并降低辐射剂量。

Abstract: Inter-crystal scatter (ICS) events pose a significant challenge in ultrahigh- resolution positron emission tomography (UHR-PET), especially as detector crystals become smaller and their readouts increasingly segmented. Current approaches either reject these events, reducing sensitivity, or accept them with suboptimal positioning algorithms, degrading image resolution. We present a feed forward neural network to optimize ICS event recovery by inferring the line-of-response belonging to the first Compton interaction. Our approach was validated using both Monte Carlo simulations and experimental data from the fully pixelated LabPET-IIbased preclinical and brain UHR-PET scanners.Results demonstrate a 70% to 106% increase in sensitivity while preserving sub-millimeter spatial resolvability (down to 1.6 mm) compared to conventional methods. This ICS recovery approach is an effective solution that compensates for the lower detection efficiency of small, pixelated detectors in UHR-PET, enabling reduced scan times and lower radiation doses while largely preserving image quality.

</details>


### [276] [Capacity Confounds and Coverage Guarantees in Adaptive Sub-model Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07157)
*Alireza Moayedikia,Alicia Troncoso Lora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过HAS-FL框架深入分析了子模型联邦学习中的自适应容量分配问题，发现三个关键结论：更新散度对客户端异质性的估计被设备容量所主导而非数据分布；自适应分配存在隐藏失败模式（被截断的参数停留在随机初始化并污染全局模型）；在预算匹配条件下，自适应分配并不优于随机分配，真正保护准确率的是参数覆盖率而非分配智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子模型联邦学习按设备资源分配容量，但已有多项研究反复建议根据服务器观察到的更新来估计客户端数据异质性并据此分配容量。作者质疑这一被广泛建议的步骤是否真的可行，希望厘清基于更新的异质性信号是否可信以及自适应分配是否真正有效。

Method: 以HAS-FL（自适应容量分配框架）为测试用例，采用三种策略对比：自适应分配、均匀分配和随机分配（匹配相同平均预算）。通过两个修正后的异质性估计器、多个数据集和所有随机种子，将更新散度估计与真实标签分布散度进行对比验证；同时检验覆盖率对模型性能的影响。

Result: (1) 更新散度估计与设备容量呈强负相关，控制容量后无数据信号残留，存在此前未被记录的混淆效应；(2) 当所有客户端都被限制在满宽度以下时，未覆盖参数停留在随机初始化并逐渐污染全局模型，覆盖率保证可以消除该失败；(3) 在匹配预算条件下，随机分配与自适应分配在两个图像基准上表现无差异，而在文本基准上自适应分配是最弱的策略且消耗容量最多。

Conclusion: 子模型训练仍有价值，因为它能矩阵级降低成本地接纳资源受限客户端，但真正保护准确率的是参数覆盖率而非分配智能。自适应分配的表面收益来源于容量预算和覆盖保证，未来的设计需要能够与容量效应分离的异质性信号。

Abstract: Sub-model federated learning lets resource-constrained clients train width-reduced versions of a global model, but existing methods allocate capacity by device resources alone. A natural next step, allocating capacity by each client's data heterogeneity as estimated from the updates the server already observes, has been repeatedly suggested. We ask whether that step is possible, using HAS-FL, an adaptive capacity-allocation framework, as a test case. Our findings are threefold. First, validated against ground-truth label-distribution divergence on reproducible partitions, update-divergence estimates of client heterogeneity are dominated by capacity rather than data: across two corrected estimators, multiple datasets, and all seeds, the estimates correlate strongly and negatively with device capacity, and no data signal remains once capacity is controlled for. This previously undocumented confound affects any method estimating client statistics from sub-model updates. Second, adaptive allocation has a hidden failure mode: when every client is capped below full width, the uncovered parameters stay at random initialization and progressively corrupt the global model. A simple coverage guarantee removes the failure and explains why uniform allocation collapses. Third, a matched-budget control settles what adaptivity contributes: random allocation to the same average budget performs no differently on both image benchmarks, and on the naturally partitioned text benchmark the adaptive policy is the weakest of the three strategies while consuming the most capacity. Sub-model training remains valuable because it admits constrained clients at quadratically reduced cost, but what protects accuracy is parameter coverage rather than allocation intelligence. Its apparent benefits come from capacity budgeting and coverage, and future designs need heterogeneity signals separable from capacity effects.

</details>


### [277] [Edge Sparsification via Temporal Forman-Ricci Curvature for Dynamic Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07158)
*Poupak Azad,Cuneyt Gurcan Akcora,Kiarash Shamsi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRicci框架，将经典Forman-Ricci曲率扩展到有向加权时序图，通过捕获结构支撑、时间近因性和局部交互竞争来指导边剪枝，在大规模动态图学习上实现约80%的稀疏化率，同时将端到端训练和推理时间平均降低55.94%，且不显著损失预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序图学习对于金融交易网络、通信系统和在线社交平台等动态交互系统至关重要，但大规模、密集且快速变化的时序图学习在计算上极具挑战性，现有方法难以高效处理这一场景。

Method: 提出网络曲率启发的边稀疏化框架TRicci，将经典Forman-Ricci曲率扩展至有向加权时序图，综合考虑结构支撑（structural support）、时间近因性（temporal recency）和局部交互竞争（local interaction competition）三个维度来评估边的重要性并进行剪枝。

Result: 在9个交易网络和3个时序图基准数据集上的实验表明，TRicci可将时序图稀疏化约80%，端到端下游训练与推理时间平均降低55.94%，同时多个图级预测任务上的预测性能保持稳定，无明显退化。

Conclusion: 时序曲率可以作为可扩展时序图学习的原理性基础，在大幅稀疏化条件下仍能保留具有预测价值的时序结构信息，为大规模动态图学习提供了一种高效且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Temporal graph learning has become essential for analyzing real-world systems whose interactions continuously evolve over time, including financial transaction networks, communication systems, and online social platforms. However, learning from large-scale temporal graphs remains computationally challenging when networks are dense and rapidly changing. To address this limitation, we propose a network-curvature-inspired edge sparsification framework for dynamic graph learning. Our proposed method, TRicci, extends classical Forman-Ricci curvature to directed weighted temporal graphs by capturing structural support, temporal recency, and local interaction competition.
  Experiments on 9 transaction networks and 3 temporal graph benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework preserves predictive performance across multiple graph-level prediction tasks. The results show that TRicci sparsifies temporal graphs by approximately 80% while reducing end-to-end downstream training and inference time by an average of 55.94%, without substantial degradation in predictive performance. Our findings suggest that temporal curvature can serve as a principled basis for scalable temporal graph learning by preserving predictive temporal-structural information under substantial sparsification.

</details>


### [278] [Fluid-DiT: Graph-Free Diffusion Transformers for Fluid Flow Simulations Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07161)
*Shentong Mo,Guolin Ke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fluid-DiT，一种无图（Graph-Free）的扩散Transformer模型，用于模拟复杂流体流动的平衡分布。它用基于注意力机制的去噪替代图消息传递，消除显式图设计，并通过潜空间公式将几何保真度与分布学习解耦，从而在层流圆柱尾流、椭圆流系统和湍流3D机翼等基准上超越基于图的扩散基线模型，在样本质量和分布精度上均取得更优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真流体模拟计算成本过高，近期DGNs将扩散模型与图神经网络结合以直接从非结构化网格采样平衡态，但图基扩散方法受限于手工设计的架构约束、消息传递感受野有限以及昂贵的多尺度设计，难以扩展到更大更复杂的域。

Method: Fluid-DiT采用无图扩散Transformer架构：1) 用注意力机制替代图消息传递进行去噪，消除显式图设计；2) 引入潜空间公式，将几何保真度与分布学习解耦，减少高频伪影并加速采样；3) 利用Transformer全局感受野自然捕获局部流动结构和长程相关性，无需分层图粗化。

Result: 在层流圆柱尾流、椭圆流系统和湍流3D机翼等标准基准上，Fluid-DiT在样本质量和分布精度上均优于图基扩散基线，取得更高的R²相关性和更低的Wasserstein距离；并能从短的不完整轨迹稳健泛化到未见过的雷诺数和几何形状。

Conclusion: Fluid-DiT通过消除图的显式设计并用注意力机制替代消息传递，证明了Transformer架构在流体平衡分布建模上的可扩展性和通用性，在分布精度和样本质量上均超越图基扩散方法。

Abstract: Simulating complex fluid flows requires capturing full equilibrium distributions rather than just mean trajectories, yet high-fidelity solvers remain computationally prohibitive. Recent advances, such as Diffusion Graph Networks (DGNs), have combined diffusion models with graph neural networks to sample equilibrium states directly from unstructured meshes, enabling distributional accuracy even from short simulations. However, graph-based diffusion approaches suffer from hand-crafted architectural constraints, limited receptive fields in message passing, and costly multi-scale designs, which restrict scalability to larger and more complex domains. We propose Fluid-DiT, a Graph-Free Diffusion Transformer that replaces graph message passing with attention-based denoising, eliminating explicit graph design while preserving the ability to model distributions of chaotic flows. Our framework introduces a latent-space formulation that disentangles geometric fidelity from distributional learning, reducing high-frequency artifacts and accelerating sampling. By leveraging the transformer's global receptive field, Fluid-DiT naturally captures both local flow structures and long-range correlations without requiring hierarchical graph coarsening. On canonical benchmarks including laminar cylinder wakes, ellipse-flow systems, and turbulent 3D wing experiments, Fluid-DiT consistently outperforms graph-based diffusion baselines in both sample quality and distributional accuracy, achieving higher $R^2$ correlations and lower Wasserstein distances. Moreover, it generalizes robustly from short, incomplete trajectories to unseen Reynolds numbers and geometries, demonstrating strong scalability.

</details>


### [279] [$\boldsymbolλ$-Orthogonality Regularization for Compatible Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16664)
*Simone Ricci,Niccolò Biondi,Federico Pernici,Ioannis Patras,Alberto Del Bimbo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出λ-正交性正则化方法，在仿射变换中施加松弛的正交约束，既实现分布特定的适配又保留原始学习到的表示空间。实验证明该方法在多种架构和数据集上能保持模型的零样本性能，并确保模型更新间的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索系统依赖深度模型学习到的表示，但由于训练成本高昂且不同模型学习到的表示存在不一致性，需要实现跨独立训练网络间的表示通信与兼容性。现有两种主流适配方法各有缺陷：仿射变换能良好适配特定分布但会显著改变原始表示，正交变换虽保留原始结构但严格几何约束限制了适应性。核心挑战是在下游分布上对齐更新模型与先前模型的潜在空间，同时保留新学习的表示空间。

Method: 提出λ-正交性正则化方法，在学习仿射变换的同时施加松弛的正交性约束。该方法在保持仿射变换灵活适应性的同时，通过λ参数控制正交性程度，从而保留原始学习到的表示空间，实现分布特定适配与表示保留之间的平衡。

Result: 在多种架构和数据集上的大量实验验证了该方法的有效性，证明其能保持模型的零样本性能，并确保模型更新之间的兼容性。

Conclusion: λ-正交性正则化是一种有效的表示适配方法，在仿射变换中融入松弛正交约束，兼顾了分布特定适配与原始表示保留，为模型更新间的表示兼容性提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval systems rely on representations learned by increasingly powerful models. However, due to the high training cost and inconsistencies in learned representations, there is significant interest in facilitating communication between representations and ensuring compatibility across independently trained neural networks. In the literature, two primary approaches are commonly used to adapt different learned representations: affine transformations, which adapt well to specific distributions but can significantly alter the original representation, and orthogonal transformations, which preserve the original structure with strict geometric constraints but limit adaptability. A key challenge is adapting the latent spaces of updated models to align with those of previous models on downstream distributions while preserving the newly learned representation spaces. In this paper, we impose a relaxed orthogonality constraint, namely $λ$-Orthogonality regularization, while learning an affine transformation, to obtain distribution-specific adaptation while retaining the original learned representations. Extensive experiments across various architectures and datasets validate our approach, demonstrating that it preserves the model's zero-shot performance and ensures compatibility across model updates. Code available at: \href{https://github.com/miccunifi/lambda_orthogonality.git}{https://github.com/miccunifi/lambda\_orthogonality}.

</details>


### [280] [Momba: Network Modernization Improves Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07180)
*Adam Štafa,Santeri Heiskanen,Petr Novotný,Joni Pajarinen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了多目标强化学习（MORL）中神经网络架构设计的重要性，通过将（i）观测与特征归一化、（ii）权重归一化以及（iii）分布回报建模与熵正则化MORL算法相结合，在标准连续控制基准上显著提升了生成解集的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习领域的进展表明，改进神经网络架构可以在不改变底层算法的情况下大幅提升样本效率和渐近性能。然而，MORL领域的研究主要集中于算法创新，对架构设计探索不足。MORL算法通常使用简单的以权衡（trade-off）为条件的全连接网络来表示最优策略和价值函数，但不同权衡下的最优策略和价值函数差异显著，因此作者质疑更强大的函数逼近器能否提升算法性能。

Method: 作者将三类近年来的神经网络设计进展整合到熵正则化MORL算法中：（i）观测与特征归一化（observation and feature normalization），（ii）权重归一化（weight normalization），（iii）分布回报建模（distributional return modeling）。这些组件在不改变底层算法核心逻辑的前提下，增强了函数逼近器的表达能力。

Result: 在标准连续控制基准上的实证结果表明，这些架构改进显著提升了算法生成解集（solution sets）的质量，且无需对底层算法进行重大修改。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了MORL领域在架构设计方面的空白，证明了更富有表现力的函数逼近器能够有效提升MORL算法的性能，为后续在MORL中探索架构创新提供了有力证据和实用指导。

Abstract: Recent advances in deep reinforcement learning (RL) have shown that improving neural network architectures can yield substantial gains in sample efficiency and asymptotic performance without altering the underlying algorithms. In contrast, work on multi-objective reinforcement learning (MORL), which aims to discover a set of policies that balance trade-offs among conflicting objectives, has predominantly focused on algorithmic innovations, leaving the area of architectures underexplored. While the optimal policies and value functions can differ significantly depending on the trade-offs, MORL algorithms commonly represent them with simple feedforward networks conditioned on the trade-off. This raises the question of whether the performance of the algorithms could be improved with more expressive function approximators. In this paper, we integrate recent advances in neural network design: (i) observation and feature normalization, (ii) weight normalization, and (iii) modeling of distributional returns with an entropy-regularized MORL algorithm. The empirical results across standard continuous control benchmarks demonstrate that these changes substantially improve the quality of the produced solution sets without requiring major changes to the underlying algorithm.

</details>


### [281] [Conformal Fusion Under Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07183)
*Alireza Moayedikia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了模态条件共形融合（MCCF），一种同时解决多模态学习中缺失模态鲁棒性与不确定性校准问题的架构。MCCF结合模态dropout训练的瓶颈融合骨干、逐模态证据头产生的Dirichlet分布、Dempster-Shafer融合规则以及基于模态存在掩码的Mondrian共形校准模块，为任意非空模态子集提供有限样本分组条件覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态融合架构假设推理时所有模态可用，但实际场景中传感器故障、采集变异和成本约束常导致观测不完整。已有工作将模态缺失视为预测准确性问题，忽略了一个更基本的问题：当整个输入流被移除时，模型的置信度估计是否仍然校准。作者主张缺失模态鲁棒性和校准不确定性应被视为单一耦合属性。

Method: MCCF由四部分组成：(1) 模态dropout训练的多模态瓶颈融合骨干；(2) 产生模态分解Dirichlet分布的逐模态证据头；(3) 将逐模态证据融合为联合预测分布的Dempster-Shafer组合规则（缺失模态贡献空证据，在结构上被忽略，融合不确定性自动反映信息减少，无需测试时插补）；(4) 以模态存在掩码为键的Mondrian共形校准模块，为每个非空模态子集提供有限样本分组条件覆盖。

Result: 在合成问题和三个真实多模态基准上，MCCF在所有模态存在子集上保持目标覆盖率，相比边际分裂共形基线大幅缩小了全模态与部分模态之间的覆盖差距，且相对温度缩放和证据基线没有可测量的精度损失。MCCF是首个通过架构整合而非事后再校准，在任意模态可用性下提供形式化覆盖保证的方法。证据分解产生的逐模态空分数可将不确定性定位到负责缺失的模态。

Conclusion: MCCF通过将缺失模态鲁棒性与校准不确定性作为耦合问题统一解决，实现了任意模态子集下的形式化覆盖保证，同时不损失预测精度，并能为不确定性归因到具体缺失模态。

Abstract: Multimodal fusion architectures typically assume all modalities are available at inference, yet sensor failures, acquisition variability, and cost constraints routinely produce incomplete observations. Existing work treats modality absence as a prediction-accuracy problem, leaving a more basic question unanswered: whether a model's confidence estimates remain calibrated when an entire input stream is removed. We argue that missing-modality robustness and calibrated uncertainty are a single coupled property, and introduce Modality-Conditioned Conformal Fusion (MCCF), an architecture that addresses both at once. MCCF combines a multimodal bottleneck fusion backbone trained with modality dropout, per-modality evidential heads producing modality-decomposed Dirichlet distributions, and a Dempster-Shafer combination rule that fuses the per-modality evidence into a joint predictive distribution; an absent modality contributes vacuous evidence that is structurally ignored, so the fused uncertainty automatically reflects the reduced information without test-time imputation. A Mondrian conformal calibration module keyed on the modality-presence mask then provides finite-sample group-conditional coverage for every non-empty modality subset. MCCF is, to our knowledge, the first method with formal coverage guarantees under arbitrary modality availability through architectural integration rather than post-hoc recalibration, and the evidential decomposition yields per-modality vacuity scores that localise uncertainty to the absent modality responsible. Across a synthetic problem and three real multimodal benchmarks, MCCF holds its target coverage on every modality-presence subset, substantially narrows the coverage gap between full and partial modalities relative to a marginal split-conformal baseline, and imposes no measurable accuracy cost relative to temperature-scaled and evidential baselines.

</details>


### [282] [MAUPITI: On-Device Prototype-Based Learning on a Smart Infrared Sensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07192)
*Beatrice Alessandra Motetti,Tanguy Dugas du Villard,Matteo Risso,Alessio Burrello,Francesco Daghero,Enrico Macii,Massimo Poncino,Marco Castellano,Alfio Basile,Daniele Jahier Pagliari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种智能多像素红外(IR)传感器系统，集成16×16热MOSFET(TMOS)阵列和扩展了低精度SIMD指令的RISC-V微控制器，在严格功耗(<1.5mW)和内存(<32kB)约束下实现姿态和手势识别。系统采用基于原型的最近类均值(NCM)分类器，CNN编码器离线训练量化，类原型在设备上流式存储更新，支持设备端学习和持续适应，准确率与传统分类器相当，且更新延迟开销极小(<0.29%)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低分辨率红外阵列传感器是隐私保护型人体感测的理想方案，但嵌入式系统面临严格的内存和功耗约束。传统基于反向传播和重放缓冲区的在线学习方法内存开销过大，难以在<32kB片内存储和约1.5mW功耗的预算内实现设备端持续学习和即时适应。

Method: 采用原型驱动的最近类均值(NCM)分类器方案：CNN编码器离线训练并量化，类原型在设备上以流式模式存储和更新；同时将RISC-V微控制器扩展低精度SIMD指令以加速计算。系统在推理和原型更新两个阶段均实现了低延迟开销。

Result: 在两个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在准确率上与常规分类器相当，分类和原型更新的延迟开销总和小于0.29%，有效实现了感知框架的在线自适应。

Conclusion: 原型式NCM分类器配合离线量化CNN编码器，是在极端资源受限平台上实现设备端持续学习和适应的可行方案，既能避免反向传播和重放缓冲区的内存开销，又能保持与传统方法相当的准确率。

Abstract: Low-resolution infrared (IR) array sensors represent an interesting solution for privacy-preserving human sensing in embedded systems. In this letter, we describe a smart multi-pixel IR sensor integrating a 16$\times$16 thermal MOSFET (TMOS) array and a RISC-V microcontroller extended with low-precision SIMD instructions, capable of on-device learning and continual adaptation for pose and gesture recognition tasks under tight memory and power constraints ($<$32kB on-chip memory, $\approx$1.5mW). To avoid the memory overheads of backpropagation and replay buffers, we adopt a prototype-based Nearest Class Mean (NCM) classifier in which a simple Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) encoder is trained and quantized offline, while class prototypes are stored and updated on the device in streaming mode. With experiments on two datasets, we show that this approach yields accuracy on par with a conventional classifier, with negligible latency overheads in both the classification and the prototype update ($<$0.29% considering both phases), effectively enabling online adaptation of the perception framework.

</details>


### [283] [An AI4AI Framework for Visual Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07193)
*Zhen Liu,Wenli Huang,Wei Song,Yuhan Liu,Zhiqin Yang,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoPrune是一个训练无关的AI4AI框架，利用大型语言模型自动设计视觉token剪枝策略。其核心是引入Token剪枝领域特定语言（TPDSL），包含131个可复用原子，并将搜索状态表示为对强基线的残差修改，以缩小搜索空间。在14个多模态基准和3个MLLM骨干上验证了有效性、效率和可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法依赖固定的手工启发式策略和昂贵的专家试错。随着剪枝目标、预算和模型架构的多样化，手动导航设计空间越来越困难。作者希望让LLM自动设计有效的视觉token剪枝算法，关键挑战在于设计合适的搜索状态表示来连接LLM内部知识与剪枝任务的结构要求。

Method: 提出AutoPrune训练无关框架，核心是Token Pruning Domain-Specific Language（TPDSL），包含131个可复用原子，覆盖预算控制、token评分、选择约束和token重组四个方面。关键特性是将每个搜索状态表示为对强基线的残差修改，从而缩小搜索空间并引导LLM关注对性能影响最大的策略组件。

Result: 在14个多模态基准和3个MLLM骨干上验证了有效性、效率和可迁移性。即使移除94.4%的视觉token，AutoPrune仍能保留超过99%的全token性能，同时将FLOPs降低9.9倍、预填充延迟降低6.4倍。

Conclusion: AutoPrune证明了LLM能够自动设计有效的视觉token剪枝算法，残差搜索状态表示是连接LLM通用知识与专用任务的关键。该框架在极端token压缩率下仍能保持性能，展现了良好的实用性和可迁移性。

Abstract: Visual-token pruning can substantially reduce the inference cost of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), yet existing methods largely rely on fixed, handcrafted heuristics and costly expert trial and error. As pruning objectives, budgets, and model architectures diversify, manually navigating the expanding design space becomes increasingly difficult. This paper aims to build an AI4AI framework for visual-token pruning by addressing a natural question: Can large language models automatically design effective visual-token reduction algorithms? Although LLMs possess broad algorithmic knowledge and strong reasoning capabilities, translating such general knowledge into effective solutions for a specialized task remains nontrivial. We argue that the key lies in designing an appropriate search-state representation that connects the internal knowledge of LLMs with the structural requirements and constraints of visual-token pruning. Based on this insight, we propose AutoPrune, a training-free framework for LLM-driven visual-token pruning policy design. At its core, AutoPrune introduces a Token Pruning Domain-Specific Language (TPDSL) comprising 131 reusable atoms for budget control, token scoring, selection constraints, and token reassembly. A key property of TPDSL is that it represents each search state as a residual modification of a strong base policy. This residual formulation narrows the search space and directs the LLM's attention toward the policy components that are most consequential for performance. Experiments on 14 multimodal benchmarks and three MLLM backbones demonstrate the effectiveness, efficiency, and transferability of AutoPrune. Even when removing 94.4% of visual tokens, AutoPrune preserves more than 99% of full-token performance while reducing FLOPs by 9.9x and prefill latency by 6.4x.

</details>


### [284] [Stochastic Autoregressive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07224)
*Ilan Doron-Arad,Idan Mehalel,Elchanan Mossel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文受LLM迭代采样生成方式的启发，提出了二元随机自回归学习的PAC学习模型，推广了Joshi等人（COLT 2025）的确定性自回归学习框架。研究三种监督形式（基础一步采样、思维链CoT、端到端e2e）下的样本复杂度，证明随机自回归学习与确定性理论存在本质差异：三种学习任务在相同尺度下不存在普适比较，但通过调整尺度可建立紧的上下界关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM通过迭代地从下一个token分布中采样来生成输出，但现有的自回归PAC学习理论（Joshi等人，COLT 2025）仅处理确定性设置。有必要建立随机自回归学习的理论基础，理解在此模型下不同监督形式（基础采样、CoT、e2e）所需的样本复杂度及其相互关系，从而更准确地刻画实际LLM训练中的统计学习特性。

Method: 提出一个统一的二元随机自回归学习模型：固定生成器为每个提示串分配Bernoulli下一token分布，通过M步迭代采样生成轨迹。定义三类监督：基础一步样本（仅单步采样）、CoT样本（完整的M步随机轨迹）、e2e样本（仅M步轨迹的最终token）。针对生成器类，分析在平方损失ε误差下学习各自目标（基础模型的一步概率、CoT和e2e模型的最终token概率）所需的最小样本数m_base、m_CoT、m_e2e。通过构造反例证明无普适比较关系，并推导经过尺度调整后的上界关系；最后对d维logistic函数类给出具体分析。

Result: （1）随机自回归学习与确定性理论有本质差异：在相同尺度ε下，m_CoT/m_base和m_e2e/m_CoT可同时任意大于M/ε（确定性结果的自然类比）。（2）经尺度调整后，每类生成器的CoT学习在ε尺度下被基础学习在ε/M²尺度下上界约束；e2e学习在ε尺度下被(M/ε)·m_CoT(Θ(ε))上界约束（忽略对数因子）。（3）上述依赖关系和尺度本质上紧。（4）对d维logistic函数类给出了补充的具体分析。

Conclusion: 随机自回归学习展现出与确定性情形截然不同的样本复杂性结构：三种监督任务在相同尺度下不可直接比较，但通过尺度调整可以建立紧的上下界。这为理解LLM等随机自回归模型中不同训练信号（如CoT监督与端到端监督）的统计效率提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Motivated by LLMs, which generate outputs by iteratively sampling from next-token distributions, we introduce a PAC-learning model for binary stochastic autoregressive learning. This generalizes the deterministic autoregressive learning framework of Joshi et al., COLT 2025. In our model, one fixed generator assigns a Bernoulli next-token distribution to every prompt string. Starting from an input prompt, a token is sampled and appended to the prompt; the same generator is then applied again to this expanded prompt; this procedure is repeated for $M$ steps. Three forms of supervision are considered: base one-step samples, chain-of-thought (CoT) samples that reveal full random trajectories of length $M$, and end-to-end (e2e) samples that reveal only the final token of length $M$ trajectories. For a generator class, we study the minimum number of samples $m_{base}(\varepsilon),m_{CoT}(\varepsilon), m_{e2e}(\varepsilon)$, resp., required to learn the one-step probabilities in the base model, and the final-token probability in the CoT and e2e models, under squared loss error~$\varepsilon$.
  We show that stochastic autoregressive learning fundamentally differs from the deterministic theory. At scale $\varepsilon$, there is no universal comparison between the three learning tasks: both $m_{CoT}/m_{base}$ and $m_{e2e}/m_{CoT}$ can be made simultaneously arbitrarily larger than $M/\varepsilon$, the natural analogue for the existing deterministic results. Nevertheless, after altering scales, for every class, CoT learning at scale $\varepsilon$ is upper-bounded by base learning at scale $\varepsilon/M^2$, whereas e2e learning at scale $\varepsilon$ is upper-bounded, up to logarithmic factors, by $(M/\varepsilon) m_{CoT}(Θ(\varepsilon))$. These dependencies and scales are essentially tight. We complement these bounds by studying dimension $d$ logistic functions in our model.

</details>


### [285] [Learning Suffers More Than the Policy Class Under Partial Observability: A Closed-Form Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07228)
*Idil Gözel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了部分可观测的线性二次控制问题中，actor-critic强化学习算法的失败原因。即使最优策略可表达且值函数足够强大，学习仍会收敛到比最可达策略差35%的结果。问题根源在于critic的偏差：agent无法归因不可观测状态变化，将其误读为值估计的曲率，导致actor偏离最优。作者推导了闭式解，并发现调整"lookahead"距离（而非记忆过去观测）可以消除该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为，当强化学习agent无法观测完整状态时，失败应归咎于策略表达能力的不足。作者质疑这一假设，试图证明在可求解的情况下，即使策略表达力足够，学习过程本身仍可能系统性失败，并找到根本原因与解决方案。

Method: 作者选择了一个部分可观测的线性二次控制问题，在其中标准actor-critic学习器可以闭式求解。通过数学推导分析critic的学习偏差（将不可观测状态变化误认为值函数曲率），得到策略、代价和设计选择的闭式表达式，并通过深度强化学习实验验证理论预测。

Result: 默认设置下，agent可表达的最优策略仅比理想全观测控制器贵10.4%，但学习算法收敛到的策略比最佳可达策略差35%。作者定位了critic的偏差是根因，并证明改变lookahead距离（即学习者在信任自身值估计之前向前看的距离）可以消除该问题；而给agent记忆过去观测并不能帮助。

Conclusion: 部分可观测下强化学习失败的根源可能在critic的估计偏差而非actor的表达限制。解决方案不是增加记忆，而是调整学习者信任自身值估计所需的前瞻距离。这一发现与深度RL实验高度吻合，为部分可观测RL提供了一条新的改进方向。

Abstract: When a reinforcement learning agent cannot observe the full state, we usually blame its policies: it cannot see enough to represent a good one. We show that in a solvable case the bigger problem lies elsewhere. Even when a good policy is available and the agent's value function is expressive enough to describe it exactly, learning still ends up somewhere far worse.
  We study a partially observed linear-quadratic problem in which a standard actor-critic learner can be solved in closed form. At our default setting the best policy the agent can represent is already close to optimal, costing 10.4% more than the ideal controller that observes everything. Learning does not find it. The algorithm instead comes to rest at a policy that is 35% worse than the best one available to it, and we can say exactly where and why.
  The cause is a bias in what the critic learns rather than a limit on what the actor can express. Because the agent cannot attribute what it sees to the part of the state it cannot observe, the critic misreads that unexplained variation as sharp curvature in its own value estimates, and the actor follows that error away from the optimum. We derive closed-form expressions for the resulting policy, for its cost, and for the one design choice that removes the problem, which is how far the learner looks ahead before trusting its own value estimates. Deep reinforcement learning experiments follow these predictions closely. Notably, giving the agent memory of past observations does not help, while changing how far it looks ahead does.

</details>


### [286] [TOFD: Target-Oriented Feature Decoupling against Poisoning Attacks in Split Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07274)
*Yuhan Xie,Jingrong Huang,Chen Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TOFD框架，通过目标推断、样本净化、解耦优化三阶段联合防御分裂联邦学习中的多种投毒攻击，在五个数据集上优于现有SOTA防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分裂联邦学习（SFL）虽有隐私保护和降低客户端开销的优势，但其分裂架构引入了独特攻击面，易受多种投毒攻击影响。现有防御方法大多未能充分利用分裂范式的特性，难以在早期阶段检测和遏制恶意行为。

Method: TOFD包含三个阶段：(1) 目标推断——通过类别特定边际扰动（Margin Perturbation, MP）细化类级安全区域来识别潜在攻击目标；(2) 样本净化——利用跨类别最小-最大归一化MP校准阈值，自适应过滤被投毒的smashed data；(3) 解耦优化——利用对抗引导模型捕获攻击诱导模式并在优化中解耦其影响，抑制残余对抗效应。

Result: 在五个数据集上的大量实验表明，TOFD在多种攻击场景下持续优于现有SOTA防御方法，实现了优越的鲁棒性，同时保持低计算开销，适合实际部署。

Conclusion: TOFD提供了理论上的收敛性保证，并通过统一框架实现了对多种投毒攻击的主动检测和鲁棒优化，证明了其在实际场景中的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Split Federated Learning (SFL) facilitates privacy-preserving collaborative training with reduced client-side overhead. However, its split architecture introduces unique attack surfaces, rendering it vulnerable to diverse poisoning attacks. Most existing defenses fail to exploit the split paradigm, limiting their ability to detect and contain malicious behaviors at an early stage. To bridge this gap, we propose Target-Oriented Feature Decoupling (TOFD), a unified framework that jointly enables proactive detection and robust optimization against a wide range of poisoning attacks. TOFD operates in three stages: (1) Target Inference, which identifies potential attack targets by refining class-wise safe zones via class-specific Margin Perturbation (MP); (2) Sample Purification, which adaptively filters poisoned smashed data using thresholds calibrated through cross-class min-max normalization of MP; and (3) Decoupling Optimization, which leverages an adversarial guidance model to capture attack-induced patterns and decouple their influence during optimization, thereby suppressing residual adversarial effects. We provide theoretical guarantees for the convergence of TOFD. Extensive experiments on five datasets demonstrate that TOFD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art defenses under diverse attack scenarios, achieving superior robustness with low computational overhead suitable for practical deployment.

</details>


### [287] [A foundation-model approach to pediatric headache classification from rs-fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07287)
*Guilherme S. Imai Aldeia,Clara Moon,Julie Shulman,Navil Sethna,Allison Smith,Alyssa Lebel,William G. La Cava,Scott Holmes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用静息态功能磁共振成像（rs-fMRI）和基础模型NeuroSTORM进行儿童头痛分类。在189个扫描样本（110名个体）上，NeuroSTORM在区分头痛与非头痛时达到AUROC 0.82和AUPRC 0.93，优于基于功能连接矩阵的传统方法（AUROC 0.67）；多分类（健康对照、慢性偏头痛、非慢性头痛）的macro-AUROC为0.69。模型能区分慢性偏头痛，但难以区分其他头痛亚型，为fMRI预测儿童头痛提供了概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头痛是儿童中最常见的神经系统疾病，严重影响生活质量，但目前缺乏客观的影像学生物标志物来支持临床分类。作者希望探索是否可以利用rs-fMRI和机器学习方法实现儿童头痛的自动分类，从而辅助临床诊断和个体化治疗。

Method: 使用NeuroSTORM这一基于rs-fMRI的基础模型编码数据并进行微调，将其与传统的基于功能连接（FC）矩阵的方法进行对比。实验使用189个rs-fMRI扫描（来自110名个体，两次随访采集，头痛患病率74%），执行二分类（头痛vs非头痛）和多分类（健康对照、慢性偏头痛、非慢性头痛亚型）任务。

Result: NeuroSTORM在头痛vs非头痛二分类中达到AUROC 0.82和AUPRC 0.93，显著优于FC矩阵方法（AUROC 0.67，AUPRC 0.85）；多分类任务中macro-AUROC为0.69。模型能有效识别慢性偏头痛，但难以区分其他头痛亚型与慢性偏头痛。

Conclusion: 在数据有限的条件下，NeuroSTORM能够捕捉rs-fMRI中的潜在表征并迁移到头痛相关任务，无需依赖FC特征。该研究为fMRI预测儿童头痛提供了概念验证，并提示其未来在头痛亚型识别和个体化治疗策略中的潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Headache is the most common neurological disorder in children and substantially affects quality of life. We investigated whether resting-state functional MRI (rs-fMRI) can support pediatric headache classification using machine learning. We encoded rs-fMRI data using NeuroSTORM, a recent foundation model, and fine-tuned it to distinguish healthy controls from children with headache and subsequently classify headache subtypes. We compared NeuroSTORM with a standard neuroscience approach using functional-connectivity (FC) matrices derived from brain activity as predictors. Using 189 rs-fMRI scans from 110 individuals collected across two visits (prevalence of any headache: 74%), NeuroSTORM achieved an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.82 (95% CI, 0.82-0.82) and an area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) of 0.93 (95% CI, 0.93-0.94) for discriminating headache from non-headache. In contrast, models trained on FC matrices showed lower performance (AUROC, 0.67 [95% CI, 0.67-0.67]; AUPRC, 0.85 [95% CI, 0.85-0.85]). In multiclass classification of healthy controls, chronic migraine, and non-chronic headaches (e.g., post-viral headache, new daily persistent headache, post-traumatic headache), NeuroSTORM achieved a macro-AUROC of 0.69 (95% CI, 0.68-0.69). Results suggest that the approach can distinguish chronic migraine but has difficulty differentiating other headache subtypes from chronic migraine. Overall, under limited-data conditions, NeuroSTORM appears to capture latent rs-fMRI representations that transfer to headache-related tasks without relying on FC features. These findings provide proof of concept for fMRI-based prediction of pediatric headache and highlight potential future utility for subtype identification and individualized treatment strategies.

</details>


### [288] [FUSE: Feature-Wise Unified Specialization with Cross-Column Exchange for Mixed-Type Tabular Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07294)
*Suman Cha,Seongchan Lee,Dohyun Ko,Hyunjoong Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FUSE是一种面向混合类型表格数据生成的框架，通过特征级统一特化与跨列信息交换，将数值特征和类别特征分别交由自适应混合模块处理，同时利用联合注意力机制保持列间信息交流。该工作还理论刻画了受限条件上下文下的超额总体风险，并以端点预测风险界定了连续Wasserstein生成误差。在八个表格数据集上的实验表明FUSE在分布保真度和下游效用指标上均表现稳定且优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成混合类型表格数据需要同时建模多样化的特征分布及其复杂的跨列依赖关系。尽管变分流匹配通过因式分解分布来处理不同的端点，但其将特征特定处理和跨列交互隐式地放在共享骨干网络中，缺乏显式的角色分离，导致特征级处理和列间依赖建模不够清晰、灵活。

Method: FUSE框架将特征特定处理与跨列信息交换两个角色显式分离。具体而言：(1) 对数值特征和类别特征分别应用独立的自适应混合模块，使每个特征能够组合共享的特化子网络；(2) 通过联合注意力机制在所有列之间保持信息交换；(3) 理论上刻画了受限条件上下文下的超额总体风险，并以端点预测风险界定连续Wasserstein生成误差。

Result: 在八个表格数据集上的综合实验中，FUSE在分布保真度和下游效用指标上均取得了强且一致的性能表现，验证了方法在混合类型表格数据生成任务中的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FUSE通过显式分离特征处理与跨列交互的角色，有效提升了混合类型表格数据生成的质量。理论分析为受限条件上下文下的生成误差提供了保证，实验证明该框架在多样化的数据集上具有良好的泛化性和一致性。

Abstract: Generating mixed-type tabular data requires jointly modeling diverse feature distributions and their complex cross-column dependencies. Variational flow matching handles distinct endpoints via factorized distributions, yet leaves feature-specific processing and cross-column interactions implicit within a shared backbone. We introduce Feature-wise Unified Specialization with cross-column Exchange (FUSE) to explicitly separate these roles. FUSE applies separate adaptive mixture modules to numerical and categorical features, allowing each feature to combine shared specialized subnetworks, while joint attention preserves information exchange across all columns. We also characterize the excess population risk from restricted conditioning contexts and bound the continuous Wasserstein generation error by endpoint-prediction risk. Comprehensive experiments on eight tabular datasets demonstrate that FUSE achieves strong and consistent performance across distributional fidelity and downstream utility metrics.

</details>


### [289] [From Optimal Actions to World Models: Identifiability of Transition Kernels in Discounted MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07301)
*Neal Batra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了仅从最优动作（而非数值Q值）出发，能恢复马尔可夫决策过程中多少转移概率信息的问题。研究发现：对状态-动作奖励r(s,a)，仅知道所有奖励下的最优动作不足以唯一确定转移核，但刻画了所有给出相同最优动作的核的完整表征（通过一个可逆矩阵L的等式）；对r(s,a,s')形式的奖励，通常能恢复转移核本身；对状态奖励r(s)，恢复能力最弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Letcher等人研究了从数值Q值（反问题）恢复转移动力学的可能性，但实际场景中观测到的往往只有最优动作而非数值Q值。作者旨在回答：仅从最优动作信息出发，能恢复多少转移概率信息？以及奖励函数的形式如何影响这种可恢复性。

Method: 作者对三类奖励函数（r(s,a)、r(s,a,s')、r(s)）分别进行理论分析。对r(s,a)情形，证明了两个核给出相同最优动作的充要条件是一个特定的矩阵等式成立，并讨论了严格正核附近的自由度（n(n-1)维族）。对r(s,a,s')情形，证明转移核通常可以确定。对r(s)情形，给出两个核等价的最优动作条件。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 对r(s,a)，最优动作信息不足以唯一确定转移核，给出等价的完整表征公式；在严格正核附近存在n(n-1)维的不同核族；(2) 考虑唯一最优动作的奖励也不改变结论；(3) 对r(s,a,s')，每个至少有两个动作的状态的行均可确定，仅有单动作的状态行可能隐藏且有精确刻画；(4) 对r(s)，等价条件为每个确定性策略对同一组奖励最优。

Conclusion: 奖励函数的形式从根本上决定了从最优动作中能学到多少动力学信息：奖励依赖下一状态时恢复能力最强，状态-动作奖励次之，纯状态奖励最弱。该结果阐明了"可观测最优动作"这一实际设定下逆强化学习/反问题的信息理论边界。

Abstract: We study what can be recovered about the transition probabilities of a Markov decision process from optimal actions alone. This is closely related to the inverse problem considered by Letcher et al., who ask when the dynamics can be recovered from numerical \(Q\)-values. Here the numerical values themselves are not observed; only the optimal actions are known, for every reward in a given class.
  For state-action rewards \(r(s,a)\), knowing the optimal actions for every reward also tells us how much better one action is than another when each is followed by the same fixed policy. This is still not enough to determine the transition probabilities uniquely. We prove that two kernels give the same optimal actions for every reward exactly when \[ Q_{s,a} =
\Bigl(P_{s,a}+\tfrac1γe_s^{\mathsf T}(L-I)\Bigr)L^{-1} \] for one invertible matrix \(L\) satisfying \(L\mathbf 1=\mathbf 1\). Near a kernel with strictly positive entries, there is an \(n(n-1)\)-dimensional family of different kernels with this property. The result is unchanged if we consider only rewards having a unique optimal action at every state.
  We then compare this with rewards of the forms \(r(s)\) and \(r(s,a,s')\). Rewards that depend on the next state can usually recover the transition kernel itself: every row at a state with at least two actions is determined, and we describe exactly when a row at a state with one action can remain hidden. State rewards reveal less: two kernels give the same optimal actions exactly when every deterministic policy is optimal for the same set of rewards. The results show how the form of the reward affects what can be learned about the dynamics from optimal actions alone.

</details>


### [290] [Is SwiGLU's Open Positive Tail Necessary? Evidence from Closed-Tail Gating with MemGLU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07323)
*Yuting Ge,Pengju Yang,Mingkai Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入MemGLU（一种由忆阻分支几何结构导出的闭合正尾部激活函数）作为SwiGLU的对照，在9M和30M规模的decoder-only语言模型预训练中（每个配三种随机种子）进行对比。结果显示MemGLU在验证NLL上与SwiGLU差异仅约0.1%，但在机制诊断中发现两者使用门控的方式不同——即使损失相近，训练好的SwiGLU检查点对正尾部抑制表现出敏感性。结论表明模型会适应预训练时提供的门控几何结构，在测试规模下SwiGLU的开放正尾部并非decoder-only FFN的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证decoder-only语言模型的FFN是否真正需要SwiGLU的开放正尾部（open positive tail），即探究SwiGLU中闭式正尾部的必要性，为激活函数设计提供理论依据。

Method: 引入MemGLU作为闭合正尾部的对照激活函数，其设计源自忆阻分支几何结构。在9M和30M两种参数规模的paired预训练运行中（各用三种随机种子），对比MemGLU与SwiGLU的验证NLL，并对训练好的SwiGLU检查点进行正尾部抑制敏感性测试，同时进行机制级门控使用方式的诊断分析。

Result: MemGLU与SwiGLU在验证NLL上的差距维持在约0.1%以内；训练的SwiGLU检查点对正尾部抑制敏感；机制诊断显示两种模型使用门控的方式不同，但总体损失相似；模型会适应预训练时可用的门控几何结构。

Conclusion: 在测试的模型规模下，SwiGLU的开放正尾部并非decoder-only语言模型FFN的必要组成部分，模型会自适应地利用预训练期间可获得的门控几何结构来达到相似的性能。

Abstract: We test whether decoder-only language-model FFNs require SwiGLU's open positive tail. We introduce MemGLU as a closed-tail comparator derived from a memristive branch geometry. Across paired 9M and 30M pretraining runs with three seeds, MemGLU remains within about 0.1% of SwiGLU in validation NLL. Trained SwiGLU checkpoints are sensitive to positive-tail suppression, while mechanism diagnostics show that the two models use their gates differently despite similar losses. These results suggest that models adapt to the gate geometry available during pretraining. At the tested scales, SwiGLU's open positive tail is not necessary for decoder-only language-model FFNs.

</details>


### [291] [When GNNs Fail: Quantifying and Overcoming Temporal Correlation Volatility in Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07333)
*Chen Shao,Yue Wang,Zhenyi Zhu,Zhanbo Huang,Tobias Käfer,Zonghan Wu,Danai Koutra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Temporal Correlation Volatility (TCV)指标量化多元时序数据中潜在图结构的分布演化程度，揭示高TCV场景下现有GNN及Transformer等模型性能严重退化的问题，并提出GLIDE图神经网络层，通过路径消息传递和静态/动态传播分离两种理论支撑的机制，在动态拓扑下大幅提升预测性能（平均提升45.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN通常假设静态图拓扑进行邻居信息聚合，但真实多元时间序列中节点间的成对相关性可能随时间剧烈变化。作者旨在探究GNN在静态和动态场景下对多变量时间序列预测的表征能力，识别当前架构的关键局限，并量化动态拓扑与模型性能退化之间的关联。

Method: 首先提出模型无关的TCV指标来量化潜在图结构的分布演化程度；然后建立TCV与性能退化间的明确联系；最后提出GLIDE图神经网络层，包含两种理论支撑的机制：(D1)路径消息传递，捕获基于路径的邻域信息；(D2)静态与动态传播分离，通过局部静态近似识别最优动态。此外实验涵盖合成和真实世界基准。

Result: 实验表明，在静态和动态设置下，GLIDE平均性能提升最高达45.6%，最大单项提升达85.7%。同时证明了高TCV环境下许多主流模型（包括Transformer）泛化能力差，甚至不如简单的结构无关基线。

Conclusion: GLIDE通过路径消息传递和静态/动态传播分离两大机制，在动态拓扑条件下显著改善了学习能力，同时在静态场景下保持鲁棒性，为多元时间序列预测中动态图结构建模提供了新的有效方案。

Abstract: Modeling multivariate time series by representing them as graphs, where individual series act as nodes and pairwise temporal corre- lations serve as edges, has gained significant traction. Recent advances in Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have demonstrated strong perfor- mance by assuming a static graph topology and aggregating information from neighboring series. In this work, we investigate the representa- tional power of GNNs for forecasting under both static and dynamic settings (i.e., when pairwise correlations evolve drastically over time) and identify critical limitations in current architectures. To formalize this, we first propose Temporal Correlation Volatility (TCV), a model- agnostic metric designed to quantify the distributional evolution of these latent structures. We establish a clear connection between TCV and performance degradation, demonstrating that many popular models, including Transformers, generalize poorly in high-TCV settings and are often outperformed by simple structure-agnostic baselines. To address these limitations, we propose Graph Layer for Inference in Dynamic En- vironments (GLIDE), a novel GNN layer enhanced by two theoretically grounded design mechanisms: (D1) Path-based Message Passing, which captures path-based neighborhoods and (D2) Static and Dynamic Propagation Separation, which identifies optimal dynamics via local static approximation. These components significantly improve learning under dynamic topology while preserving robustness in static scenarios. Ex- tensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that GLIDE improves average performance by up to 45.6% across static and dynamic settings, with the largest gain reaching 85.7%. The source code is available at https://github.com/ChenS676/GLIDE.

</details>


### [292] [Aftab: A Comprehensive Benchmark of CNN Encoders and Advanced Value Functions in Parallelized Q-Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07335)
*Taha Shieenavaz,Shabnam Zareshahraki,Loris Nanni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了并行化Q网络（PQN）在无回放缓冲区设置下的卷积神经网络架构设计空间，提出了复合架构Aftab，在Atari-57基准上达到6.479的IQM人类标准化分数，优于标准PQN基线，并在Procgen Hard基准上展现出更强的分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习中，PQN等简化并行化算法因无需回放缓冲区和目标网络而备受青睐，但在此类无缓冲区设置下视觉编码器的表示能力和参数效率尚未被充分探索。

Method: 设计并严格评估了八种不同的CNN拓扑结构，在严格参数约束下优化样本效率；同时整合了Hadamax编码范式以及分布式、集成式和决斗头等高级Q学习扩展，构建了复合架构Aftab。

Result: 在Atari-57基准上，Aftab达到6.479的IQM人类标准化分数，相对标准PQN基线有0.86的提升概率；在Procgen Hard基准上IQM标准化分数为0.418，优于基线0.382，验证了分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个高效且概率上更优的无模型强化学习结构参考，同时保持了无缓冲并行化优化的简单性和内存效率。完整框架已开源。

Abstract: Recent advancements in deep reinforcement learning have increasingly favored simplified, highly parallelized paradigms. Notably, the Parallelized Q-Network (PQN) algorithm achieves stable off-policy learning without relying on computationally expensive replay buffers or target networks. However, the representational capacity and parameter efficiency of visual encoders operating in these buffer-free settings remain underexplored. In this work, we systematically investigate the architectural design space of Convolutional Neural Networks for PQN. We design and rigorously evaluate eight distinct CNN topologies, optimizing for sample efficiency under strict parameter constraints. Furthermore, we study the impact of representation and value estimation enhancements by integrating the Hadamax encoding paradigm and advanced Q-learning extensions, including distributional, ensemble, and dueling heads. Extensive experiments on the Atari-57 benchmark demonstrate that our proposed composite architecture, Aftab, achieves an Interquartile Mean (IQM) Human-Normalized Score of 6.479, establishing a 0.86 Probability of Improvement over the standard PQN baseline. Additionally, structural resilience evaluations on the highly non-stationary Procgen Hard benchmark confirm out-of-distribution generalization, with Aftab yielding an IQM Procgen Normalized Score of 0.418 compared to the baseline's 0.382. Ultimately, this work establishes an efficient, probabilistically superior structural reference for model-free reinforcement learning, all while preserving the simplicity and memory efficiency of unbuffered, parallelized optimization.
  The complete Aftab framework, including all model definitions, training configurations, and raw experimental logs, is open-sourced and available on our GitHub repository: https://github.com/tahashieenavaz/aftab

</details>


### [293] [Residual Algebra for Representation-Preserving Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07349)
*Yao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"代数学化残差"的异质表示学习方法：将每种表示视为拥有自身坐标系和未解残差的类型化对象，学习是有序的算子组合（保留或消除类型）。Fold在10x10秩网格上用时点条件均值场实现对象，FPRC-PQ以relax-aggregate-close实现代数。在367万条中国A股日观测数据上（2023-2026），净收益从13.52%提升至19.10%，夏普从1.42升至2.09，超过所有对照方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
